## CONTEXT

A refactoring block was applied to a Java project, but the configured Maven build failed.

The goal of this step is to repair the current workspace so that the applied refactoring block compiles and passes the configured build command.

This is a repair step, not a new refactoring step.

## TASK

Fix only the errors reported in the build log.

Use the current code state, the staged block, the files already changed by the executor, and the files mentioned by the build log to produce a minimal repair.

Do not redo the refactoring from scratch.

Do not change the refactoring strategy.

Do not undo the refactoring unless that is the smallest safe repair.

## CONSTRAINTS

- Modify only files listed in allowed_files.
- Return the full new content for every modified file.
- Do not return partial patches.
- Do not edit unrelated files.
- Do not edit unrelated tests.
- Do not remove tests.
- Do not weaken assertions.
- Do not change expected behavior to match a broken implementation.
- Do not introduce compatibility wrappers unless they are necessary to make the current refactoring compile.
- Do not undo previous successful blocks.
- If the error cannot be safely repaired using only allowed_files, return an empty files_to_write list and explain why in notes.
- Return only files that actually changed.
- Do not include unchanged files in files_to_write.

      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2012-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.core.configuration.support.database;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.configuration.BatchConfigurationException;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.configuration.support.DefaultBatchConfiguration;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.converter.DateToStringConverter;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.converter.LocalDateTimeToStringConverter;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.converter.LocalDateToStringConverter;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.converter.LocalTimeToStringConverter;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.converter.StringToDateConverter;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.converter.StringToLocalDateConverter;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.converter.StringToLocalDateTimeConverter;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.converter.StringToLocalTimeConverter;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.job.DefaultJobKeyGenerator;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.job.JobInstance;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.job.JobKeyGenerator;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.launch.JobOperator;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.repository.ExecutionContextSerializer;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.repository.JobRepository;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.repository.dao.AbstractJdbcBatchMetadataDao;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.repository.dao.DefaultExecutionContextSerializer;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.repository.dao.jdbc.JdbcExecutionContextDao;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.repository.dao.jdbc.JdbcJobExecutionDao;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.repository.dao.jdbc.JdbcStepExecutionDao;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.repository.support.JdbcJobRepositoryFactoryBean;\nimport org.springframework.infrastructure.item.database.support.DataFieldMaxValueIncrementerFactory;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.database.support.DefaultDataFieldMaxValueIncrementerFactory;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType;\nimport org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;\nimport org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;\nimport org.springframework.core.convert.support.ConfigurableConversionService;\nimport org.springframework.core.convert.support.DefaultConversionService;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcOperations;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.support.MetaDataAccessException;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.support.incrementer.DataFieldMaxValueIncrementer;\nimport org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;\nimport org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Isolation;\n\nimport javax.sql.DataSource;\nimport java.nio.charset.Charset;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\nimport java.sql.Types;\n\n/**\n * Base {@link Configuration} class that provides common JDBC-based infrastructure beans\n * for enabling and using Spring Batch.\n *\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @since 6.0\n */\n@Configuration(proxyBeanMethods = false)\npublic class JdbcDefaultBatchConfiguration extends DefaultBatchConfiguration {\n\n\t@Bean\n\t@Override\n\tpublic JobRepository jobRepository() throws BatchConfigurationException {\n\t\tJdbcJobRepositoryFactoryBean jobRepositoryFactoryBean = new JdbcJobRepositoryFactoryBean();\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setDataSource(getDataSource());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setTransactionManager(getTransactionManager());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setDatabaseType(getDatabaseType());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setIncrementerFactory(getIncrementerFactory());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setJobKeyGenerator(getJobKeyGenerator());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setClobType(getClobType());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setTablePrefix(getTablePrefix());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setSerializer(getExecutionContextSerializer());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setConversionService(getConversionService());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setJdbcOperations(getJdbcOperations());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setCharset(getCharset());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setMaxVarCharLength(getMaxVarCharLength());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setIsolationLevelForCreateEnum(getIsolationLevelForCreate());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setValidateTransactionState(getValidateTransactionState());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.afterPropertiesSet();\n\t\t\treturn jobRepositoryFactoryBean.getObject();\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (Exception e) {\n\t\t\tthrow new BatchConfigurationException(\"Unable to configure the default job repository\", e);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tprotected DataSource getDataSource() {\n\t\tString errorMessage = \" To use the default configuration, a data source bean named 'dataSource'\"\n\t\t\t\t+ \" should be defined in the application context but none was found. Override getDataSource()\"\n\t\t\t\t+ \" to provide the data source to use for Batch meta-data.\";\n\t\tif (this.applicationContext.getBeansOfType(DataSource.class).isEmpty()) {\n\t\t\tthrow new BatchConfigurationException(\n\t\t\t\t\t\"Unable to find a DataSource bean in the application context.\" + errorMessage);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (!this.applicationContext.containsBean(\"dataSource\")) {\n\t\t\tthrow new BatchConfigurationException(errorMessage);\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn this.applicationContext.getBean(\"dataSource\", DataSource.class);\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected PlatformTransactionManager getTransactionManager() {\n\t\tString errorMessage = \" To use the default configuration, a PlatformTransactionManager bean named 'transactionManager'\"\n\t\t\t\t+ \" should be defined in the application context but none was found. Override getTransactionManager()\"\n\t\t\t\t+ \" to provide the transaction manager to use for the job repository.\";\n\t\tif (this.applicationContext.getBeansOfType(PlatformTransactionManager.class).isEmpty()) {\n\t\t\tthrow new BatchConfigurationException(\n\t\t\t\t\t\"Unable to find a PlatformTransactionManager bean in the application context.\" + errorMessage);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (!this.applicationContext.containsBean(\"transactionManager\")) {\n\t\t\tthrow new BatchConfigurationException(errorMessage);\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn this.applicationContext.getBean(\"transactionManager\", PlatformTransactionManager.class);\n\t}\n\n\tprotected int getMaxVarCharLength() { return AbstractJdbcBatchMetadataDao.DEFAULT_EXIT_MESSAGE_LENGTH; }\n\tprotected String getTablePrefix() { return AbstractJdbcBatchMetadataDao.DEFAULT_TABLE_PREFIX; }\n\tprotected Charset getCharset() { return StandardCharsets.UTF_8; }\n\tprotected JdbcOperations getJdbcOperations() { return new JdbcTemplate(getDataSource()); }\n\tprotected ExecutionContextSerializer getExecutionContextSerializer() { return new DefaultExecutionContextSerializer(); }\n\tprotected int getClobType() { return Types.CLOB; }\n\tprotected DataFieldMaxValueIncrementerFactory getIncrementerFactory() {\n\t\treturn new DefaultDataFieldMaxValueIncrementerFactory(getDataSource());\n\t}\n\tprotected String getDatabaseType() throws MetaDataAccessException { return DatabaseType.fromMetaData(getDataSource()).name(); }\n\tprotected ConfigurableConversionService getConversionService() {\n\t\tDefaultConversionService conversionService = new DefaultConversionService();\n\t\tconversionService.addConverter(new DateToStringConverter());\n\t\tconversionService.addConverter(new StringToDateConverter());\n\t\tconversionService.addConverter(new LocalDateToStringConverter());\n\t\tconversionService.addConverter(new StringToLocalDateConverter());\n\t\tconversionService.addConverter(new LocalTimeToStringConverter());\n\t\tconversionService.addConverter(new StringToLocalTimeConverter());\n\t\tconversionService.addConverter(new LocalDateTimeToStringConverter());\n\t\tconversionService.addConverter(new StringToLocalDateTimeConverter());\n\t\treturn conversionService;\n\t}\n\tprotected boolean getValidateTransactionState() { return true; }\n\tprotected Isolation getIsolationLevelForCreate() { return Isolation.SERIALIZABLE; }\n\tprotected JobKeyGenerator getJobKeyGenerator() { return new DefaultJobKeyGenerator(); }\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-core/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/core/configuration/support/database/MongoDefaultBatchConfiguration.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2012-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.core.configuration.support.database;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.configuration.BatchConfigurationException;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.configuration.support.DefaultBatchConfiguration;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.job.DefaultJobKeyGenerator;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.job.JobInstance;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.job.JobKeyGenerator;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.launch.JobOperator;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.repository.JobRepository;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.repository.dao.mongodb.MongoSequenceIncrementer;\nimport org.springframework.batch.core.repository.support.MongoJobRepositoryFactoryBean;\nimport org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;\nimport org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;\nimport org.springframework.data.mongodb.MongoTransactionManager;\nimport org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoOperations;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.support.incrementer.DataFieldMaxValueIncrementer;\nimport org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Isolation;\n\n/**\n * Base {@link Configuration} class that provides common MongoDB-based infrastructure\n * beans for enabling and using Spring Batch.\n *\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @since 6.0\n */\n@Configuration(proxyBeanMethods = false)\npublic class MongoDefaultBatchConfiguration extends DefaultBatchConfiguration {\n\n\t@Bean\n\t@Override\n\tpublic JobRepository jobRepository() throws BatchConfigurationException {\n\t\tMongoJobRepositoryFactoryBean jobRepositoryFactoryBean = new MongoJobRepositoryFactoryBean();\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setMongoOperations(getMongoOperations());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setTransactionManager(getTransactionManager());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setIsolationLevelForCreateEnum(getIsolationLevelForCreate());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setValidateTransactionState(getValidateTransactionState());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setJobKeyGenerator(getJobKeyGenerator());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setJobInstanceIncrementer(getJobInstanceIncrementer());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setJobExecutionIncrementer(getJobExecutionIncrementer());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.setStepExecutionIncrementer(getStepExecutionIncrementer());\n\t\t\tjobRepositoryFactoryBean.afterPropertiesSet();\n\t\t\treturn jobRepositoryFactoryBean.getObject();\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (Exception e) {\n\t\t\tthrow new BatchConfigurationException(\"Unable to configure the default job repository\", e);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tprotected MongoOperations getMongoOperations() {\n\t\tString errorMessage = \" To use the default configuration, a MongoOperations bean named 'mongoTemplate'\"\n\t\t\t\t+ \" should be defined in the application context but none was found. Override getMongoOperations()\"\n\t\t\t\t+ \" to provide the MongoOperations for Batch meta-data.\";\n\t\tif (this.applicationContext.getBeansOfType(MongoOperations.class).isEmpty()) {\n\t\t\tthrow new BatchConfigurationException(\n\t\t\t\t\t\"Unable to find a MongoOperations bean in the application context.\" + errorMessage);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (!this.applicationContext.containsBean(\"mongoTemplate\")) {\n\t\t\tthrow new BatchConfigurationException(errorMessage);\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn this.applicationContext.getBean(\"mongoTemplate\", MongoOperations.class);\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected MongoTransactionManager getTransactionManager() {\n\t\tString errorMessage = \" To use the default configuration, a MongoTransactionManager bean named 'transactionManager'\"\n\t\t\t\t+ \" should be defined in the application context but none was found. Override getTransactionManager()\"\n\t\t\t\t+ \" to provide the transaction manager to use for the job repository.\";\n\t\tif (this.applicationContext.getBeansOfType(MongoTransactionManager.class).isEmpty()) {\n\t\t\tthrow new BatchConfigurationException(\n\t\t\t\t\t\"Unable to find a MongoTransactionManager bean in the application context.\" + errorMessage);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (!this.applicationContext.containsBean(\"transactionManager\")) {\n\t\t\tthrow new BatchConfigurationException(errorMessage);\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn this.applicationContext.getBean(\"transactionManager\", MongoTransactionManager.class);\n\t}\n\n\tprotected boolean getValidateTransactionState() { return true; }\n\tprotected Isolation getIsolationLevelForCreate() { return Isolation.SERIALIZABLE; }\n\tprotected JobKeyGenerator getJobKeyGenerator() { return new DefaultJobKeyGenerator(); }\n\tprotected DataFieldMaxValueIncrementer getJobInstanceIncrementer() {\n\t\treturn new MongoSequenceIncrementer(getMongoOperations(), \"BATCH_JOB_INSTANCE_SEQ\");\n\t}\n\tprotected DataFieldMaxValueIncrementer getJobExecutionIncrementer() {\n\t\treturn new MongoSequenceIncrementer(getMongoOperations(), \"BATCH_JOB_EXECUTION_SEQ\");\n\t}\n\tprotected DataFieldMaxValueIncrementer getStepExecutionIncrementer() {\n\t\treturn new MongoSequenceIncrementer(getMongoOperations(), \"BATCH_STEP_EXECUTION_SEQ\");\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/Chunk.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport java.io.Serializable;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Arrays;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.Iterator;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Objects;\n\n/**\n * Encapsulation of a list of items to be processed and possibly a list of failed items to\n * be skipped. To mark an item as skipped, clients should iterate over the chunk using the\n * {@link #iterator()} method, and if there is a failure call\n * {@link Chunk.ChunkIterator#remove()} on the iterator. The skipped items are then\n * available through the chunk.\n *\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Jinwoo Bae\n * @author Stefano Cordio\n * @since 2.0\n */\npublic class Chunk<W> implements Iterable<W>, Serializable {\n\n\tprivate final List<W> items = new ArrayList<>();\n\n\tprivate final List<SkipWrapper<W>> skips = new ArrayList<>();\n\n\tprivate final List<Exception> errors = new ArrayList<>();\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Object userData;\n\n\tprivate boolean end;\n\n\tprivate boolean busy;\n\n\t@SafeVarargs\n\tpublic Chunk(W... items) {\n\t\tthis(Arrays.asList(items));\n\t}\n\n\t@SafeVarargs\n\tpublic static <W> Chunk<W> of(W... items) {\n\t\treturn new Chunk<>(items);\n\t}\n\n\tpublic Chunk(List<? extends W> items) {\n\t\tthis(items, null);\n\t}\n\n\t@Deprecated(since = \"6.0\", forRemoval = true)\n\tpublic Chunk(@Nullable List<? extends W> items, @Nullable List<SkipWrapper<W>> skips) {\n\t\tif (items != null) {\n\t\t\tthis.items.addAll(items);\n\t\t}\n\t\tif (skips != null) {\n\t\t\tthis.skips.addAll(skips);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Add the item to the chunk.\n\t * @param item the item to add\n\t */\n\tpublic void add(W item) {\n\t\titems.add(item);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Add all items to the chunk.\n\t * @param items the items to add\n\t */\n\tpublic void addAll(List<W> items) {\n\t\tthis.items.addAll(items);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Clear the items down to signal that we are done.\n\t */\n\tpublic void clear() {\n\t\titems.clear();\n\t\tskips.clear();\n\t\tuserData = null;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return a copy of the items to be processed as an unmodifiable list\n\t */\n\tpublic List<W> getItems() {\n\t\treturn Collections.unmodifiableList(items);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return a copy of the skips as an unmodifiable list\n\t */\n\t@Deprecated(since = \"6.0\", forRemoval = true)\n\tpublic List<SkipWrapper<W>> getSkips() {\n\t\treturn Collections.unmodifiableList(skips);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return a copy of the anonymous errors as an unmodifiable list\n\t */\n\t@Deprecated(since = \"6.0\", forRemoval = true)\n\tpublic List<Exception> getErrors() {\n\t\treturn Collections.unmodifiableList(errors);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Register an anonymous skip. To skip an individual item, use\n\t * {@link ChunkIterator#remove()}.\n\t * @param e the exception that caused the skip\n\t */\n\t@Deprecated(since = \"6.0\", forRemoval = true)\n\tpublic void skip(Exception e) {\n\t\terrors.add(e);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return {@code true} if there are no items in the chunk\n\t */\n\tpublic boolean isEmpty() {\n\t\treturn items.isEmpty();\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Get an unmodifiable iterator for the underlying items.\n\t * @see java.lang.Iterable#iterator()\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic ChunkIterator iterator() {\n\t\treturn new ChunkIterator(items);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return the number of items (excluding skips)\n\t */\n\tpublic int size() {\n\t\treturn items.size();\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return the number of skipped items\n\t */\n\t@Deprecated(since = \"6.0\", forRemoval = true)\n\tpublic int getSkipsSize() {\n\t\treturn skips.size();\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Flag to indicate if the source data is exhausted.\n\t *\n\t * <p>\n\t * Note: This may return false if the last chunk has the same number of items as the\n\t * configured commit interval. Consequently, in such cases,there will be a last empty\n\t * chunk that won't be processed. It is recommended to consider this behavior when\n\t * utilizing this method.\n\t * </p>\n\t * @return true if there is no more data to process\n\t */\n\t@Deprecated(since = \"6.0\", forRemoval = true)\n\tpublic boolean isEnd() {\n\t\treturn end;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Set the flag to say that this chunk represents an end of stream (there is no more\n\t * data to process).\n\t */\n\t@Deprecated(since = \"6.0\", forRemoval = true)\n\tpublic void setEnd() {\n\t\tthis.end = true;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Query the chunk to see if anyone has registered an interest in keeping a reference\n\t * to it.\n\t * @return the busy flag\n\t */\n\t@Deprecated(since = \"6.0\", forRemoval = true)\n\tpublic boolean isBusy() {\n\t\treturn busy;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Register an interest in the chunk to prevent it from being cleaned up before the\n\t * flag is reset to false.\n\t * @param busy the flag to set\n\t */\n\t@Deprecated(since = \"6.0\", forRemoval = true)\n\tpublic void setBusy(boolean busy) {\n\t\tthis.busy = busy;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Clear only the skips list.\n\t */\n\t@Deprecated(since = \"6.0\", forRemoval = true)\n\tpublic void clearSkips() {\n\t\tskips.clear();\n\t}\n\n\t@Deprecated(since = \"6.0\", forRemoval = true)\n\tpublic @Nullable Object getUserData() {\n\t\treturn userData;\n\t}\n\n\t@Deprecated(since = \"6.0\", forRemoval = true)\n\tpublic void setUserData(Object userData) {\n\t\tthis.userData = userData;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic String toString() {\n\t\treturn String.format(\"[items=%s, skips=%s]\", items, skips);\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic boolean equals(Object obj) {\n\t\tif (obj == this) {\n\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t}\n\t\tif (!(obj instanceof Chunk<?> other)) {\n\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn Objects.equals(this.items, other.items) && Objects.equals(this.skips, other.skips)\n\t\t\t\t&& Objects.equals(this.errors, other.errors) && Objects.equals(this.userData, other.userData)\n\t\t\t\t&& this.end == other.end && this.busy == other.busy;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic int hashCode() {\n\t\tint result = 17;\n\t\tresult = 31 * result + items.hashCode();\n\t\tresult = 31 * result + skips.hashCode();\n\t\tresult = 31 * result + errors.hashCode();\n\t\tresult = 31 * result + Objects.hashCode(userData);\n\t\tresult = 31 * result + (end ? 1 : 0);\n\t\tresult = 31 * result + (busy ? 1 : 0);\n\t\treturn result;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Special iterator for a chunk providing the {@link #remove(Throwable)} method for\n\t * dynamically removing an item and adding it to the skips.\n\t *\n\t * @author Dave Syer\n\t *\n\t */\n\tpublic class ChunkIterator implements Iterator<W> {\n\n\t\tprivate final Iterator<W> iterator;\n\n\t\tprivate @Nullable W next;\n\n\t\tpublic ChunkIterator(List<W> items) {\n\t\t\titerator = items.iterator();\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t@Override\n\t\tpublic boolean hasNext() {\n\t\t\treturn iterator.hasNext();\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t@Override\n\t\tpublic W next() {\n\t\t\tnext = iterator.next();\n\t\t\treturn next;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tpublic void remove(Throwable e) {\n\t\t\tremove();\n\t\t\tskips.add(new SkipWrapper<>(next, e));\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t@Override\n\t\tpublic void remove() {\n\t\t\tif (next == null) {\n\t\t\t\tif (iterator.hasNext()) {\n\t\t\t\t\tnext = iterator.next();\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\titerator.remove();\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t@Override\n\t\tpublic String toString() {\n\t\t\treturn String.format(\"[items=%s, skips=%s]\", items, skips);\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/adapter/AbstractMethodInvokingDelegator.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.adapter;\n\nimport java.lang.reflect.InvocationTargetException;\nimport java.lang.reflect.Method;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Arrays;\nimport java.util.List;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\nimport org.springframework.beans.factory.InitializingBean;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\nimport org.springframework.util.ClassUtils;\nimport org.springframework.util.MethodInvoker;\nimport org.springframework.util.StringUtils;\n\n/**\n * Superclass for delegating classes which dynamically call a custom method of an injected\n * object. Provides a convenient API for dynamic method invocation shielding subclasses\n * from low-level details and exception handling.\n * <p>\n * {@link Exception}s thrown by a successfully invoked delegate method are re-thrown\n * without wrapping. In case the delegate method throws a {@link Throwable} that doesn't\n * subclass {@link Exception} it will be wrapped by\n * {@link InvocationTargetThrowableWrapper}.\n *\n * @author Robert Kasanicky\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Glenn Renfro\n * @author Stefano Cordio\n */\npublic abstract class AbstractMethodInvokingDelegator<T> implements InitializingBean {\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Object targetObject;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String targetMethod;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Object @Nullable [] arguments;\n\n\t/**\n\t * Invoker the target method with arguments set by {@link #setArguments(Object[])}.\n\t * @return object returned by invoked method\n\t * @throws Exception exception thrown when executing the delegate method.\n\t */\n\tprotected @Nullable T invokeDelegateMethod() throws Exception {\n\t\tMethodInvoker invoker = createMethodInvoker(targetObject, targetMethod);\n\t\tinvoker.setArguments(arguments);\n\t\treturn doInvoke(invoker);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Invokes the target method with given argument.\n\t * @param object argument for the target method\n\t * @return object returned by target method\n\t * @throws Exception exception thrown when executing the delegate method.\n\t */\n\tprotected @Nullable T invokeDelegateMethodWithArgument(Object object) throws Exception {\n\t\tMethodInvoker invoker = createMethodInvoker(targetObject, targetMethod);\n\t\tinvoker.setArguments(object);\n\t\treturn doInvoke(invoker);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Invokes the target method with given arguments.\n\t * @param args arguments for the invoked method\n\t * @return object returned by invoked method\n\t * @throws Exception exception thrown when executing the delegate method.\n\t */\n\tprotected @Nullable T invokeDelegateMethodWithArguments(@Nullable Object[] args) throws Exception {\n\t\tMethodInvoker invoker = createMethodInvoker(targetObject, targetMethod);\n\t\tinvoker.setArguments(args);\n\t\treturn doInvoke(invoker);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Create a new configured instance of {@link MethodInvoker}.\n\t */\n\tprivate MethodInvoker createMethodInvoker(@Nullable Object targetObject, @Nullable String targetMethod) {\n\t\tHippyMethodInvoker invoker = new HippyMethodInvoker();\n\t\tinvoker.setTargetObject(targetObject);\n\t\tinvoker.setTargetMethod(targetMethod);\n\t\tinvoker.setArguments(arguments);\n\t\treturn invoker;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Prepare and invoke the invoker, rethrow checked exceptions as unchecked.\n\t * @param invoker configured invoker\n\t * @return return value of the invoked method\n\t */\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"unchecked\")\n\tprivate @Nullable T doInvoke(MethodInvoker invoker) throws Exception {\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tinvoker.prepare();\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (ClassNotFoundException | NoSuchMethodException e) {\n\t\t\tthrow new DynamicMethodInvocationException(e);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\treturn (T) invoker.invoke();\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (InvocationTargetException e) {\n\t\t\tif (e.getCause() instanceof Exception) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow (Exception) e.getCause();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new InvocationTargetThrowableWrapper(e.getCause());\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (IllegalAccessException e) {\n\t\t\tthrow new DynamicMethodInvocationException(e);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void afterPropertiesSet() throws Exception {\n\t\tAssert.state(targetObject != null, \"targetObject must not be null\");\n\t\tAssert.state(StringUtils.hasText(targetMethod), \"targetMethod must not be empty\");\n\t\tAssert.state(targetClassDeclaresTargetMethod(),\n\t\t\t\t\"target class must declare a method with matching name and parameter types\");\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return true if target class declares a method matching target method name with\n\t * given number of arguments of appropriate type.\n\t */\n\tprivate boolean targetClassDeclaresTargetMethod() {\n\t\tMethodInvoker invoker = createMethodInvoker(targetObject, targetMethod);\n\n\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t\tMethod[] memberMethods = invoker.getTargetClass().getMethods();\n\t\tMethod[] declaredMethods = invoker.getTargetClass().getDeclaredMethods();\n\n\t\tList<Method> allMethods = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\tallMethods.addAll(Arrays.asList(memberMethods));\n\t\tallMethods.addAll(Arrays.asList(declaredMethods));\n\n\t\tString targetMethodName = invoker.getTargetMethod();\n\n\t\tfor (Method method : allMethods) {\n\t\t\tif (method.getName().equals(targetMethodName)) {\n\t\t\t\tClass<?>[] params = method.getParameterTypes();\n\t\t\t\tif (arguments == null) {\n\t\t\t\t\t// don't check signature, assume arguments will be supplied\n\t\t\t\t\t// correctly at runtime\n\t\t\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif (arguments.length == params.length) {\n\t\t\t\t\tboolean argumentsMatchParameters = true;\n\t\t\t\t\tfor (int j = 0; j < params.length; j++) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tif (arguments[j] == null) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tcontinue;\n\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\t\tif (!ClassUtils.isAssignableValue(params[j], arguments[j])) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\targumentsMatchParameters = false;\n\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tif (argumentsMatchParameters) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn false;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param targetObject the delegate - bean id can be used to set this value in Spring\n\t * configuration\n\t */\n\tpublic void setTargetObject(Object targetObject) {\n\t\tthis.targetObject = targetObject;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param targetMethod name of the method to be invoked on\n\t * {@link #setTargetObject(Object)}.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setTargetMethod(String targetMethod) {\n\t\tthis.targetMethod = targetMethod;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param arguments arguments values for the { {@link #setTargetMethod(String)}. These\n\t * will be used only when the subclass tries to invoke the target method without\n\t * providing explicit argument values.\n\t * <p>\n\t * If arguments are set to not-null value {@link #afterPropertiesSet()} will check the\n\t * values are compatible with target method's signature. In case arguments are\n\t * {@code null} (not set), the method signature will not be checked, and it is assumed\n\t * correct values will be supplied at runtime.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setArguments(Object @Nullable [] arguments) {\n\t\tthis.arguments = arguments == null ? null : arguments.clone();\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Return arguments.\n\t * @return arguments\n\t */\n\tprotected @Nullable Object @Nullable [] getArguments() {\n\t\treturn arguments;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return the object on which the method will be invoked.\n\t * @since 5.1\n\t */\n\tprotected @Nullable Object getTargetObject() {\n\t\treturn targetObject;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return the name of the method to be invoked.\n\t * @since 5.1\n\t */\n\tprotected @Nullable String getTargetMethod() {\n\t\treturn targetMethod;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Used to wrap a {@link Throwable} (not an {@link Exception}) thrown by a\n\t * reflectively-invoked delegate.\n\t *\n\t * @author Robert Kasanicky\n\t */\n\tpublic static class InvocationTargetThrowableWrapper extends RuntimeException {\n\n\t\tpublic InvocationTargetThrowableWrapper(@Nullable Throwable cause) {\n\t\t\tsuper(cause);\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/adapter/PropertyExtractingDelegatingItemWriter.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.adapter;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.Chunk;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemWriter;\nimport org.springframework.beans.BeanWrapper;\nimport org.springframework.beans.BeanWrapperImpl;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\nimport org.springframework.util.ObjectUtils;\n\n/**\n * Delegates processing to a custom method - extracts property values from item object and\n * uses them as arguments for the delegate method.\n *\n * <p>\n * This writer is thread-safe as long as the delegate <code>ItemWriter</code> is\n * thread-safe.\n * </p>\n *\n * @see ItemWriterAdapter\n * @author Robert Kasanicky\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n */\npublic class PropertyExtractingDelegatingItemWriter<T> extends AbstractMethodInvokingDelegator<T>\n\t\timplements ItemWriter<T> {\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String @Nullable [] fieldsUsedAsTargetMethodArguments;\n\n\t/**\n\t * Extracts values from item's fields named in fieldsUsedAsTargetMethodArguments and\n\t * passes them as arguments to the delegate method.\n\t */\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void write(Chunk<? extends T> items) throws Exception {\n\t\tfor (T item : items) {\n\n\t\t\t// helper for extracting property values from a bean\n\t\t\tBeanWrapper beanWrapper = new BeanWrapperImpl(item);\n\n\t\t\t@Nullable Object[] methodArguments = new Object[fieldsUsedAsTargetMethodArguments.length];\n\n\t\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < fieldsUsedAsTargetMethodArguments.length; i++) {\n\t\t\t\tmethodArguments[i] = beanWrapper.getPropertyValue(fieldsUsedAsTargetMethodArguments[i]);\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tinvokeDelegateMethodWithArguments(methodArguments);\n\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void afterPropertiesSet() throws Exception {\n\t\tsuper.afterPropertiesSet();\n\t\tAssert.state(!ObjectUtils.isEmpty(fieldsUsedAsTargetMethodArguments),\n\t\t\t\t\"fieldsUsedAsTargetMethodArguments must not be empty\");\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param fieldsUsedAsMethodArguments the values of the these item's fields will be\n\t * used as arguments for the delegate method. Nested property values are supported,\n\t * e.g. <code>address.city</code>\n\t */\n\tpublic void setFieldsUsedAsTargetMethodArguments(String[] fieldsUsedAsMethodArguments) {\n\t\tthis.fieldsUsedAsTargetMethodArguments = fieldsUsedAsMethodArguments.clone();\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2019 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.avro.builder;\n\nimport org.apache.avro.Schema;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ExecutionContext;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemStreamSupport;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.support.AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.avro.AvroItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.core.io.ByteArrayResource;\nimport org.springframework.core.io.Resource;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\nimport org.springframework.util.StringUtils;\n\n/**\n * A builder implementation for the {@link AvroItemReader}.\n *\n * @author David Turanski\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Stefano Cordio\n * @since 4.2\n */\npublic class AvroItemReaderBuilder<T> {\n\n\tprivate boolean saveState = true;\n\n\tprivate String name = AvroItemReader.class.getSimpleName();\n\n\tprivate int maxItemCount = Integer.MAX_VALUE;\n\n\tprivate int currentItemCount;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Resource schema;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Resource resource;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Class<T> type;\n\n\tprivate boolean embeddedSchema = true;\n\n\t/**\n\t * Configure a {@link Resource} containing Avro serialized objects.\n\t * @param resource an existing Resource.\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder.\n\t */\n\tpublic AvroItemReaderBuilder<T> resource(Resource resource) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(resource, \"A 'resource' is required.\");\n\t\tAssert.state(resource.exists(), \"Resource \" + resource.getFilename() + \" does not exist.\");\n\t\tthis.resource = resource;\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Configure an Avro {@link Schema} from a {@link Resource}.\n\t * @param schema an existing schema Resource.\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder.\n\t */\n\tpublic AvroItemReaderBuilder<T> schema(Resource schema) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(schema, \"A 'schema' Resource is required.\");\n\t\tAssert.state(schema.exists(), \"Resource \" + schema.getFilename() + \" does not exist.\");\n\t\tthis.schema = schema;\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Configure an Avro {@link Schema} from a String.\n\t * @param schemaString the schema String.\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder.\n\t */\n\tpublic AvroItemReaderBuilder<T> schema(String schemaString) {\n\t\tAssert.hasText(schemaString, \"A 'schema' is required.\");\n\t\tthis.schema = new ByteArrayResource(schemaString.getBytes());\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Configure a type to be deserialized.\n\t * @param type the class to be deserialized.\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder.\n\t */\n\tpublic AvroItemReaderBuilder<T> type(Class<T> type) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(type, \"A 'type' is required.\");\n\t\tthis.type = type;\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Disable or enable reading an embedded Avro schema. True by default.\n\t * @param embeddedSchema set to false to if the input does not contain an Avro schema.\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder.\n\t */\n\tpublic AvroItemReaderBuilder<T> embeddedSchema(boolean embeddedSchema) {\n\t\tthis.embeddedSchema = embeddedSchema;\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Configure if the state of the {@link ItemStreamSupport} should be persisted within\n\t * the {@link ExecutionContext} for restart purposes.\n\t * @param saveState defaults to true\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder.\n\t */\n\tpublic AvroItemReaderBuilder<T> saveState(boolean saveState) {\n\t\tthis.saveState = saveState;\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The name used to calculate the key within the {@link ExecutionContext}. Required if\n\t * {@link #saveState(boolean)} is set to true.\n\t * @param name name of the reader instance\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder.\n\t * @see ItemStreamSupport#setName(String)\n\t */\n\tpublic AvroItemReaderBuilder<T> name(String name) {\n\t\tthis.name = name;\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Configure the max number of items to be read.\n\t * @param maxItemCount the max items to be read\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder.\n\t * @see AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader#setMaxItemCount(int)\n\t */\n\tpublic AvroItemReaderBuilder<T> maxItemCount(int maxItemCount) {\n\t\tthis.maxItemCount = maxItemCount;\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Index for the current item. Used on restarts to indicate where to start from.\n\t * @param currentItemCount current index\n\t * @return this instance for method chaining\n\t * @see AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader#setCurrentItemCount(int)\n\t */\n\tpublic AvroItemReaderBuilder<T> currentItemCount(int currentItemCount) {\n\t\tthis.currentItemCount = currentItemCount;\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Build an instance of {@link AvroItemReader}.\n\t * @return the instance;\n\t */\n\tpublic AvroItemReader<T> build() {\n\t\tAvroItemReader<T> avroItemReader;\n\n\t\tAssert.notNull(this.resource, \"A 'resource' is required.\");\n\n\t\tif (this.type != null) {\n\t\t\tAssert.isNull(this.schema, \"You cannot specify a schema and 'type'.\");\n\t\t\tavroItemReader = new AvroItemReader<>(this.resource, this.type);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\tAssert.notNull(this.schema, \"'schema' is required.\");\n\t\t\tavroItemReader = new AvroItemReader<>(this.resource, this.schema);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tavroItemReader.setSaveState(this.saveState);\n\n\t\tif (this.saveState) {\n\t\t\tAssert.state(StringUtils.hasText(this.name), \"A name is required when saveState is set to true.\");\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tavroItemReader.setName(this.name);\n\t\tavroItemReader.setCurrentItemCount(this.currentItemCount);\n\t\tavroItemReader.setMaxItemCount(this.maxItemCount);\n\t\tavroItemReader.setEmbeddedSchema(this.embeddedSchema);\n\n\t\treturn avroItemReader;\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2019 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.avro.builder;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ExecutionContext;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemStreamSupport;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.avro.AvroItemWriter;\nimport org.springframework.core.io.ByteArrayResource;\nimport org.springframework.core.io.Resource;\nimport org.springframework.core.io.WritableResource;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\n\n/**\n * A builder implementation for the {@link AvroItemWriter}.\n *\n * @author David Turanski\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Stefano Cordio\n * @since 4.2\n */\npublic class AvroItemWriterBuilder<T> {\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Class<T> type;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable WritableResource resource;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Resource schema;\n\n\tprivate String name = AvroItemWriter.class.getSimpleName();\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param resource the {@link WritableResource} used to write the serialized data.\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder.\n\t */\n\tpublic AvroItemWriterBuilder<T> resource(WritableResource resource) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(resource, \"A 'resource' is required.\");\n\t\tthis.resource = resource;\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param schema the Resource containing the schema JSON used to serialize the output.\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder.\n\t */\n\tpublic AvroItemWriterBuilder<T> schema(Resource schema) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(schema, \"A 'schema' is required.\");\n\t\tAssert.state(schema.exists(), \"Resource \" + schema.getFilename() + \"does not exist.\");\n\t\tthis.schema = schema;\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param schemaString the String containing the schema JSON used to serialize the\n\t * output.\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder.\n\t */\n\tpublic AvroItemWriterBuilder<T> schema(String schemaString) {\n\t\tAssert.hasText(schemaString, \"A 'schemaString' is required.\");\n\t\tthis.schema = new ByteArrayResource(schemaString.getBytes());\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param type the Class of objects to be serialized.\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder.\n\t */\n\tpublic AvroItemWriterBuilder<T> type(Class<T> type) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(type, \"A 'type' is required.\");\n\t\tthis.type = type;\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The name used to calculate the key within the {@link ExecutionContext}.\n\t * @param name name of the reader instance\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder.\n\t * @see ItemStreamSupport#setName(String)\n\t */\n\tpublic AvroItemWriterBuilder<T> name(String name) {\n\t\tAssert.hasText(name, \"A 'name' is required.\");\n\t\tthis.name = name;\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Build an instance of {@link AvroItemWriter}.\n\t * @return the instance;\n\t */\n\tpublic AvroItemWriter<T> build() {\n\n\t\tAssert.notNull(this.resource, \"A 'resource' is required.\");\n\n\t\tAssert.notNull(this.type, \"A 'type' is required.\");\n\n\t\tAvroItemWriter<T> avroItemWriter = this.schema != null\n\t\t\t\t? new AvroItemWriter<>(this.resource, this.schema, this.type)\n\t\t\t\t: new AvroItemWriter<>(this.resource, this.type);\n\t\tavroItemWriter.setName(this.name);\n\t\treturn avroItemWriter;\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/data/AbstractPaginatedDataItemReader.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2013-2024 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.data;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemStreamReader;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.support.AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\n\nimport java.util.Iterator;\nimport java.util.concurrent.locks.Lock;\nimport java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\n/**\n * A base class that handles basic reading logic based on the paginated semantics of\n * Spring Data's paginated facilities. It also handles the semantics required for\n * restartability based on those facilities.\n * <p>\n * This reader is <b>not</b> thread-safe.\n *\n * @author Michael Minella\n * @author Glenn Renfro\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Stefano Cordio\n * @since 2.2\n * @param <T> Type of item to be read\n */\npublic abstract class AbstractPaginatedDataItemReader<T> extends AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader<T> {\n\n\tprotected volatile int page = 0;\n\n\tprotected int pageSize = 10;\n\n\tprotected @Nullable Iterator<T> results;\n\n\tprivate final Lock lock = new ReentrantLock();\n\n\t/**\n\t * The number of items to be read with each page.\n\t * @param pageSize the number of items. pageSize must be greater than zero.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setPageSize(int pageSize) {\n\t\tAssert.isTrue(pageSize > 0, \"pageSize must be greater than zero\");\n\t\tthis.pageSize = pageSize;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected @Nullable T doRead() throws Exception {\n\n\t\tthis.lock.lock();\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tif (results == null || !results.hasNext()) {\n\n\t\t\t\tresults = doPageRead();\n\n\t\t\t\tpage++;\n\n\t\t\t\tif (!results.hasNext()) {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\treturn results.next();\n\t\t}\n\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\tthis.lock.unlock();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Method this {@link ItemStreamReader} delegates to for the actual work of reading a\n\t * page. Each time this method is called, the resulting {@link Iterator} should\n\t * contain the items read within the next page. <br>\n\t * <br>\n\t * If the {@link Iterator} is empty when it is returned, this {@link ItemReader} will\n\t * assume that the input has been exhausted.\n\t * @return an {@link Iterator} containing the items within a page.\n\t */\n\tprotected abstract Iterator<T> doPageRead();\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void doOpen() throws Exception {\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void doClose() throws Exception {\n\t\tthis.lock.lock();\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tthis.page = 0;\n\t\t\tthis.results = null;\n\t\t}\n\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\tthis.lock.unlock();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void jumpToItem(int itemLastIndex) throws Exception {\n\t\tthis.lock.lock();\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tpage = itemLastIndex / pageSize;\n\t\t\tint current = itemLastIndex % pageSize;\n\n\t\t\tIterator<T> initialPage = doPageRead();\n\n\t\t\tfor (; current >= 0; current--) {\n\t\t\t\tinitialPage.next();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\tthis.lock.unlock();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2023-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.data;\n\nimport java.time.Duration;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Iterator;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Map;\nimport java.util.stream.Stream;\n\nimport org.bson.Document;\nimport org.bson.codecs.DecoderContext;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.support.AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.beans.factory.InitializingBean;\nimport org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;\nimport org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoOperations;\nimport org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.BasicQuery;\nimport org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;\nimport org.springframework.data.mongodb.util.json.ParameterBindingDocumentCodec;\nimport org.springframework.data.mongodb.util.json.ParameterBindingJsonReader;\nimport org.springframework.data.util.CloseableIterator;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\nimport org.springframework.util.StringUtils;\n\n/**\n * Cursor-based {@link ItemReader} implementation for MongoDB.\n *\n * @author LEE Juchan\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Jimmy Praet\n * @since 5.1\n */\npublic class MongoCursorItemReader<T> extends AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader<T> implements InitializingBean {\n\n\tprivate MongoOperations template;\n\n\tprivate Class<? extends T> targetType;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String collection;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Query query;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String queryString;\n\n\tprivate List<Object> parameterValues = new ArrayList<>();\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String fields;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Sort sort;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String hint;\n\n\tprivate int batchSize;\n\n\tprivate int limit;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Duration maxTime;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable CloseableIterator<? extends T> cursor;\n\n\t/**\n\t * Create a new {@link MongoCursorItemReader}.\n\t * @param template the {@link MongoOperations} to use\n\t * @param targetType the target type\n\t * @since 6.0\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReader(MongoOperations template, Class<? extends T> targetType) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(template, \"MongoOperations must not be null\");\n\t\tAssert.notNull(targetType, \"Target type must not be null\");\n\t\tthis.template = template;\n\t\tthis.targetType = targetType;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Used to perform operations against the MongoDB instance. Also handles the mapping\n\t * of documents to objects.\n\t * @param template the MongoOperations instance to use\n\t * @see MongoOperations\n\t */\n\tpublic void setTemplate(MongoOperations template) {\n\t\tthis.template = template;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The targetType of object to be returned for each {@link #read()} call.\n\t * @param targetType the targetType of object to return\n\t */\n\tpublic void setTargetType(Class<? extends T> targetType) {\n\t\tthis.targetType = targetType;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param collection Mongo collection to be queried.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setCollection(String collection) {\n\t\tthis.collection = collection;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * A Mongo Query to be used.\n\t * @param query Mongo Query to be used.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setQuery(Query query) {\n\t\tthis.query = query;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * A JSON formatted MongoDB query. Parameterization of the provided query is allowed\n\t * via ?&lt;index&gt; placeholders where the &lt;index&gt; indicates the index of the\n\t * parameterValue to substitute.\n\t * @param queryString JSON formatted Mongo query\n\t */\n\tpublic void setQuery(String queryString) {\n\t\tthis.queryString = queryString;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * {@link List} of values to be substituted in for each of the parameters in the\n\t * query.\n\t * @param parameterValues values\n\t */\n\tpublic void setParameterValues(List<Object> parameterValues) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(parameterValues, \"Parameter values must not be null\");\n\t\tthis.parameterValues = parameterValues;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * JSON defining the fields to be returned from the matching documents by MongoDB.\n\t * @param fields JSON string that identifies the fields to sort by.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setFields(String fields) {\n\t\tthis.fields = fields;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * {@link Map} of property\n\t * names/{@link org.springframework.data.domain.Sort.Direction} values to sort the\n\t * input by.\n\t * @param sorts map of properties and direction to sort each.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setSort(Map<String, Sort.Direction> sorts) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(sorts, \"Sorts must not be null\");\n\t\tthis.sort = convertToSort(sorts);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * JSON String telling MongoDB what index to use.\n\t * @param hint string indicating what index to use.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setHint(String hint) {\n\t\tthis.hint = hint;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The size of batches to use when iterating over results.\n\t * @param batchSize size the batch size to apply to the cursor\n\t * @see Query#cursorBatchSize(int)\n\t */\n\tpublic void setBatchSize(int batchSize) {\n\t\tthis.batchSize = batchSize;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The query limit.\n\t * @param limit The limit\n\t * @see Query#limit(int)\n\t */\n\tpublic void setLimit(int limit) {\n\t\tthis.limit = limit;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The maximum execution time for the query\n\t * @param maxTime The max time\n\t * @see Query#maxTime(Duration)\n\t */\n\tpublic void setMaxTime(Duration maxTime) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(maxTime, \"maxTime must not be null.\");\n\t\tthis.maxTime = maxTime;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Checks mandatory properties\n\t *\n\t * @see InitializingBean#afterPropertiesSet()\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void afterPropertiesSet() {\n\t\tAssert.state(queryString != null || query != null, \"A query is required.\");\n\n\t\tif (queryString != null) {\n\t\t\tAssert.state(sort != null, \"A sort is required.\");\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void doOpen() throws Exception {\n\t\tQuery mongoQuery = queryString != null ? createQuery() : query;\n\n\t\tStream<? extends T> stream;\n\t\tif (StringUtils.hasText(collection)) {\n\t\t\tstream = template.stream(mongoQuery, targetType, collection);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\tstream = template.stream(mongoQuery, targetType);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tthis.cursor = streamToIterator(stream);\n\t}\n\n\tprivate Query createQuery() {\n\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t\tString populatedQuery = replacePlaceholders(queryString, parameterValues);\n\n\t\tQuery mongoQuery;\n\t\tif (StringUtils.hasText(fields)) {\n\t\t\tmongoQuery = new BasicQuery(populatedQuery, fields);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\tmongoQuery = new BasicQuery(populatedQuery);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (sort != null) {\n\t\t\tmongoQuery.with(sort);\n\t\t}\n\t\tif (StringUtils.hasText(hint)) {\n\t\t\tmongoQuery.withHint(hint);\n\t\t}\n\t\tmongoQuery.cursorBatchSize(batchSize);\n\t\tmongoQuery.limit(limit);\n\t\tif (maxTime != null) {\n\t\t\tmongoQuery.maxTime(maxTime);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\tmongoQuery.noCursorTimeout();\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn mongoQuery;\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tprotected T doRead() throws Exception {\n\t\treturn cursor.hasNext() ? cursor.next() : null;\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void doClose() throws Exception {\n\t\tthis.cursor.close();\n\t}\n\n\tprivate Sort convertToSort(Map<String, Sort.Direction> sorts) {\n\t\tList<Sort.Order> sortValues = new ArrayList<>(sorts.size());\n\n\t\tfor (Map.Entry<String, Sort.Direction> curSort : sorts.entrySet()) {\n\t\t\tsortValues.add(new Sort.Order(curSort.getValue(), curSort.getKey()));\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn Sort.by(sortValues);\n\t}\n\n\tprivate String replacePlaceholders(String input, List<Object> values) {\n\t\tParameterBindingJsonReader reader = new ParameterBindingJsonReader(input, values.toArray());\n\t\tDecoderContext decoderContext = DecoderContext.builder().build();\n\t\tDocument document = new ParameterBindingDocumentCodec().decode(reader, decoderContext);\n\t\treturn document.toJson();\n\t}\n\n\tprivate CloseableIterator<? extends T> streamToIterator(Stream<? extends T> stream) {\n\t\treturn new CloseableIterator<>() {\n\t\t\tfinal private Iterator<? extends T> delegate = stream.iterator();\n\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic boolean hasNext() {\n\t\t\t\treturn delegate.hasNext();\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic T next() {\n\t\t\t\treturn delegate.next();\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic void close() {\n\t\t\t\tstream.close();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t};\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2012-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.data;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Iterator;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Map;\n\nimport org.bson.Document;\nimport org.bson.codecs.DecoderContext;\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ExecutionContext;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.beans.factory.InitializingBean;\nimport org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;\nimport org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;\nimport org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;\nimport org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoOperations;\nimport org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.BasicQuery;\nimport org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;\nimport org.springframework.data.mongodb.util.json.ParameterBindingDocumentCodec;\nimport org.springframework.data.mongodb.util.json.ParameterBindingJsonReader;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\nimport org.springframework.util.ClassUtils;\nimport org.springframework.util.StringUtils;\n\n/**\n * <p>\n * Restartable {@link ItemReader} that reads documents from MongoDB via a paging\n * technique.\n * </p>\n *\n * <p>\n * If you set JSON String query {@link #setQuery(String)} then it executes the JSON to\n * retrieve the requested documents.\n * </p>\n *\n * <p>\n * If you set Query object {@link #setQuery(Query)} then it executes the Query to retrieve\n * the requested documents.\n * </p>\n *\n * <p>\n * The query is executed using paged requests specified in the {@link #setPageSize(int)}.\n * Additional pages are requested as needed to provide data when the {@link #read()}\n * method is called.\n * </p>\n *\n * <p>\n * The JSON String query provided supports parameter substitution via ?&lt;index&gt;\n * placeholders where the &lt;index&gt; indicates the index of the parameterValue to\n * substitute.\n * </p>\n *\n * <p>\n * The implementation is thread-safe between calls to {@link #open(ExecutionContext)}, but\n * remember to use <code>saveState=false</code> if used in a multi-threaded client (no\n * restart available).\n * </p>\n *\n * @param <T> type of items to read\n * @since 5.1\n * @author Michael Minella\n * @author Takaaki Iida\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Parikshit Dutta\n * @author Jimmy Praet\n */\npublic class MongoPagingItemReader<T> extends AbstractPaginatedDataItemReader<T> implements InitializingBean {\n\n\tprotected MongoOperations template;\n\n\tprotected @Nullable Query query;\n\n\tprotected @Nullable String queryString;\n\n\tprotected Class<? extends T> type;\n\n\tprotected @Nullable Sort sort;\n\n\tprotected @Nullable String hint;\n\n\tprotected @Nullable String fields;\n\n\tprotected @Nullable String collection;\n\n\tprotected List<Object> parameterValues = new ArrayList<>();\n\n\t/**\n\t * Create a new instance of {@link MongoPagingItemReader}.\n\t * @param template the {@link MongoOperations} to use\n\t * @param type the target type\n\t * @since 6.0\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoPagingItemReader(MongoOperations template, Class<? extends T> type) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(template, \"MongoOperations must not be null\");\n\t\tAssert.notNull(type, \"Target type must not be null\");\n\t\tthis.template = template;\n\t\tthis.type = type;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * A Mongo Query to be used.\n\t * @param query Mongo Query to be used.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setQuery(Query query) {\n\t\tthis.query = query;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Used to perform operations against the MongoDB instance. Also handles the mapping\n\t * of documents to objects.\n\t * @param template the MongoOperations instance to use\n\t * @see MongoOperations\n\t */\n\tpublic void setTemplate(MongoOperations template) {\n\t\tthis.template = template;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * A JSON formatted MongoDB query. Parameterization of the provided query is allowed\n\t * via ?&lt;index&gt; placeholders where the &lt;index&gt; indicates the index of the\n\t * parameterValue to substitute.\n\t * @param queryString JSON formatted Mongo query\n\t */\n\tpublic void setQuery(String queryString) {\n\t\tthis.queryString = queryString;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The type of object to be returned for each {@link #read()} call.\n\t * @param type the type of object to return\n\t */\n\tpublic void setTargetType(Class<? extends T> type) {\n\t\tthis.type = type;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * {@link List} of values to be substituted in for each of the parameters in the\n\t * query.\n\t * @param parameterValues values\n\t */\n\tpublic void setParameterValues(List<Object> parameterValues) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(parameterValues, \"Parameter values must not be null\");\n\t\tthis.parameterValues = parameterValues;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * JSON defining the fields to be returned from the matching documents by MongoDB.\n\t * @param fields JSON string that identifies the fields to sort by.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setFields(String fields) {\n\t\tthis.fields = fields;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * {@link Map} of property\n\t * names/{@link org.springframework.data.domain.Sort.Direction} values to sort the\n\t * input by.\n\t * @param sorts map of properties and direction to sort each.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setSort(Map<String, Sort.Direction> sorts) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(sorts, \"Sorts must not be null\");\n\t\tthis.sort = convertToSort(sorts);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param collection Mongo collection to be queried.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setCollection(String collection) {\n\t\tthis.collection = collection;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * JSON String telling MongoDB what index to use.\n\t * @param hint string indicating what index to use.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setHint(String hint) {\n\t\tthis.hint = hint;\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings({ \"unchecked\", \"DataFlowIssue\" })\n\t@Override\n\tprotected Iterator<T> doPageRead() {\n\t\tif (queryString != null) {\n\t\t\tPageable pageRequest = PageRequest.of(page, pageSize, sort);\n\n\t\t\tString populatedQuery = replacePlaceholders(queryString, parameterValues);\n\n\t\t\tQuery mongoQuery;\n\n\t\t\tif (StringUtils.hasText(fields)) {\n\t\t\t\tmongoQuery = new BasicQuery(populatedQuery, fields);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\tmongoQuery = new BasicQuery(populatedQuery);\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tmongoQuery.with(pageRequest);\n\n\t\t\tif (StringUtils.hasText(hint)) {\n\t\t\t\tmongoQuery.withHint(hint);\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\treturn StringUtils.hasText(collection) //\n\t\t\t\t\t? (Iterator<T>) template.find(mongoQuery, type, collection).iterator()\n\t\t\t\t\t: (Iterator<T>) template.find(mongoQuery, type).iterator();\n\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\tPageable pageRequest = PageRequest.of(page, pageSize);\n\t\t\tquery.with(pageRequest);\n\n\t\t\treturn StringUtils.hasText(collection) //\n\t\t\t\t\t? (Iterator<T>) template.find(query, type, collection).iterator()\n\t\t\t\t\t: (Iterator<T>) template.find(query, type).iterator();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Checks mandatory properties\n\t *\n\t * @see InitializingBean#afterPropertiesSet()\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void afterPropertiesSet() throws Exception {\n\t\tAssert.state(queryString != null || query != null, \"A query is required.\");\n\n\t\tif (queryString != null) {\n\t\t\tAssert.state(sort != null, \"A sort is required.\");\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tprotected String replacePlaceholders(String input, List<Object> values) {\n\t\tParameterBindingJsonReader reader = new ParameterBindingJsonReader(input, values.toArray());\n\t\tDecoderContext decoderContext = DecoderContext.builder().build();\n\t\tDocument document = new ParameterBindingDocumentCodec().decode(reader, decoderContext);\n\t\treturn document.toJson();\n\t}\n\n\tprotected Sort convertToSort(Map<String, Sort.Direction> sorts) {\n\t\tList<Sort.Order> sortValues = new ArrayList<>(sorts.size());\n\n\t\tfor (Map.Entry<String, Sort.Direction> curSort : sorts.entrySet()) {\n\t\t\tsortValues.add(new Sort.Order(curSort.getValue(), curSort.getKey()));\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn Sort.by(sortValues);\n\t}\n\n}"

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/data/RepositoryItemReader.java",

      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2012-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.data;\n\nimport java.lang.reflect.InvocationTargetException;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Map;\nimport java.util.concurrent.locks.Lock;\nimport java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock;\n\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.Log;\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.LogFactory;\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ExecutionContext;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.adapter.AbstractMethodInvokingDelegator.InvocationTargetThrowableWrapper;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.adapter.DynamicMethodInvocationException;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.support.AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.beans.factory.InitializingBean;\nimport org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;\nimport org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;\nimport org.springframework.data.domain.Slice;\nimport org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;\nimport org.springframework.data.repository.PagingAndSortingRepository;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\nimport org.springframework.util.ClassUtils;\nimport org.springframework.util.MethodInvoker;\nimport org.springframework.util.StringUtils;\n\n/**\n * <p>\n * A {@link ItemReader} that reads records utilizing a {@link PagingAndSortingRepository}.\n * </p>\n *\n * <p>\n * Performance of the reader is dependent on the repository implementation, however\n * setting a reasonably large page size and matching that to the commit interval should\n * yield better performance.\n * </p>\n *\n * <p>\n * The reader must be configured with a {@link PagingAndSortingRepository}, a\n * {@link Sort}, and a pageSize greater than 0.\n * </p>\n *\n * <p>\n * This implementation is thread-safe between calls to {@link #open(ExecutionContext)},\n * but remember to use <code>saveState=false</code> if used in a multi-threaded client (no\n * restart available).\n * </p>\n *\n * <p>\n * It is important to note that this is a paging item reader and exceptions that are\n * thrown while reading the page itself (mapping results to objects, etc in the\n * {@link RepositoryItemReader#doPageRead()}) will not be skippable since this reader has\n * no way of knowing if an exception should be skipped and therefore will continue to read\n * the same page until the skip limit is exceeded.\n * </p>\n *\n * <p>\n * NOTE: The {@code RepositoryItemReader} only reads Java Objects i.e. non primitives.\n * </p>\n *\n * @author Michael Minella\n * @author Antoine Kapps\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Jimmy Praet\n * @since 2.2\n */\npublic class RepositoryItemReader<T> extends AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader<T> implements InitializingBean {\n\n\tprotected Log logger = LogFactory.getLog(getClass());\n\n\tprivate PagingAndSortingRepository<?, ?> repository;\n\n\tprivate Map<String, Sort.Direction> sorts;\n\n\tprivate volatile int page = 0;\n\n\tprivate int pageSize = 10;\n\n\tprivate volatile int current = 0;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable List<?> arguments;\n\n\tprivate volatile @Nullable List<T> results;\n\n\tprivate final Lock lock = new ReentrantLock();\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String methodName;\n\n\t/**\n\t * Create a new {@link RepositoryItemReader}.\n\t * @param repository the {@link PagingAndSortingRepository} to use\n\t * @param sorts the sort parameters to pass to the repository\n\t * @since 6.0\n\t */\n\tpublic RepositoryItemReader(PagingAndSortingRepository<?, ?> repository, Map<String, Sort.Direction> sorts) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(repository, \"A PagingAndSortingRepository is required.\");\n\t\tAssert.notNull(sorts, \"A Map of sorts is required.\");\n\t\tthis.repository = repository;\n\t\tthis.sorts = sorts;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Arguments to be passed to the data providing method.\n\t * @param arguments list of method arguments to be passed to the repository\n\t */\n\tpublic void setArguments(List<?> arguments) {\n\t\tthis.arguments = arguments;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Provides ordering of the results so that order is maintained between paged queries.\n\t * Use a {@link java.util.LinkedHashMap} in case of multiple sort entries to keep the\n\t * order.\n\t * @param sorts the fields to sort by and the directions\n\t */\n\tpublic void setSorts(Map<String, Sort.Direction> sorts) {\n\t\tthis.sorts = sorts;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param pageSize The number of items to retrieve per page. Must be greater than 0.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setPageSize(int pageSize) {\n\t\tthis.pageSize = pageSize;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The {@link PagingAndSortingRepository} implementation used to read input from.\n\t * @param repository underlying repository for input to be read from.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setRepository(PagingAndSortingRepository<?, ?> repository) {\n\t\tthis.repository = repository;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Specifies what method on the repository to call. This method must take\n\t * {@link org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable} as the <em>last</em> argument.\n\t * @param methodName name of the method to invoke\n\t */\n\tpublic void setMethodName(String methodName) {\n\t\tthis.methodName = methodName;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void afterPropertiesSet() throws Exception {\n\t\tAssert.state(pageSize > 0, \"Page size must be greater than 0\");\n\t\tAssert.state(this.methodName != null && !this.methodName.isEmpty(), \"methodName is required.\");\n\t\tif (isSaveState()) {\n\t\t\tAssert.state(StringUtils.hasText(getName()), \"A name is required when saveState is set to true.\");\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected @Nullable T doRead() throws Exception {\n\n\t\tthis.lock.lock();\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tboolean nextPageNeeded = (results != null && current >= results.size());\n\n\t\t\tif (results == null || nextPageNeeded) {\n\n\t\t\t\tif (logger.isDebugEnabled()) {\n\t\t\t\t\tlogger.debug(\"Reading page \" + page);\n\t\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t\tresults = doPageRead();\n\t\t\t\tpage++;\n\n\t\t\t\tif (results.isEmpty()) {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t\tif (nextPageNeeded) {\n\t\t\t\t\tcurrent = 0;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif (current < results.size()) {\n\t\t\t\tT curLine = results.get(current);\n\t\t\t\tcurrent++;\n\t\t\t\treturn curLine;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\tthis.lock.unlock();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void jumpToItem(int itemLastIndex) throws Exception {\n\t\tthis.lock.lock();\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tpage = itemLastIndex / pageSize;\n\t\t\tcurrent = itemLastIndex % pageSize;\n\t\t}\n\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\tthis.lock.unlock();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Performs the actual reading of a page via the repository. Available for overriding\n\t * as needed.\n\t * @return the list of items that make up the page\n\t * @throws Exception Based on what the underlying method throws or related to the\n\t * calling of the method\n\t */\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"unchecked\")\n\tprotected List<T> doPageRead() throws Exception {\n\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t\tPageable pageRequest = PageRequest.of(page, pageSize, convertToSort(sorts));\n\n\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t\tMethodInvoker invoker = createMethodInvoker(repository, methodName);\n\n\t\tList<Object> parameters = new ArrayList<>();\n\n\t\tif (arguments != null && !arguments.isEmpty()) {\n\t\t\tparameters.addAll(arguments);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tparameters.add(pageRequest);\n\n\t\tinvoker.setArguments(parameters.toArray());\n\n\t\tSlice<T> curPage = (Slice<T>) doInvoke(invoker);\n\n\t\treturn curPage.getContent();\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void doOpen() throws Exception {\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void doClose() throws Exception {\n\t\tthis.lock.lock();\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tcurrent = 0;\n\t\t\tpage = 0;\n\t\t\tresults = null;\n\t\t}\n\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\tthis.lock.unlock();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tprivate Sort convertToSort(Map<String, Sort.Direction> sorts) {\n\t\tList<Sort.Order> sortValues = new ArrayList<>();\n\n\t\tfor (Map.Entry<String, Sort.Direction> curSort : sorts.entrySet()) {\n\t\t\tsortValues.add(new Sort.Order(curSort.getValue(), curSort.getKey()));\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn Sort.by(sortValues);\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\tprivate Object doInvoke(MethodInvoker invoker) throws Exception {\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tinvoker.prepare();\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (ClassNotFoundException | NoSuchMethodException e) {\n\t\t\tthrow new DynamicMethodInvocationException(e);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\treturn invoker.invoke();\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (InvocationTargetException e) {\n\t\t\tif (e.getCause() instanceof Exception) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow (Exception) e.getCause();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new InvocationTargetThrowableWrapper(e.getCause());\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (IllegalAccessException e) {\n\t\t\tthrow new DynamicMethodInvocationException(e);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tprivate MethodInvoker createMethodInvoker(Object targetObject, String targetMethod) {\n\t\tMethodInvoker invoker = new MethodInvoker();\n\t\tinvoker.setTargetObject(targetObject);\n\t\tinvoker.setTargetMethod(targetMethod);\n\t\treturn invoker;\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },
