      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/data/builder/MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2023-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.data.builder;\n\nimport java.time.Duration;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Map;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ExecutionContext;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemStreamSupport;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.support.AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.data.MongoCursorItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;\nimport org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoOperations;\nimport org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\nimport org.springframework.util.StringUtils;\n\n/**\n * @author LEE Juchan\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Stefano Cordio\n * @since 5.1\n * @see MongoCursorItemReader\n */\npublic class MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder<T> {\n\n\tprivate boolean saveState = true;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String name;\n\n\tprivate int maxItemCount = Integer.MAX_VALUE;\n\n\tprivate int currentItemCount;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable MongoOperations template;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Class<? extends T> targetType;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String collection;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Query query;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String jsonQuery;\n\n\tprivate List<Object> parameterValues = new ArrayList<>();\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String fields;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Map<String, Sort.Direction> sorts;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String hint;\n\n\tprivate int batchSize;\n\n\tprivate int limit;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Duration maxTime;\n\n\t/**\n\t * Configure if the state of the {@link ItemStreamSupport} should be persisted within\n\t * the {@link ExecutionContext} for restart purposes.\n\t * @param saveState defaults to true\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder.\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder<T> saveState(boolean saveState) {\n\t\tthis.saveState = saveState;\n\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The name used to calculate the key within the {@link ExecutionContext}. Required if\n\t * {@link #saveState(boolean)} is set to true.\n\t * @param name name of the reader instance\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder.\n\t * @see ItemStreamSupport#setName(String)\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder<T> name(String name) {\n\t\tthis.name = name;\n\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Configure the max number of items to be read.\n\t * @param maxItemCount the max items to be read\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder.\n\t * @see AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader#setMaxItemCount(int)\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder<T> maxItemCount(int maxItemCount) {\n\t\tthis.maxItemCount = maxItemCount;\n\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Index for the current item. Used on restarts to indicate where to start from.\n\t * @param currentItemCount current index\n\t * @return this instance for method chaining\n\t * @see AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader#setCurrentItemCount(int)\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder<T> currentItemCount(int currentItemCount) {\n\t\tthis.currentItemCount = currentItemCount;\n\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Used to perform operations against the MongoDB instance. Also handles the mapping\n\t * of documents to objects.\n\t * @param template the MongoOperations instance to use\n\t * @see MongoOperations\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder\n\t * @see MongoCursorItemReader#setTemplate(MongoOperations)\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder<T> template(MongoOperations template) {\n\t\tthis.template = template;\n\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The targetType of object to be returned for each\n\t * {@link MongoCursorItemReader#read()} call.\n\t * @param targetType the targetType of object to return\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder\n\t * @see MongoCursorItemReader#setTargetType(Class)\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder<T> targetType(Class<? extends T> targetType) {\n\t\tthis.targetType = targetType;\n\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Establish an optional collection that can be queried.\n\t * @param collection Mongo collection to be queried.\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder\n\t * @see MongoCursorItemReader#setCollection(String)\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder<T> collection(String collection) {\n\t\tthis.collection = collection;\n\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Provide a Spring Data Mongo {@link Query}. This will take precedence over a JSON\n\t * configured query.\n\t * @param query Query to execute\n\t * @return this instance for method chaining\n\t * @see MongoCursorItemReader#setQuery(Query)\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder<T> query(Query query) {\n\t\tthis.query = query;\n\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * A JSON formatted MongoDB jsonQuery. Parameterization of the provided jsonQuery is\n\t * allowed via ?&lt;index&gt; placeholders where the &lt;index&gt; indicates the index\n\t * of the parameterValue to substitute.\n\t * @param query JSON formatted Mongo jsonQuery\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder\n\t * @see MongoCursorItemReader#setQuery(String)\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder<T> jsonQuery(String query) {\n\t\tthis.jsonQuery = query;\n\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Values to be substituted in for each of the parameters in the query.\n\t * @param parameterValues values\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder\n\t * @see MongoCursorItemReader#setParameterValues(List)\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder<T> parameterValues(List<Object> parameterValues) {\n\t\tthis.parameterValues = parameterValues;\n\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * JSON defining the fields to be returned from the matching documents by MongoDB.\n\t * @param fields JSON string that identifies the fields to sort by.\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder\n\t * @see MongoCursorItemReader#setFields(String)\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder<T> fields(String fields) {\n\t\tthis.fields = fields;\n\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * {@link Map} of property\n\t * names/{@link org.springframework.data.domain.Sort.Direction} values to sort the\n\t * input by.\n\t * @param sorts map of properties and direction to sort each.\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder\n\t * @see MongoCursorItemReader#setSort(Map)\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder<T> sorts(Map<String, Sort.Direction> sorts) {\n\t\tthis.sorts = sorts;\n\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * JSON String telling MongoDB what index to use.\n\t * @param hint string indicating what index to use.\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder\n\t * @see MongoCursorItemReader#setHint(String)\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder<T> hint(String hint) {\n\t\tthis.hint = hint;\n\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The size of batches to use when iterating over results.\n\t * @param batchSize string indicating what index to use.\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder\n\t * @see MongoCursorItemReader#setHint(String)\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder<T> batchSize(int batchSize) {\n\t\tthis.batchSize = batchSize;\n\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The query limit\n\t * @param limit The limit\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder\n\t * @see MongoCursorItemReader#setLimit(int)\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder<T> limit(int limit) {\n\t\tthis.limit = limit;\n\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The maximum execution time for the query\n\t * @param maxTime The max time\n\t * @return The current instance of the builder\n\t * @see MongoCursorItemReader#setMaxTime(Duration)\n\t */\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReaderBuilder<T> maxTime(Duration maxTime) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(maxTime, \"maxTime must not be null.\");\n\t\tthis.maxTime = maxTime;\n\n\t\treturn this;\n\t}\n\n\tpublic MongoCursorItemReader<T> build() {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(this.template, \"template is required.\");\n\t\tif (this.saveState) {\n\t\t\tAssert.hasText(this.name, \"A name is required when saveState is set to true\");\n\t\t}\n\t\tAssert.notNull(this.targetType, \"targetType is required.\");\n\t\tAssert.state(StringUtils.hasText(this.jsonQuery) || this.query != null, \"A query is required\");\n\t\tAssert.notNull(this.sorts, \"sorts map is required.\");\n\n\t\tMongoCursorItemReader<T> reader = new MongoCursorItemReader<>(this.template, this.targetType);\n\t\treader.setSaveState(this.saveState);\n\t\tif (this.name != null) {\n\t\t\treader.setName(this.name);\n\t\t}\n\t\treader.setCurrentItemCount(this.currentItemCount);\n\t\treader.setMaxItemCount(this.maxItemCount);\n\n\t\treader.setTemplate(this.template);\n\t\treader.setTargetType(this.targetType);\n\t\tif (this.collection != null) {\n\t\t\treader.setCollection(this.collection);\n\t\t}\n\t\tif (this.query != null) {\n\t\t\treader.setQuery(this.query);\n\t\t}\n\t\tif (StringUtils.hasText(this.jsonQuery)) {\n\t\t\treader.setQuery(this.jsonQuery);\n\t\t}\n\t\treader.setParameterValues(this.parameterValues);\n\t\tif (this.fields != null) {\n\t\t\treader.setFields(this.fields);\n\t\t}\n\t\treader.setSort(this.sorts);\n\t\tif (this.hint != null) {\n\t\t\treader.setHint(this.hint);\n\t\t}\n\t\treader.setBatchSize(this.batchSize);\n\t\treader.setLimit(this.limit);\n\t\tif (this.maxTime != null) {\n\t\t\treader.setMaxTime(this.maxTime);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn reader;\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/database/AbstractPagingItemReader.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.database;\n\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.concurrent.locks.Lock;\nimport java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock;\n\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.Log;\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.LogFactory;\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemStreamReader;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.support.AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.beans.factory.InitializingBean;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\n\n/**\n * Abstract {@link ItemStreamReader} for to extend when reading database records in a\n * paging fashion.\n *\n * <p>\n * Implementations should execute queries using paged requests of a size specified in\n * {@link #setPageSize(int)}. Additional pages are requested when needed as\n * {@link #read()} method is called, returning an object corresponding to current\n * position.\n * </p>\n *\n * This reader is <b>not</b> thread-safe.\n *\n * @author Thomas Risberg\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Stefano Cordio\n * @author Jimmy Praet\n * @since 2.0\n */\npublic abstract class AbstractPagingItemReader<T> extends AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader<T>\n\t\timplements InitializingBean {\n\n\tprotected Log logger = LogFactory.getLog(getClass());\n\n\tprivate volatile boolean initialized = false;\n\n\tprivate int pageSize = 10;\n\n\tprivate volatile int current = 0;\n\n\tprivate volatile int page = 0;\n\n\tprotected volatile @Nullable List<T> results;\n\n\tprivate final Lock lock = new ReentrantLock();\n\n\tpublic AbstractPagingItemReader() {\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The current page number.\n\t * @return the current page\n\t */\n\tpublic int getPage() {\n\t\treturn page;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The page size configured for this reader.\n\t * @return the page size\n\t */\n\tpublic int getPageSize() {\n\t\treturn pageSize;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The number of rows to retrieve at a time.\n\t * @param pageSize the number of rows to fetch per page\n\t */\n\tpublic void setPageSize(int pageSize) {\n\t\tthis.pageSize = pageSize;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Check mandatory properties.\n\t * @see org.springframework.beans.factory.InitializingBean#afterPropertiesSet()\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void afterPropertiesSet() throws Exception {\n\t\tAssert.state(pageSize > 0, \"pageSize must be greater than zero\");\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tprotected @Nullable T doRead() throws Exception {\n\n\t\tthis.lock.lock();\n\t\ttry {\n\n\t\t\tif (results == null || current >= pageSize) {\n\n\t\t\t\tif (logger.isDebugEnabled()) {\n\t\t\t\t\tlogger.debug(\"Reading page \" + getPage());\n\t\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t\tdoReadPage();\n\t\t\t\tpage++;\n\t\t\t\tif (current >= pageSize) {\n\t\t\t\t\tcurrent = 0;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tint next = current++;\n\t\t\tif (next < results.size()) {\n\t\t\t\treturn results.get(next);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t}\n\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\tthis.lock.unlock();\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n\tabstract protected void doReadPage();\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void doOpen() throws Exception {\n\n\t\tAssert.state(!initialized, \"Cannot open an already opened ItemReader, call close first\");\n\t\tinitialized = true;\n\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void doClose() throws Exception {\n\n\t\tthis.lock.lock();\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tinitialized = false;\n\t\t\tcurrent = 0;\n\t\t\tpage = 0;\n\t\t\tresults = null;\n\t\t}\n\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\tthis.lock.unlock();\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void jumpToItem(int itemIndex) throws Exception {\n\n\t\tthis.lock.lock();\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tpage = itemIndex / pageSize;\n\t\t\tcurrent = itemIndex % pageSize;\n\t\t}\n\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\tthis.lock.unlock();\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (logger.isDebugEnabled()) {\n\t\t\tlogger.debug(\"Jumping to page \" + getPage() + \" and index \" + current);\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/database/ExtendedConnectionDataSourceProxy.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2002-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.database;\n\nimport java.io.PrintWriter;\nimport java.lang.reflect.InvocationHandler;\nimport java.lang.reflect.InvocationTargetException;\nimport java.lang.reflect.Method;\nimport java.lang.reflect.Proxy;\nimport java.sql.Connection;\nimport java.sql.SQLException;\nimport java.sql.SQLFeatureNotSupportedException;\nimport java.util.concurrent.locks.Lock;\nimport java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock;\nimport java.util.logging.Logger;\n\nimport javax.sql.DataSource;\n\nimport org.springframework.beans.factory.InitializingBean;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.datasource.ConnectionProxy;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.datasource.DataSourceUtils;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.datasource.SmartDataSource;\nimport org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\n\n/**\n * Implementation of {@link SmartDataSource} that is capable of keeping a single JDBC\n * Connection which is NOT closed after each use even if {@link Connection#close()} is\n * called.\n * <p>\n * The connection can be kept open over multiple transactions when used together with any\n * of Spring's {@link org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager}\n * implementations.\n *\n * <p>\n * Loosely based on the SingleConnectionDataSource implementation in Spring Core. Intended\n * to be used with the {@link JdbcCursorItemReader} to provide a connection that remains\n * open across transaction boundaries, It remains open for the life of the cursor, and can\n * be shared with the main transaction of the rest of the step processing.\n *\n * <p>\n * Once close suppression has been turned on for a connection, it will be returned for the\n * first {@link #getConnection()} call. Any subsequent calls to {@link #getConnection()}\n * will retrieve a new connection from the wrapped {@link DataSource} until the\n * {@link DataSourceUtils} queries whether the connection should be closed or not by\n * calling {@link #shouldClose(Connection)} for the close-suppressed {@link Connection}.\n * At that point the cycle starts over again, and the next {@link #getConnection()} call\n * will have the {@link Connection} that is being close-suppressed returned. This allows\n * the use of the close-suppressed {@link Connection} to be the main {@link Connection}\n * for an extended data access process. The close suppression is turned off by calling\n * {@link #stopCloseSuppression(Connection)}.\n *\n * <p>\n * This class is not multi-threading capable.\n *\n * <p>\n * The connection returned will be a close-suppressing proxy instead of the physical\n * {@link Connection}. Be aware that you will not be able to cast this to a native\n * <code>OracleConnection</code> or the like anymore; you'd be required to use\n * {@link Connection#unwrap(Class)}.\n *\n * @author Thomas Risberg\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Stefano Cordio\n * @see #getConnection()\n * @see Connection#close()\n * @see DataSourceUtils#releaseConnection\n * @see Connection#unwrap(Class)\n * @since 2.0\n */\npublic class ExtendedConnectionDataSourceProxy implements SmartDataSource, InitializingBean {\n\n\t/** Provided DataSource */\n\tprivate @Nullable DataSource dataSource;\n\n\t/** The connection to suppress close calls for */\n\tprivate @Nullable Connection closeSuppressedConnection;\n\n\t/** The connection to suppress close calls for */\n\tprivate boolean borrowedConnection = false;\n\n\t/** Synchronization monitor for the shared Connection */\n\tprivate final Lock connectionMonitor = new ReentrantLock();\n\n\t/**\n\t * No arg constructor for use when configured using JavaBean style.\n\t */\n\tpublic ExtendedConnectionDataSourceProxy() {\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Constructor that takes as a parameter with the {@link DataSource} to be wrapped.\n\t * @param dataSource DataSource to be used\n\t */\n\tpublic ExtendedConnectionDataSourceProxy(DataSource dataSource) {\n\t\tthis.dataSource = dataSource;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Setter for the {@link DataSource} that is to be wrapped.\n\t * @param dataSource the DataSource\n\t */\n\tpublic void setDataSource(DataSource dataSource) {\n\t\tthis.dataSource = dataSource;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @see SmartDataSource\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic boolean shouldClose(Connection connection) {\n\t\tif (borrowedConnection && isCloseSuppressionActive(connection)) {\n\t\t\tborrowedConnection = false;\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn !isCloseSuppressionActive(connection);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Return the status of close suppression being activated for a given\n\t * {@link Connection}\n\t * @param connection the {@link Connection} that the close suppression status is\n\t * requested for\n\t * @return true or false\n\t */\n\tpublic boolean isCloseSuppressionActive(Connection connection) {\n\t\treturn connection.equals(closeSuppressedConnection);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param connection the {@link Connection} that close suppression is requested for\n\t */\n\tpublic void startCloseSuppression(Connection connection) {\n\t\tthis.connectionMonitor.lock();\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tcloseSuppressedConnection = connection;\n\t\t\tif (TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive()) {\n\t\t\t\tborrowedConnection = true;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\tthis.connectionMonitor.unlock();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param connection the {@link Connection} that close suppression should be turned\n\t * off for\n\t */\n\tpublic void stopCloseSuppression(Connection connection) {\n\t\tthis.connectionMonitor.lock();\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tcloseSuppressedConnection = null;\n\t\t\tborrowedConnection = false;\n\t\t}\n\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\tthis.connectionMonitor.unlock();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic Connection getConnection() throws SQLException {\n\t\tthis.connectionMonitor.lock();\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\treturn initConnection(null, null);\n\t\t}\n\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\tthis.connectionMonitor.unlock();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic Connection getConnection(String username, String password) throws SQLException {\n\t\tthis.connectionMonitor.lock();\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\treturn initConnection(username, password);\n\t\t}\n\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\tthis.connectionMonitor.unlock();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\tprivate Connection initConnection(@Nullable String username, @Nullable String password) throws SQLException {\n\t\tif (closeSuppressedConnection != null) {\n\t\t\tif (!borrowedConnection) {\n\t\t\t\tborrowedConnection = true;\n\t\t\t\treturn closeSuppressedConnection;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tConnection target;\n\t\tif (username != null) {\n\t\t\ttarget = dataSource.getConnection(username, password);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\ttarget = dataSource.getConnection();\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn getCloseSuppressingConnectionProxy(target);\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tpublic PrintWriter getLogWriter() throws SQLException {\n\t\treturn dataSource.getLogWriter();\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tpublic int getLoginTimeout() throws SQLException {\n\t\treturn dataSource.getLoginTimeout();\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void setLogWriter(PrintWriter out) throws SQLException {\n\t\tdataSource.setLogWriter(out);\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void setLoginTimeout(int seconds) throws SQLException {\n\t\tdataSource.setLoginTimeout(seconds);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Wrap the given Connection with a proxy that delegates every method call to it but\n\t * suppresses close calls.\n\t * @param target the original Connection to wrap\n\t * @return the wrapped Connection\n\t */\n\tprotected Connection getCloseSuppressingConnectionProxy(Connection target) {\n\t\treturn (Connection) Proxy.newProxyInstance(ConnectionProxy.class.getClassLoader(),\n\t\t\t\tnew Class[] { ConnectionProxy.class }, new CloseSuppressingInvocationHandler(target, this));\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Invocation handler that suppresses close calls on JDBC Connections until the\n\t * associated instance of the ExtendedConnectionDataSourceProxy determines the\n\t * connection should actually be closed.\n\t */\n\tprivate static class CloseSuppressingInvocationHandler implements InvocationHandler {\n\n\t\tprivate final Connection target;\n\n\t\tprivate final ExtendedConnectionDataSourceProxy dataSource;\n\n\t\tpublic CloseSuppressingInvocationHandler(Connection target, ExtendedConnectionDataSourceProxy dataSource) {\n\t\t\tthis.dataSource = dataSource;\n\t\t\tthis.target = target;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t@Override\n\t\tpublic @Nullable Object invoke(Object proxy, Method method, Object[] args) throws Throwable {\n\t\t\t// Invocation on ConnectionProxy interface coming in...\n\n\t\t\tswitch (method.getName()) {\n\t\t\t\tcase \"equals\" -> {\n\t\t\t\t\t// Only consider equal when proxies are identical.\n\t\t\t\t\treturn (proxy == args[0] ? Boolean.TRUE : Boolean.FALSE);\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tcase \"hashCode\" -> {\n\t\t\t\t\t// Use hashCode of Connection proxy.\n\t\t\t\t\treturn System.identityHashCode(proxy);\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tcase \"close\" -> {\n\t\t\t\t\t// Handle close method: don't pass the call on if we are\n\t\t\t\t\t// suppressing close calls.\n\t\t\t\t\tif (dataSource.shouldClose((Connection) proxy)) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tthis.target.close();\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tcase \"getTargetConnection\" -> {\n\t\t\t\t\t// Handle getTargetConnection method: return underlying\n\t\t\t\t\t// Connection.\n\t\t\t\t\treturn this.target;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Invoke method on target Connection.\n\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\treturn method.invoke(this.target, args);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcatch (InvocationTargetException ex) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow ex.getTargetException();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Performs only a 'shallow' non-recursive check of self's and delegate's class to\n\t * retain Java 5 compatibility.\n\t */\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tpublic boolean isWrapperFor(Class<?> iface) {\n\t\treturn iface.isAssignableFrom(SmartDataSource.class) || iface.isAssignableFrom(dataSource.getClass());\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Returns either self or delegate (in this order) if one of them can be cast to\n\t * supplied parameter class. Does *not* support recursive unwrapping of the delegate\n\t * to retain Java 5 compatibility.\n\t */\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tpublic <T> T unwrap(Class<T> iface) throws SQLException {\n\t\tif (iface.isAssignableFrom(SmartDataSource.class)) {\n\t\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"unchecked\")\n\t\t\tT casted = (T) this;\n\t\t\treturn casted;\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (iface.isAssignableFrom(dataSource.getClass())) {\n\t\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"unchecked\")\n\t\t\tT casted = (T) dataSource;\n\t\t\treturn casted;\n\t\t}\n\t\tthrow new SQLException(\"Unsupported class \" + iface.getSimpleName());\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void afterPropertiesSet() throws Exception {\n\t\tAssert.state(dataSource != null, \"DataSource is required\");\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tpublic Logger getParentLogger() throws SQLFeatureNotSupportedException {\n\t\treturn dataSource.getParentLogger();\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/database/JpaPagingItemReader.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.database;\n\nimport java.util.HashMap;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Map;\nimport java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList;\n\nimport jakarta.persistence.EntityManager;\nimport jakarta.persistence.EntityManagerFactory;\nimport jakarta.persistence.EntityTransaction;\nimport jakarta.persistence.Query;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ExecutionContext;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.database.orm.JpaQueryProvider;\nimport org.springframework.dao.DataAccessResourceFailureException;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\nimport org.springframework.util.StringUtils;\n\n/**\n * <p>\n * {@link ItemReader} for reading database records built on top of JPA.\n * </p>\n *\n * <p>\n * It executes the JPQL {@link #setQueryString(String)} to retrieve requested data. The\n * query is executed using paged requests of a size specified in\n * {@link #setPageSize(int)}. Additional pages are requested when needed as\n * {@link #read()} method is called, returning an object corresponding to current\n * position.\n * </p>\n *\n * <p>\n * The performance of the paging depends on the JPA implementation and its use of database\n * specific features to limit the number of returned rows.\n * </p>\n *\n * <p>\n * Setting a fairly large page size and using a commit interval that matches the page size\n * should provide better performance.\n * </p>\n *\n * <p>\n * In order to reduce the memory usage for large results the persistence context is\n * flushed and cleared after each page is read. This causes any entities read to be\n * detached. If you make changes to the entities and want the changes persisted then you\n * must explicitly merge the entities.\n * </p>\n *\n * <p>\n * The reader must be configured with an {@link jakarta.persistence.EntityManagerFactory}.\n * All entity access is performed within a new transaction, independent of any existing\n * Spring managed transactions.\n * </p>\n *\n * <p>\n * The implementation is thread-safe in between calls to {@link #open(ExecutionContext)},\n * but remember to use <code>saveState=false</code> if used in a multi-threaded client (no\n * restart available).\n * </p>\n *\n * @author Thomas Risberg\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Will Schipp\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Jinwoo Bae\n * @author Jimmy Praet\n * @since 2.0\n */\npublic class JpaPagingItemReader<T> extends AbstractPagingItemReader<T> {\n\n\tprivate EntityManagerFactory entityManagerFactory;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable EntityManager entityManager;\n\n\tprivate final Map<String, Object> jpaPropertyMap = new HashMap<>();\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String queryString;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable JpaQueryProvider queryProvider;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Map<String, Object> parameterValues;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Map<String, Object> hintValues;\n\n\tprivate boolean transacted = true;// default value\n\n\t/**\n\t * Create a new {@link JpaPagingItemReader} instance.\n\t * @param entityManagerFactory the JPA entity manager factory.\n\t * @since 6.0\n\t */\n\tpublic JpaPagingItemReader(EntityManagerFactory entityManagerFactory) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(entityManagerFactory, \"EntityManagerFactory must not be null.\");\n\t\tthis.entityManagerFactory = entityManagerFactory;\n\t}\n\n\tpublic void setEntityManagerFactory(EntityManagerFactory entityManagerFactory) {\n\t\tthis.entityManagerFactory = entityManagerFactory;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The parameter values to be used for the query execution.\n\t * @param parameterValues the values keyed by the parameter named used in the query\n\t * string.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setParameterValues(Map<String, Object> parameterValues) {\n\t\tthis.parameterValues = parameterValues;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Set the query hint values for the JPA query. Query hints can be used to give\n\t * instructions to the JPA provider.\n\t * @param hintValues a map where each key is the name of the hint, and the\n\t * corresponding value is the hint's value.\n\t * @since 5.2\n\t */\n\tpublic void setHintValues(Map<String, Object> hintValues) {\n\t\tthis.hintValues = hintValues;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * By default (true) the EntityTransaction will be started and committed around the\n\t * read. Can be overridden (false) in cases where the JPA implementation doesn't\n\t * support a particular transaction. (e.g. Hibernate with a JTA transaction). NOTE:\n\t * may cause problems in guaranteeing the object consistency in the\n\t * EntityManagerFactory.\n\t * @param transacted indicator\n\t */\n\tpublic void setTransacted(boolean transacted) {\n\t\tthis.transacted = transacted;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void afterPropertiesSet() throws Exception {\n\t\tsuper.afterPropertiesSet();\n\n\t\tif (queryProvider == null) {\n\t\t\t// Assertion on EMF is wrong here since the EMF is called in doOpen regardless\n\t\t\t// of whether the queryProvider is set or not.\n\t\t\tAssert.state(StringUtils.hasLength(queryString), \"Query string is required when queryProvider is null\");\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param queryString JPQL query string\n\t */\n\tpublic void setQueryString(String queryString) {\n\t\tthis.queryString = queryString;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param queryProvider JPA query provider\n\t */\n\tpublic void setQueryProvider(JpaQueryProvider queryProvider) {\n\t\tthis.queryProvider = queryProvider;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void doOpen() throws Exception {\n\t\tsuper.doOpen();\n\n\t\tentityManager = entityManagerFactory.createEntityManager(jpaPropertyMap);\n\t\tif (entityManager == null) {\n\t\t\tthrow new DataAccessResourceFailureException(\"Unable to obtain an EntityManager\");\n\t\t}\n\t\t// set entityManager to queryProvider, so it participates\n\t\t// in JpaPagingItemReader's managed transaction\n\t\tif (queryProvider != null) {\n\t\t\tqueryProvider.setEntityManager(entityManager);\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings({ \"unchecked\", \"DataFlowIssue\" })\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void doReadPage() {\n\n\t\tEntityTransaction tx = null;\n\n\t\tif (transacted) {\n\t\t\ttx = entityManager.getTransaction();\n\t\t\ttx.begin();\n\n\t\t\tentityManager.flush();\n\t\t\tentityManager.clear();\n\t\t} // end if\n\n\t\tQuery query = createQuery().setFirstResult(getPage() * getPageSize()).setMaxResults(getPageSize());\n\n\t\tif (parameterValues != null) {\n\t\t\tfor (Map.Entry<String, Object> me : parameterValues.entrySet()) {\n\t\t\t\tquery.setParameter(me.getKey(), me.getValue());\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (this.hintValues != null) {\n\t\t\tthis.hintValues.forEach(query::setHint);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (results == null) {\n\t\t\tresults = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\tresults.clear();\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (!transacted) {\n\t\t\tList<T> queryResult = query.getResultList();\n\t\t\tfor (T entity : queryResult) {\n\t\t\t\tentityManager.detach(entity);\n\t\t\t\tresults.add(entity);\n\t\t\t} // end if\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\tresults.addAll(query.getResultList());\n\t\t\ttx.commit();\n\t\t} // end if\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Create a query using an appropriate query provider (entityManager OR\n\t * queryProvider).\n\t */\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\tprivate Query createQuery() {\n\t\tif (queryProvider == null) {\n\t\t\treturn entityManager.createQuery(queryString);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\treturn queryProvider.createQuery();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void doClose() throws Exception {\n\t\tentityManager.close();\n\t\tsuper.doClose();\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/database/orm/JpaNamedQueryProvider.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2020-2022 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.database.orm;\n\nimport jakarta.persistence.Query;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\nimport org.springframework.util.StringUtils;\n\n/**\n * This query provider creates JPA named {@link Query}s.\n *\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Parikshit Dutta\n * @author Stefano Cordio\n * @since 4.3\n * @param <E> entity returned by executing the query\n */\npublic class JpaNamedQueryProvider<E> extends AbstractJpaQueryProvider {\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Class<E> entityClass;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String namedQuery;\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tpublic Query createQuery() {\n\t\treturn getEntityManager().createNamedQuery(this.namedQuery, this.entityClass);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param namedQuery name of a jpa named query\n\t */\n\tpublic void setNamedQuery(String namedQuery) {\n\t\tthis.namedQuery = namedQuery;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param entityClazz name of a jpa entity class\n\t */\n\tpublic void setEntityClass(Class<E> entityClazz) {\n\t\tthis.entityClass = entityClazz;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void afterPropertiesSet() throws Exception {\n\t\tAssert.state(StringUtils.hasText(this.namedQuery), \"Named query cannot be empty\");\n\t\tAssert.state(this.entityClass != null, \"Entity class cannot be NULL\");\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/database/support/AbstractSqlPagingQueryProvider.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2024 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.database.support;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.LinkedHashMap;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Map;\nimport javax.sql.DataSource;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.database.JdbcParameterUtils;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.database.Order;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.database.PagingQueryProvider;\nimport org.springframework.dao.InvalidDataAccessApiUsageException;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\nimport org.springframework.util.StringUtils;\n\n/**\n * Abstract SQL Paging Query Provider to serve as a base class for all provided SQL paging\n * query providers.\n * <p>\n * Any implementation must provide a way to specify the select clause, from clause and\n * optionally a where clause. In addition a way to specify a single column sort key must\n * also be provided. This sort key will be used to provide the paging functionality. It is\n * recommended that there should be an index for the sort key to provide better\n * performance.\n * <p>\n * Provides properties and preparation for the mandatory \"selectClause\" and \"fromClause\"\n * as well as for the optional \"whereClause\". Also provides property for the mandatory\n * \"sortKeys\". <b>Note:</b> The columns that make up the sort key must be a true key and\n * not just a column to order by. It is important to have a unique key constraint on the\n * sort key to guarantee that no data is lost between executions.\n *\n * @author Thomas Risberg\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Michael Minella\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Benjamin Hetz\n * @author Stefano Cordio\n * @since 2.0\n */\npublic abstract class AbstractSqlPagingQueryProvider implements PagingQueryProvider {\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String selectClause;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String fromClause;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String whereClause;\n\n\tprivate Map<String, Order> sortKeys = new LinkedHashMap<>();\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String groupClause;\n\n\tprivate int parameterCount;\n\n\tprivate boolean usingNamedParameters;\n\n\t/**\n\t * The setter for the group by clause\n\t * @param groupClause SQL GROUP BY clause part of the SQL query string\n\t */\n\tpublic void setGroupClause(@Nullable String groupClause) {\n\t\tthis.groupClause = StringUtils.hasText(groupClause) ? removeKeyWord(\"group by\", groupClause) : null;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The getter for the group by clause\n\t * @return SQL GROUP BY clause part of the SQL query string\n\t */\n\tpublic @Nullable String getGroupClause() {\n\t\treturn this.groupClause;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param selectClause SELECT clause part of SQL query string\n\t */\n\tpublic void setSelectClause(String selectClause) {\n\t\tthis.selectClause = removeKeyWord(\"select\", selectClause);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return SQL SELECT clause part of SQL query string\n\t */\n\tprotected @Nullable String getSelectClause() {\n\t\treturn selectClause;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param fromClause FROM clause part of SQL query string\n\t */\n\tpublic void setFromClause(String fromClause) {\n\t\tthis.fromClause = removeKeyWord(\"from\", fromClause);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return SQL FROM clause part of SQL query string\n\t */\n\tprotected @Nullable String getFromClause() {\n\t\treturn fromClause;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param whereClause WHERE clause part of SQL query string\n\t */\n\tpublic void setWhereClause(@Nullable String whereClause) {\n\t\tif (StringUtils.hasText(whereClause)) {\n\t\t\tthis.whereClause = removeKeyWord(\"where\", whereClause);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\tthis.whereClause = null;\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return SQL WHERE clause part of SQL query string\n\t */\n\tprotected @Nullable String getWhereClause() {\n\t\treturn whereClause;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param sortKeys key to use to sort and limit page content\n\t */\n\tpublic void setSortKeys(Map<String, Order> sortKeys) {\n\t\tthis.sortKeys = sortKeys;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * A Map&lt;String, Boolean&gt; of sort columns as the key and boolean for\n\t * ascending/descending (ascending = true).\n\t * @return keys to use to sort and limit page content\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic Map<String, Order> getSortKeys() {\n\t\treturn sortKeys;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic int getParameterCount() {\n\t\treturn parameterCount;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic boolean isUsingNamedParameters() {\n\t\treturn usingNamedParameters;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The sort key placeholder will vary depending on whether named parameters or\n\t * traditional placeholders are used in query strings.\n\t * @return placeholder for sortKey.\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic String getSortKeyPlaceHolder(String keyName) {\n\t\treturn usingNamedParameters ? \":_\" + keyName : \"?\";\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Check mandatory properties.\n\t * @see org.springframework.beans.factory.InitializingBean#afterPropertiesSet()\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void init(DataSource dataSource) throws Exception {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(dataSource, \"A DataSource is required\");\n\t\tAssert.hasLength(selectClause, \"selectClause must be specified\");\n\t\tAssert.hasLength(fromClause, \"fromClause must be specified\");\n\t\tAssert.notEmpty(sortKeys, \"sortKey must be specified\");\n\t\tStringBuilder sql = new StringBuilder(64);\n\t\tsql.append(\"SELECT \").append(selectClause);\n\t\tsql.append(\" FROM \").append(fromClause);\n\t\tif (whereClause != null) {\n\t\t\tsql.append(\" WHERE \").append(whereClause);\n\t\t}\n\t\tif (groupClause != null) {\n\t\t\tsql.append(\" GROUP BY \").append(groupClause);\n\t\t}\n\t\tList<String> namedParameters = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\tparameterCount = JdbcParameterUtils.countParameterPlaceholders(sql.toString(), namedParameters);\n\t\tif (!namedParameters.isEmpty()) {\n\t\t\tif (parameterCount != namedParameters.size()) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new InvalidDataAccessApiUsageException(\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\"You can't use both named parameters and classic \\\"?\\\" placeholders: \" + sql);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tusingNamedParameters = true;\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Method generating the query string to be used for retrieving the first page. This\n\t * method must be implemented in subclasses.\n\t * @param pageSize number of rows to read per page\n\t * @return query string\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic abstract String generateFirstPageQuery(int pageSize);\n\n\t/**\n\t * Method generating the query string to be used for retrieving the pages following\n\t * the first page. This method must be implemented in subclasses.\n\t * @param pageSize number of rows to read per page\n\t * @return query string\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic abstract String generateRemainingPagesQuery(int pageSize);\n\n\tprivate String removeKeyWord(String keyWord, String clause) {\n\t\tString temp = clause.trim();\n\t\tint length = keyWord.length();\n\t\tif (temp.toLowerCase().startsWith(keyWord) && Character.isWhitespace(temp.charAt(length))\n\t\t\t\t&& temp.length() > length + 1) {\n\t\t\treturn temp.substring(length + 1);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\treturn temp;\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return sortKey key to use to sort and limit page content (without alias)\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic Map<String, Order> getSortKeysWithoutAliases() {\n\t\tMap<String, Order> sortKeysWithoutAliases = new LinkedHashMap<>();\n\n\t\tfor (Map.Entry<String, Order> sortKeyEntry : sortKeys.entrySet()) {\n\t\t\tString key = sortKeyEntry.getKey();\n\t\t\tint separator = key.indexOf('.');\n\t\t\tif (separator > 0) {\n\t\t\t\tint columnIndex = separator + 1;\n\t\t\t\tif (columnIndex < key.length()) {\n\t\t\t\t\tsortKeysWithoutAliases.put(key.substring(columnIndex), sortKeyEntry.getValue());\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\tsortKeysWithoutAliases.put(sortKeyEntry.getKey(), sortKeyEntry.getValue());\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn sortKeysWithoutAliases;\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/database/support/DefaultDataFieldMaxValueIncrementerFactory.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.database.support;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport javax.sql.DataSource;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.support.incrementer.Db2LuwMaxValueIncrementer;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.support.incrementer.Db2MainframeMaxValueIncrementer;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.support.incrementer.DataFieldMaxValueIncrementer;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.support.incrementer.DerbyMaxValueIncrementer;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.support.incrementer.H2SequenceMaxValueIncrementer;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.support.incrementer.HanaSequenceMaxValueIncrementer;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.support.incrementer.HsqlMaxValueIncrementer;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.support.incrementer.MySQLMaxValueIncrementer;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.support.incrementer.OracleSequenceMaxValueIncrementer;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.support.incrementer.PostgresSequenceMaxValueIncrementer;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.support.incrementer.SqlServerSequenceMaxValueIncrementer;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.support.incrementer.SybaseMaxValueIncrementer;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.support.incrementer.MariaDBSequenceMaxValueIncrementer;\n\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.DB2;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.DB2AS400;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.DB2ZOS;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.DERBY;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.H2;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.HANA;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.HSQL;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.MARIADB;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.MYSQL;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.ORACLE;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.POSTGRES;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.SQLITE;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.SQLSERVER;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.SYBASE;\n\n/**\n * Default implementation of the {@link DataFieldMaxValueIncrementerFactory} interface.\n * Valid database types are given by the {@link DatabaseType} enum.\n * <p>\n * Note: For MySql databases, the\n * {@link MySQLMaxValueIncrementer#setUseNewConnection(boolean)} will be set to true.\n *\n * @author Lucas Ward\n * @author Michael Minella\n * @author Drummond Dawson\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @see DatabaseType\n */\npublic class DefaultDataFieldMaxValueIncrementerFactory implements DataFieldMaxValueIncrementerFactory {\n\n\tprivate final DataSource dataSource;\n\n\tprivate String incrementerColumnName = \"ID\";\n\n\t/**\n\t * Public setter for the column name (defaults to \"ID\") in the incrementer. Only used\n\t * by some platforms (Derby, HSQL, MySQL, SQL Server and Sybase), and should be fine\n\t * for use with Spring Batch meta data as long as the default batch schema hasn't been\n\t * changed.\n\t * @param incrementerColumnName the primary key column name to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setIncrementerColumnName(String incrementerColumnName) {\n\t\tthis.incrementerColumnName = incrementerColumnName;\n\t}\n\n\tpublic DefaultDataFieldMaxValueIncrementerFactory(DataSource dataSource) {\n\t\tthis.dataSource = dataSource;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic DataFieldMaxValueIncrementer getIncrementer(String incrementerType, String incrementerName) {\n\t\tDatabaseType databaseType = DatabaseType.valueOf(incrementerType.toUpperCase());\n\n\t\tif (databaseType == DB2 || databaseType == DB2AS400) {\n\t\t\treturn new Db2LuwMaxValueIncrementer(dataSource, incrementerName);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (databaseType == DB2ZOS) {\n\t\t\treturn new Db2MainframeMaxValueIncrementer(dataSource, incrementerName);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (databaseType == DERBY) {\n\t\t\treturn new DerbyMaxValueIncrementer(dataSource, incrementerName, incrementerColumnName);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (databaseType == HSQL) {\n\t\t\treturn new HsqlMaxValueIncrementer(dataSource, incrementerName, incrementerColumnName);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (databaseType == H2) {\n\t\t\treturn new H2SequenceMaxValueIncrementer(dataSource, incrementerName);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (databaseType == HANA) {\n\t\t\treturn new HanaSequenceMaxValueIncrementer(dataSource, incrementerName);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (databaseType == MYSQL) {\n\t\t\tMySQLMaxValueIncrementer mySQLMaxValueIncrementer = new MySQLMaxValueIncrementer(dataSource,\n\t\t\t\t\tincrementerName, incrementerColumnName);\n\t\t\tmySQLMaxValueIncrementer.setUseNewConnection(true);\n\t\t\treturn mySQLMaxValueIncrementer;\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (databaseType == MARIADB) {\n\t\t\treturn new MariaDBSequenceMaxValueIncrementer(dataSource, incrementerName);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (databaseType == ORACLE) {\n\t\t\treturn new OracleSequenceMaxValueIncrementer(dataSource, incrementerName);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (databaseType == POSTGRES) {\n\t\t\treturn new PostgresSequenceMaxValueIncrementer(dataSource, incrementerName);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (databaseType == SQLITE) {\n\t\t\treturn new SqliteMaxValueIncrementer(dataSource, incrementerName, incrementerColumnName);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (databaseType == SQLSERVER) {\n\t\t\treturn new SqlServerSequenceMaxValueIncrementer(dataSource, incrementerName);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (databaseType == SYBASE) {\n\t\t\treturn new SybaseMaxValueIncrementer(dataSource, incrementerName, incrementerColumnName);\n\t\t}\n\t\tthrow new IllegalArgumentException(\"databaseType argument was not on the approved list\");\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic boolean isSupportedIncrementerType(String incrementerType) {\n\t\tfor (DatabaseType type : DatabaseType.values()) {\n\t\t\tif (type.name().equalsIgnoreCase(incrementerType)) {\n\t\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn false;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic String[] getSupportedIncrementerTypes() {\n\n\t\tList<String> types = new ArrayList<>();\n\n\t\tfor (DatabaseType type : DatabaseType.values()) {\n\t\t\ttypes.add(type.name());\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn types.toArray(new String[types.size()]);\n\t}\n\n}"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/database/support/SqlPagingQueryProviderFactoryBean.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.database.support;\n\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.DB2;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.DB2VSE;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.DB2ZOS;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.DB2AS400;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.DERBY;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.H2;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.HANA;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.HSQL;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.MARIADB;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.MYSQL;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.ORACLE;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.POSTGRES;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.SQLITE;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.SQLSERVER;\nimport static org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType.SYBASE;\n\nimport java.util.HashMap;\nimport java.util.LinkedHashMap;\nimport java.util.Map;\n\nimport javax.sql.DataSource;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.database.Order;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.database.PagingQueryProvider;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DatabaseType;\nimport org.springframework.beans.factory.FactoryBean;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.support.MetaDataAccessException;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\nimport org.springframework.util.StringUtils;\n\n/**\n * Factory bean for {@link PagingQueryProvider} interface. The database type will be\n * determined from the data source if not provided explicitly. Valid types are given by\n * the {@link DatabaseType} enum.\n *\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Michael Minella\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n */\npublic class SqlPagingQueryProviderFactoryBean implements FactoryBean<PagingQueryProvider> {\n\n\tprivate @Nullable DataSource dataSource;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String databaseType;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String fromClause;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String whereClause;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String selectClause;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String groupClause;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Map<String, Order> sortKeys;\n\n\tprivate final Map<DatabaseType, AbstractSqlPagingQueryProvider> providers = new HashMap<>();\n\n\t{\n\t\tproviders.put(DB2, new Db2PagingQueryProvider());\n\t\tproviders.put(DB2VSE, new Db2PagingQueryProvider());\n\t\tproviders.put(DB2ZOS, new Db2PagingQueryProvider());\n\t\tproviders.put(DB2AS400, new Db2PagingQueryProvider());\n\t\tproviders.put(DERBY, new DerbyPagingQueryProvider());\n\t\tproviders.put(HSQL, new HsqlPagingQueryProvider());\n\t\tproviders.put(H2, new H2PagingQueryProvider());\n\t\tproviders.put(HANA, new HanaPagingQueryProvider());\n\t\tproviders.put(MYSQL, new MySqlPagingQueryProvider());\n\t\tproviders.put(MARIADB, new MariaDBPagingQueryProvider());\n\t\tproviders.put(ORACLE, new OraclePagingQueryProvider());\n\t\tproviders.put(POSTGRES, new PostgresPagingQueryProvider());\n\t\tproviders.put(SQLITE, new SqlitePagingQueryProvider());\n\t\tproviders.put(SQLSERVER, new SqlServerPagingQueryProvider());\n\t\tproviders.put(SYBASE, new SybasePagingQueryProvider());\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param groupClause SQL GROUP BY clause part of the SQL query string\n\t */\n\tpublic void setGroupClause(String groupClause) {\n\t\tthis.groupClause = groupClause;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param databaseType the databaseType to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setDatabaseType(String databaseType) {\n\t\tthis.databaseType = databaseType;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param dataSource the dataSource to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setDataSource(DataSource dataSource) {\n\t\tthis.dataSource = dataSource;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param fromClause the fromClause to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setFromClause(String fromClause) {\n\t\tthis.fromClause = fromClause;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param whereClause the whereClause to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setWhereClause(String whereClause) {\n\t\tthis.whereClause = whereClause;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param selectClause the selectClause to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setSelectClause(String selectClause) {\n\t\tthis.selectClause = selectClause;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param sortKeys the sortKeys to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setSortKeys(Map<String, Order> sortKeys) {\n\t\tthis.sortKeys = sortKeys;\n\t}\n\n\tpublic void setSortKey(String key) {\n\t\tAssert.doesNotContain(key, \",\", \"String setter is valid for a single ASC key only\");\n\t\tthis.sortKeys = Map.of(key, Order.ASCENDING);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Get a {@link PagingQueryProvider} instance using the provided properties and\n\t * appropriate for the given database type.\n\t *\n\t * @see FactoryBean#getObject()\n\t */\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tpublic PagingQueryProvider getObject() throws Exception {\n\n\t\tDatabaseType type;\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\ttype = databaseType != null ? DatabaseType.valueOf(databaseType.toUpperCase())\n\t\t\t\t\t: DatabaseType.fromMetaData(dataSource);\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (MetaDataAccessException e) {\n\t\t\tthrow new IllegalArgumentException(\n\t\t\t\t\t\"Could not inspect meta data for database type.  You have to supply it explicitly.\", e);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tAbstractSqlPagingQueryProvider provider = providers.get(type);\n\t\tAssert.state(provider != null, \"Should not happen: missing PagingQueryProvider for DatabaseType=\" + type);\n\n\t\tprovider.setFromClause(fromClause);\n\t\tprovider.setWhereClause(whereClause);\n\t\tprovider.setSortKeys(sortKeys);\n\t\tif (StringUtils.hasText(selectClause)) {\n\t\t\tprovider.setSelectClause(selectClause);\n\t\t}\n\t\tif (StringUtils.hasText(groupClause)) {\n\t\t\tprovider.setGroupClause(groupClause);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tprovider.init(dataSource);\n\n\t\treturn provider;\n\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Always returns {@link PagingQueryProvider}.\n\t *\n\t * @see FactoryBean#getObjectType()\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic Class<PagingQueryProvider> getObjectType() {\n\t\treturn PagingQueryProvider.class;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Always returns true.\n\t * @see FactoryBean#isSingleton()\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic boolean isSingleton() {\n\t\treturn true;\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/file/DefaultBufferedReaderFactory.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2024 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file;\n\nimport java.io.BufferedReader;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.io.InputStreamReader;\n\nimport org.springframework.core.io.Resource;\n\n/**\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @since 2.1\n */\npublic class DefaultBufferedReaderFactory implements BufferedReaderFactory {\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic BufferedReader create(Resource resource, String encoding) throws IOException {\n\t\treturn new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(resource.getInputStream(), encoding));\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/file/FlatFileItemReader.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file;\n\nimport java.io.BufferedReader;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\n\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.Log;\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.LogFactory;\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ExecutionContext;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ReaderNotOpenException;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file.separator.RecordSeparatorPolicy;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file.separator.SimpleRecordSeparatorPolicy;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.support.AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.core.io.Resource;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\nimport org.springframework.util.StringUtils;\n\n/**\n * Restartable {@link ItemReader} that reads lines from input\n * {@link #setResource(Resource)}. Line is defined by the\n * {@link #setRecordSeparatorPolicy(RecordSeparatorPolicy)} and mapped to item using\n * {@link #setLineMapper(LineMapper)}. If an exception is thrown during line mapping it is\n * rethrown as {@link FlatFileParseException} adding information about the problematic\n * line and its line number.\n *\n * <p>\n * This reader is <b>not</b> thread-safe.\n * </p>\n *\n * @author Robert Kasanicky\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Stefano Cordio\n * @author Jimmy Praet\n */\n// FIXME the design of creating a flat file reader with an optional resource (to support\n// the multi-resource case) is broken.\n// FIXME The multi-resource reader should create the delegate with the current resource\npublic class FlatFileItemReader<T> extends AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader<T>\n\t\timplements ResourceAwareItemReaderItemStream<T> {\n\n\tprivate static final Log logger = LogFactory.getLog(FlatFileItemReader.class);\n\n\tpublic static final String DEFAULT_CHARSET = StandardCharsets.UTF_8.name();\n\n\tpublic static final String[] DEFAULT_COMMENT_PREFIXES = new String[] { \"#\" };\n\n\tprivate RecordSeparatorPolicy recordSeparatorPolicy = new SimpleRecordSeparatorPolicy();\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Resource resource;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable BufferedReader reader;\n\n\tprivate int lineCount = 0;\n\n\tprotected String[] comments = DEFAULT_COMMENT_PREFIXES;\n\n\tprivate boolean noInput = false;\n\n\tprivate String encoding = DEFAULT_CHARSET;\n\n\tprivate LineMapper<T> lineMapper;\n\n\tprivate int linesToSkip = 0;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable LineCallbackHandler skippedLinesCallback;\n\n\tprivate boolean strict = true;\n\n\tprivate BufferedReaderFactory bufferedReaderFactory = new DefaultBufferedReaderFactory();\n\n\t/**\n\t * Create a new {@link FlatFileItemReader} with a {@link LineMapper}.\n\t * @param lineMapper to use to map lines to items\n\t * @since 6.0\n\t */\n\tpublic FlatFileItemReader(LineMapper<T> lineMapper) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(lineMapper, \"A LineMapper is required\");\n\t\tthis.lineMapper = lineMapper;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Create a new {@link FlatFileItemReader} with a {@link Resource} and a\n\t * {@link LineMapper}.\n\t * @param resource the input resource\n\t * @param lineMapper to use to map lines to items\n\t * @since 6.0\n\t */\n\tpublic FlatFileItemReader(Resource resource, LineMapper<T> lineMapper) {\n\t\tthis(lineMapper);\n\t\tAssert.notNull(resource, \"The resource must not be null\");\n\t\tthis.resource = resource;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * In strict mode the reader will throw an exception on\n\t * {@link #open(ExecutionContext)} if the input resource does not exist.\n\t * @param strict <code>true</code> by default\n\t */\n\tpublic void setStrict(boolean strict) {\n\t\tthis.strict = strict;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param skippedLinesCallback will be called for each one of the initial skipped\n\t * lines before any items are read.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setSkippedLinesCallback(LineCallbackHandler skippedLinesCallback) {\n\t\tthis.skippedLinesCallback = skippedLinesCallback;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Public setter for the number of lines to skip at the start of a file. Can be used\n\t * if the file contains a header without useful (column name) information, and without\n\t * a comment delimiter at the beginning of the lines.\n\t * @param linesToSkip the number of lines to skip\n\t */\n\tpublic void setLinesToSkip(int linesToSkip) {\n\t\tthis.linesToSkip = linesToSkip;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Setter for line mapper. This property is required to be set.\n\t * @param lineMapper maps line to item\n\t */\n\tpublic void setLineMapper(LineMapper<T> lineMapper) {\n\t\tthis.lineMapper = lineMapper;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Setter for the encoding for this input source. Default value is\n\t * {@link #DEFAULT_CHARSET}.\n\t * @param encoding a properties object which possibly contains the encoding for this\n\t * input file;\n\t */\n\tpublic void setEncoding(String encoding) {\n\t\tthis.encoding = encoding;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Factory for the {@link BufferedReader} that will be used to extract lines from the\n\t * file. The default is fine for plain text files, but this is a useful strategy for\n\t * binary files where the standard BufferedReader from java.io is limiting.\n\t * @param bufferedReaderFactory the bufferedReaderFactory to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setBufferedReaderFactory(BufferedReaderFactory bufferedReaderFactory) {\n\t\tthis.bufferedReaderFactory = bufferedReaderFactory;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Setter for comment prefixes. Can be used to ignore header lines as well by using\n\t * e.g. the first couple of column names as a prefix. Defaults to\n\t * {@link #DEFAULT_COMMENT_PREFIXES}.\n\t * @param comments an array of comment line prefixes.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setComments(String[] comments) {\n\t\tthis.comments = new String[comments.length];\n\t\tSystem.arraycopy(comments, 0, this.comments, 0, comments.length);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Public setter for the input resource.\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void setResource(@Nullable Resource resource) {\n\t\tthis.resource = resource;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Public setter for the recordSeparatorPolicy. Used to determine where the line\n\t * endings are and do things like continue over a line ending if inside a quoted\n\t * string. Defaults to {@link SimpleRecordSeparatorPolicy}.\n\t * @param recordSeparatorPolicy the recordSeparatorPolicy to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setRecordSeparatorPolicy(RecordSeparatorPolicy recordSeparatorPolicy) {\n\t\tthis.recordSeparatorPolicy = recordSeparatorPolicy;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return string corresponding to logical record according to\n\t * {@link #setRecordSeparatorPolicy(RecordSeparatorPolicy)} (might span multiple lines\n\t * in file).\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tprotected @Nullable T doRead() throws Exception {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(resource, \"Input resource must be set\");\n\n\t\tif (noInput) {\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tString line = readLine();\n\n\t\tif (line == null) {\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\treturn lineMapper.mapLine(line, lineCount);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcatch (Exception ex) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new FlatFileParseException(\"Parsing error at line: \" + lineCount + \" in resource=[\"\n\t\t\t\t\t\t+ resource.getDescription() + \"], input=[\" + line + \"]\", ex, line, lineCount);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return next line (skip comments).getCurrentResource\n\t */\n\tprivate @Nullable String readLine() {\n\n\t\tif (reader == null) {\n\t\t\tthrow new ReaderNotOpenException(\"Reader must be open before it can be read.\");\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tString line = null;\n\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tdo {\n\t\t\t\tline = reader.readLine();\n\t\t\t\tif (line == null) {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tlineCount++;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\twhile (isComment(line));\n\n\t\t\tline = applyRecordSeparatorPolicy(line);\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (IOException e) {\n\t\t\t// Prevent IOException from recurring indefinitely\n\t\t\t// if client keeps catching and re-calling\n\t\t\tnoInput = true;\n\t\t\tif (line == null) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new NonTransientFlatFileException(\"Unable to read from resource: [\" + resource + \"]\", e);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new NonTransientFlatFileException(\"Unable to read from resource: [\" + resource + \"]\", e, line,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tlineCount);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn line;\n\t}\n\n\tprotected boolean isComment(String line) {\n\t\tfor (String prefix : comments) {\n\t\t\tif (line.startsWith(prefix)) {\n\t\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn false;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void doClose() throws Exception {\n\t\tlineCount = 0;\n\t\tif (reader != null) {\n\t\t\treader.close();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void doOpen() throws Exception {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(resource, \"Input resource must be set\");\n\n\t\tnoInput = true;\n\t\tif (!resource.exists()) {\n\t\t\tif (strict) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new IllegalStateException(\"Input resource must exist (reader is in 'strict' mode): \" + resource);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tlogger.warn(\"Input resource does not exist \" + resource.getDescription());\n\t\t\treturn;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (!resource.isReadable()) {\n\t\t\tif (strict) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new IllegalStateException(\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\"Input resource must be readable (reader is in 'strict' mode): \" + resource);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tlogger.warn(\"Input resource is not readable \" + resource.getDescription());\n\t\t\treturn;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treader = bufferedReaderFactory.create(resource, encoding);\n\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < linesToSkip; i++) {\n\t\t\tString line = readLine();\n\t\t\tif (skippedLinesCallback != null && line != null) {\n\t\t\t\tskippedLinesCallback.handleLine(line);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tnoInput = false;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void jumpToItem(int itemIndex) throws Exception {\n\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < itemIndex; i++) {\n\t\t\treadLine();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tprivate String applyRecordSeparatorPolicy(String line) throws IOException {\n\t\tif (reader == null) {\n\t\t\tthrow new ReaderNotOpenException(\"Reader must be open before it can be read.\");\n\t\t}\n\t\tString record = line;\n\t\twhile (!recordSeparatorPolicy.isEndOfRecord(record)) {\n\t\t\tline = this.reader.readLine();\n\t\t\tif (line == null) {\n\t\t\t\tif (StringUtils.hasText(record)) {\n\t\t\t\t\t// A record was partially complete since it hasn't ended but\n\t\t\t\t\t// the line is null\n\t\t\t\t\tthrow new FlatFileParseException(\"Unexpected end of file before record complete\", record,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tlineCount);\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\t\t// Record has no text but it might still be post processed\n\t\t\t\t\t// to something (skipping preProcess since that was already\n\t\t\t\t\t// done)\n\t\t\t\t\tbreak;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\tlineCount++;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\trecord = recordSeparatorPolicy.preProcess(record) + line;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn recordSeparatorPolicy.postProcess(record);\n\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },
