      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/file/MultiResourceItemReader.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file;\n\nimport java.util.Arrays;\nimport java.util.Comparator;\n\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.Log;\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.LogFactory;\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ExecutionContext;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemStream;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemStreamException;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ResourceAware;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.support.AbstractItemStreamItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.core.io.Resource;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\n\n/**\n * Reads items from multiple resources sequentially - resource list is given by\n * {@link #setResources(Resource[])}, the actual reading is delegated to\n * {@link #setDelegate(ResourceAwareItemReaderItemStream)}.\n * <p>\n * Input resources are ordered using {@link #setComparator(Comparator)} to make sure\n * resource ordering is preserved between job runs in restart scenario.\n *\n * @author Robert Kasanicky\n * @author Lucas Ward\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Jimmy Praet\n */\npublic class MultiResourceItemReader<T> extends AbstractItemStreamItemReader<T> {\n\n\tprivate static final Log logger = LogFactory.getLog(MultiResourceItemReader.class);\n\n\tprivate static final String RESOURCE_KEY = \"resourceIndex\";\n\n\tprivate ResourceAwareItemReaderItemStream<? extends T> delegate;\n\n\tprivate Resource @Nullable [] resources;\n\n\tprivate boolean saveState = true;\n\n\tprivate int currentResource = -1;\n\n\t// signals there are no resources to read -> just return null on first read\n\tprivate boolean noInput;\n\n\tprivate boolean strict = false;\n\n\t/**\n\t * In strict mode the reader will throw an exception on\n\t * {@link #open(ExecutionContext)} if there are no resources to read.\n\t * @param strict false by default\n\t */\n\tpublic void setStrict(boolean strict) {\n\t\tthis.strict = strict;\n\t}\n\n\tprivate Comparator<Resource> comparator = new Comparator<>() {\n\n\t\t/**\n\t\t * Compares resource filenames.\n\t\t */\n\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t\t@Override\n\t\tpublic int compare(Resource r1, Resource r2) {\n\t\t\treturn r1.getFilename().compareTo(r2.getFilename());\n\t\t}\n\n\t};\n\n\t/**\n\t * Create a new {@link MultiResourceItemReader} instance with the given delegate.\n\t * @param delegate the delegate {@link ResourceAwareItemReaderItemStream} to use\n\t * @since 6.0\n\t */\n\tpublic MultiResourceItemReader(ResourceAwareItemReaderItemStream<? extends T> delegate) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(delegate, \"The delegate reader must not be null\");\n\t\tthis.delegate = delegate;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Reads the next item, jumping to next resource if necessary.\n\t */\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tpublic @Nullable T read() throws Exception {\n\n\t\tif (noInput) {\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// If there is no resource, then this is the first item, set the current\n\t\t// resource to 0 and open the first delegate.\n\t\tif (currentResource == -1) {\n\t\t\tcurrentResource = 0;\n\t\t\tdelegate.setResource(resources[currentResource]);\n\t\t\tdelegate.open(new ExecutionContext());\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn readNextItem();\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Use the delegate to read the next item, jump to next resource if current one is\n\t * exhausted. Items are appended to the buffer.\n\t * @return next item from input\n\t */\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\tprivate @Nullable T readNextItem() throws Exception {\n\n\t\tT item = readFromDelegate();\n\n\t\twhile (item == null) {\n\n\t\t\tcurrentResource++;\n\n\t\t\tif (currentResource >= resources.length) {\n\t\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tdelegate.close();\n\t\t\tdelegate.setResource(resources[currentResource]);\n\t\t\tdelegate.open(new ExecutionContext());\n\n\t\t\titem = readFromDelegate();\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn item;\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\tprivate @Nullable T readFromDelegate() throws Exception {\n\t\tT item = delegate.read();\n\t\tif (item instanceof ResourceAware resourceAware) {\n\t\t\tresourceAware.setResource(resources[currentResource]);\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn item;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Close the {@link #setDelegate(ResourceAwareItemReaderItemStream)} reader and reset\n\t * instance variable values.\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void close() throws ItemStreamException {\n\t\tsuper.close();\n\n\t\tif (!this.noInput) {\n\t\t\tdelegate.close();\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tnoInput = false;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Figure out which resource to start with in case of restart, open the delegate and\n\t * restore delegate's position in the resource.\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void open(ExecutionContext executionContext) throws ItemStreamException {\n\t\tsuper.open(executionContext);\n\t\tAssert.notNull(resources, \"Resources must be set\");\n\n\t\tnoInput = false;\n\t\tif (resources.length == 0) {\n\t\t\tif (strict) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new IllegalStateException(\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\"No resources to read. Set strict=false if this is not an error condition.\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\tlogger.warn(\"No resources to read. Set strict=true if this should be an error condition.\");\n\t\t\t\tnoInput = true;\n\t\t\t\treturn;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tArrays.sort(resources, comparator);\n\n\t\tif (executionContext.containsKey(getExecutionContextKey(RESOURCE_KEY))) {\n\t\t\tcurrentResource = executionContext.getInt(getExecutionContextKey(RESOURCE_KEY));\n\n\t\t\t// context could have been saved before reading anything\n\t\t\tif (currentResource == -1) {\n\t\t\t\tcurrentResource = 0;\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tdelegate.setResource(resources[currentResource]);\n\t\t\tdelegate.open(executionContext);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\tcurrentResource = -1;\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Store the current resource index and position in the resource.\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void update(ExecutionContext executionContext) throws ItemStreamException {\n\t\tsuper.update(executionContext);\n\t\tif (saveState) {\n\t\t\texecutionContext.putInt(getExecutionContextKey(RESOURCE_KEY), currentResource);\n\t\t\tdelegate.update(executionContext);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param delegate reads items from single {@link Resource}.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setDelegate(ResourceAwareItemReaderItemStream<? extends T> delegate) {\n\t\tthis.delegate = delegate;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Set the boolean indicating whether state should be saved in the provided\n\t * {@link ExecutionContext} during the {@link ItemStream} call to update.\n\t * @param saveState true to update ExecutionContext. False do not update\n\t * ExecutionContext.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setSaveState(boolean saveState) {\n\t\tthis.saveState = saveState;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param comparator used to order the injected resources, by default compares\n\t * {@link Resource#getFilename()} values.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setComparator(Comparator<Resource> comparator) {\n\t\tthis.comparator = comparator;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param resources input resources\n\t */\n\tpublic void setResources(Resource[] resources) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(resources, \"The resources must not be null\");\n\t\tthis.resources = resources.clone();\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/file/SimpleBinaryBufferedReaderFactory.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2024 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file;\n\nimport java.io.BufferedReader;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.io.InputStreamReader;\nimport java.io.Reader;\nimport java.util.concurrent.locks.Lock;\nimport java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock;\n\nimport org.springframework.core.io.Resource;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\n/**\n * A {@link BufferedReaderFactory} useful for reading simple binary (or text) files with\n * no line endings, such as those produced by mainframe copy books. The reader splits a\n * stream up across fixed line endings (rather than the usual convention based on plain\n * text). The line endings are discarded, just as with the default plain text\n * implementation.\n *\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @since 2.1\n */\npublic class SimpleBinaryBufferedReaderFactory implements BufferedReaderFactory {\n\n\t/**\n\t * The default line ending value.\n\t */\n\tprivate static final String DEFAULT_LINE_ENDING = \"\\n\";\n\n\tprivate String lineEnding = DEFAULT_LINE_ENDING;\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param lineEnding {@link String} indicating what defines the end of a \"line\".\n\t */\n\tpublic void setLineEnding(String lineEnding) {\n\t\tthis.lineEnding = lineEnding;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic BufferedReader create(Resource resource, String encoding) throws IOException {\n\t\treturn new BinaryBufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(resource.getInputStream(), encoding), lineEnding);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * BufferedReader extension that splits lines based on a line ending, rather than the\n\t * usual plain text conventions.\n\t *\n\t * @author Dave Syer\n\t * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n\t *\n\t */\n\tprivate static final class BinaryBufferedReader extends BufferedReader {\n\n\t\tprivate final String ending;\n\n\t\tprivate final Lock lock = new ReentrantLock();\n\n\t\tprivate BinaryBufferedReader(Reader in, String ending) {\n\t\t\tsuper(in);\n\t\t\tthis.ending = ending;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t@Override\n\t\tpublic @Nullable String readLine() throws IOException {\n\n\t\t\tStringBuilder buffer;\n\n\t\t\tthis.lock.lock();\n\t\t\ttry {\n\n\t\t\t\tint next = read();\n\t\t\t\tif (next == -1) {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t\tbuffer = new StringBuilder();\n\t\t\t\tStringBuilder candidateEnding = new StringBuilder();\n\n\t\t\t\twhile (!isEndOfLine(buffer, candidateEnding, next)) {\n\t\t\t\t\tnext = read();\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tbuffer.append(candidateEnding);\n\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\t\tthis.lock.unlock();\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif (buffer != null && buffer.length() > 0) {\n\t\t\t\treturn buffer.toString();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t/**\n\t\t * Check for end of line and accumulate a buffer for next time.\n\t\t * @param buffer the current line excluding the candidate ending\n\t\t * @param candidate a buffer containing accumulated state\n\t\t * @param next the next character (or -1 for end of file)\n\t\t * @return true if the values together signify the end of a file\n\t\t */\n\t\tprivate boolean isEndOfLine(StringBuilder buffer, StringBuilder candidate, int next) {\n\n\t\t\tif (next == -1) {\n\t\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tchar c = (char) next;\n\t\t\tif (ending.charAt(0) == c || !candidate.isEmpty()) {\n\t\t\t\tcandidate.append(c);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\tbuffer.append(c);\n\t\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif (ending.contentEquals(candidate)) {\n\t\t\t\tcandidate.delete(0, candidate.length());\n\t\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\twhile (!ending.startsWith(candidate.toString())) {\n\t\t\t\tbuffer.append(candidate.charAt(0));\n\t\t\t\tcandidate.delete(0, 1);\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\treturn false;\n\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/file/mapping/BeanWrapperFieldSetMapper.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file.mapping;\n\nimport java.beans.PropertyEditor;\nimport java.lang.reflect.InvocationTargetException;\nimport java.util.HashMap;\nimport java.util.HashSet;\nimport java.util.Map;\nimport java.util.Properties;\nimport java.util.Set;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentMap;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file.transform.FieldSet;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.DefaultPropertyEditorRegistrar;\nimport org.springframework.beans.BeanWrapperImpl;\nimport org.springframework.beans.MutablePropertyValues;\nimport org.springframework.beans.NotWritablePropertyException;\nimport org.springframework.beans.PropertyAccessor;\nimport org.springframework.beans.PropertyAccessorUtils;\nimport org.springframework.beans.PropertyEditorRegistry;\nimport org.springframework.beans.factory.BeanFactory;\nimport org.springframework.beans.factory.BeanFactoryAware;\nimport org.springframework.beans.factory.InitializingBean;\nimport org.springframework.beans.factory.config.CustomEditorConfigurer;\nimport org.springframework.core.convert.ConversionService;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\nimport org.springframework.util.ReflectionUtils;\nimport org.springframework.validation.BindException;\nimport org.springframework.validation.DataBinder;\n\n/**\n * {@link FieldSetMapper} implementation based on bean property paths. The\n * {@link FieldSet} to be mapped should have field name meta data corresponding to bean\n * property paths in an instance of the desired type. The instance is created and\n * initialized either by referring to a prototype object by bean name in the enclosing\n * BeanFactory, or by providing a class to instantiate reflectively.<br>\n * <br>\n *\n * Nested property paths, including indexed properties in maps and collections, can be\n * referenced by the {@link FieldSet} names. They will be converted to nested bean\n * properties inside the prototype. The {@link FieldSet} and the prototype are thus\n * tightly coupled by the fields that are available and those that can be initialized. If\n * some of the nested properties are optional (e.g. collection members) they need to be\n * removed by a post processor.<br>\n * <br>\n *\n * To customize the way that {@link FieldSet} values are converted to the desired type for\n * injecting into the prototype there are several choices. You can inject\n * {@link PropertyEditor} instances directly through the {@link #setCustomEditors(Map)\n * customEditors} property, or you can override the {@link #createBinder(Object)} and\n * {@link #initBinder(DataBinder)} methods, or you can provide a custom {@link FieldSet}\n * implementation. You can also use a {@link ConversionService} to convert to the desired\n * type through the {@link #setConversionService(ConversionService) conversionService}\n * property. <br>\n * <br>\n *\n * Property name matching is \"fuzzy\" in the sense that it tolerates close matches, as long\n * as the match is unique. For instance:\n *\n * <ul>\n * <li>Quantity = quantity (field names can be capitalised)</li>\n * <li>ISIN = isin (acronyms can be lower case bean property names, as per Java Beans\n * recommendations)</li>\n * <li>DuckPate = duckPate (capitalisation including camel casing)</li>\n * <li>ITEM_ID = itemId (capitalisation and replacing word boundary with underscore)</li>\n * <li>ORDER.CUSTOMER_ID = order.customerId (nested paths are recursively checked)</li>\n * </ul>\n *\n * The algorithm used to match a property name is to start with an exact match and then\n * search successively through more distant matches until precisely one match is found. If\n * more than one match is found there will be an error.\n *\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Stefano Cordio\n */\npublic class BeanWrapperFieldSetMapper<T> extends DefaultPropertyEditorRegistrar\n\t\timplements FieldSetMapper<T>, BeanFactoryAware, InitializingBean {\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String name;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Class<? extends T> type;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable BeanFactory beanFactory;\n\n\tprivate final ConcurrentMap<DistanceHolder, ConcurrentMap<String, String>> propertiesMatched = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();\n\n\tprivate int distanceLimit = 5;\n\n\tprivate boolean strict = true;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable ConversionService conversionService;\n\n\tprivate boolean isCustomEditorsSet;\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void setBeanFactory(BeanFactory beanFactory) {\n\t\tthis.beanFactory = beanFactory;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The maximum difference that can be tolerated in spelling between input key names\n\t * and bean property names. Defaults to 5, but could be set lower if the field names\n\t * match the bean names.\n\t * @param distanceLimit the distance limit to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setDistanceLimit(int distanceLimit) {\n\t\tthis.distanceLimit = distanceLimit;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * The bean name (id) for an object that can be populated from the field set that will\n\t * be passed into {@link #mapFieldSet(FieldSet)}. Typically a prototype scoped bean so\n\t * that a new instance is returned for each field set mapped.\n\t * <p>\n\t * Either this property or the type property must be specified, but not both.\n\t * @param name the name of a prototype bean in the enclosing BeanFactory\n\t */\n\tpublic void setPrototypeBeanName(String name) {\n\t\tthis.name = name;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Public setter for the type of bean to create instead of using a prototype bean. An\n\t * object of this type will be created from its default constructor for every call to\n\t * {@link #mapFieldSet(FieldSet)}.<br>\n\t *\n\t * Either this property or the prototype bean name must be specified, but not both.\n\t * @param type the type to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setTargetType(Class<? extends T> type) {\n\t\tthis.type = type;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Check that precisely one of type or prototype bean name is specified.\n\t * @throws IllegalStateException if neither is set or both properties are set.\n\t *\n\t * @see InitializingBean#afterPropertiesSet()\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void afterPropertiesSet() throws Exception {\n\t\tAssert.state(name != null || type != null, \"Either name or type must be provided.\");\n\t\tAssert.state(name == null || type == null, \"Both name and type cannot be specified together.\");\n\t\tAssert.state(!this.isCustomEditorsSet || this.conversionService == null,\n\t\t\t\t\"Both customEditor and conversionService cannot be specified together.\");\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Map the {@link FieldSet} to an object retrieved from the enclosing Spring context,\n\t * or to a new instance of the required type if no prototype is available.\n\t * @throws BindException if there is a type conversion or other error (if the\n\t * {@link DataBinder} from {@link #createBinder(Object)} has errors after binding).\n\t * @throws NotWritablePropertyException if the {@link FieldSet} contains a field that\n\t * cannot be mapped to a bean property.\n\t * @see FieldSetMapper#mapFieldSet(FieldSet)\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic T mapFieldSet(FieldSet fs) throws BindException {\n\t\tT copy = getBean();\n\t\tDataBinder binder = createBinder(copy);\n\t\tbinder.bind(new MutablePropertyValues(getBeanProperties(copy, fs.getProperties())));\n\t\tif (binder.getBindingResult().hasErrors()) {\n\t\t\tthrow new BindException(binder.getBindingResult());\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn copy;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Create a binder for the target object. The binder will then be used to bind the\n\t * properties form a field set into the target object. This implementation creates a\n\t * new {@link DataBinder} and calls out to {@link #initBinder(DataBinder)} and\n\t * {@link #registerCustomEditors(PropertyEditorRegistry)}.\n\t * @param target Object to bind to\n\t * @return a {@link DataBinder} that can be used to bind properties to the target.\n\t */\n\tprotected DataBinder createBinder(Object target) {\n\t\tDataBinder binder = new DataBinder(target);\n\t\tbinder.setIgnoreUnknownFields(!this.strict);\n\t\tinitBinder(binder);\n\t\tregisterCustomEditors(binder);\n\t\tif (this.conversionService != null) {\n\t\t\tbinder.setConversionService(this.conversionService);\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn binder;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Initialize a new binder instance. This hook allows customization of binder settings\n\t * such as the {@link DataBinder#initDirectFieldAccess() direct field access}. Called\n\t * by {@link #createBinder(Object)}.\n\t * <p>\n\t * Note that registration of custom property editors can be done in\n\t * {@link #registerCustomEditors(PropertyEditorRegistry)}.\n\t * </p>\n\t * @param binder new binder instance\n\t * @see #createBinder(Object)\n\t */\n\tprotected void initBinder(DataBinder binder) {\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings({ \"unchecked\", \"DataFlowIssue\" })\n\tprivate T getBean() {\n\t\tif (name != null) {\n\t\t\treturn (T) beanFactory.getBean(name);\n\t\t}\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\treturn type.getDeclaredConstructor().newInstance();\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (InstantiationException | IllegalAccessException | NoSuchMethodException | InvocationTargetException e) {\n\t\t\tReflectionUtils.handleReflectionException(e);\n\t\t}\n\t\t// should not happen\n\t\tthrow new IllegalStateException(\"Internal error: could not create bean instance for mapping.\");\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param bean Object to get properties for\n\t * @param properties Properties to retrieve\n\t */\n\tprivate Properties getBeanProperties(Object bean, Properties properties) {\n\n\t\tif (this.distanceLimit == 0) {\n\t\t\treturn properties;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tClass<?> cls = bean.getClass();\n\n\t\t// Map from field names to property names\n\t\tDistanceHolder distanceKey = new DistanceHolder(cls, distanceLimit);\n\t\tif (!propertiesMatched.containsKey(distanceKey)) {\n\t\t\tpropertiesMatched.putIfAbsent(distanceKey, new ConcurrentHashMap<>());\n\t\t}\n\t\tMap<String, String> matches = new HashMap<>(propertiesMatched.get(distanceKey));\n\n\t\t@SuppressWarnings({ \"unchecked\", \"rawtypes\" })\n\t\tSet<String> keys = new HashSet(properties.keySet());\n\t\tfor (String key : keys) {\n\n\t\t\tif (matches.containsKey(key)) {\n\t\t\t\tswitchPropertyNames(properties, key, matches.get(key));\n\t\t\t\tcontinue;\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tString name = findPropertyName(bean, key);\n\n\t\t\tif (name != null) {\n\t\t\t\tif (matches.containsValue(name)) {\n\t\t\t\t\tthrow new NotWritablePropertyException(cls, name, \"Duplicate match with distance <= \"\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t+ distanceLimit + \" found for this property in input keys: \" + keys\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t+ \". (Consider reducing the distance limit or changing the input key names to get a closer match.)\");\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tmatches.put(key, name);\n\t\t\t\tswitchPropertyNames(properties, key, name);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tpropertiesMatched.replace(distanceKey, new ConcurrentHashMap<>(matches));\n\t\treturn properties;\n\t}\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String findPropertyName(Object bean, String key) {\n\n\t\tClass<?> cls = bean.getClass();\n\n\t\tint index = PropertyAccessorUtils.getFirstNestedPropertySeparatorIndex(key);\n\t\tString prefix;\n\t\tString suffix;\n\n\t\t// If the property name is nested recurse down through the properties\n\t\t// looking for a match.\n\t\tif (index > 0) {\n\t\t\tprefix = key.substring(0, index);\n\t\t\tsuffix = key.substring(index + 1);\n\t\t\tString nestedName = findPropertyName(bean, prefix);\n\t\t\tif (nestedName == null) {\n\t\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tObject nestedValue = getPropertyValue(bean, nestedName);\n\t\t\tString nestedPropertyName = findPropertyName(nestedValue, suffix);\n\t\t\treturn nestedPropertyName == null ? null : nestedName + \".\" + nestedPropertyName;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tString name = null;\n\t\tint distance = 0;\n\t\tindex = key.indexOf(PropertyAccessor.PROPERTY_KEY_PREFIX_CHAR);\n\n\t\tif (index > 0) {\n\t\t\tprefix = key.substring(0, index);\n\t\t\tsuffix = key.substring(index);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\tprefix = key;\n\t\t\tsuffix = \"\";\n\t\t}\n\n\t\twhile (name == null && distance <= distanceLimit) {\n\t\t\tString[] candidates = PropertyMatches.forProperty(prefix, cls, distance).getPossibleMatches();\n\t\t\t// If we find precisely one match, then use that one...\n\t\t\tif (candidates.length == 1) {\n\t\t\t\tString candidate = candidates[0];\n\t\t\t\tif (candidate.equals(prefix)) { // if it's the same don't\n\t\t\t\t\t// replace it...\n\t\t\t\t\tname = key;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\t\tname = candidate + suffix;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tdistance++;\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn name;\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\tprivate Object getPropertyValue(Object bean, String nestedName) {\n\t\tBeanWrapperImpl wrapper = new BeanWrapperImpl(bean);\n\t\twrapper.setAutoGrowNestedPaths(true);\n\n\t\tObject nestedValue = wrapper.getPropertyValue(nestedName);\n\t\tif (nestedValue == null) {\n\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\tnestedValue = wrapper.getPropertyType(nestedName).getDeclaredConstructor().newInstance();\n\t\t\t\twrapper.setPropertyValue(nestedName, nestedValue);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcatch (InstantiationException | IllegalAccessException | NoSuchMethodException\n\t\t\t\t\t| InvocationTargetException e) {\n\t\t\t\tReflectionUtils.handleReflectionException(e);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nestedValue;\n\t}\n\n\tprivate void switchPropertyNames(Properties properties, String oldName, String newName) {\n\t\tString value = properties.getProperty(oldName);\n\t\tproperties.remove(oldName);\n\t\tproperties.setProperty(newName, value);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Public setter for the 'strict' property. If true, then\n\t * {@link #mapFieldSet(FieldSet)} will fail of the FieldSet contains fields that\n\t * cannot be mapped to the bean.\n\t * @param strict indicator\n\t */\n\tpublic void setStrict(boolean strict) {\n\t\tthis.strict = strict;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Public setter for the 'conversionService' property. {@link #createBinder(Object)}\n\t * will use it if not null.\n\t * @param conversionService {@link ConversionService} to be used for type conversions\n\t */\n\tpublic void setConversionService(ConversionService conversionService) {\n\t\tthis.conversionService = conversionService;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Specify the {@link PropertyEditor custom editors} to register.\n\t * @param customEditors a map of Class to PropertyEditor (or class name to\n\t * PropertyEditor).\n\t * @see CustomEditorConfigurer#setCustomEditors(Map)\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void setCustomEditors(Map<?, ? extends PropertyEditor> customEditors) {\n\t\tthis.isCustomEditorsSet = true;\n\t\tsuper.setCustomEditors(customEditors);\n\t}\n\n\tprivate static class DistanceHolder {\n\n\t\tprivate final Class<?> cls;\n\n\t\tprivate final int distance;\n\n\t\tpublic DistanceHolder(Class<?> cls, int distance) {\n\t\t\tthis.cls = cls;\n\t\t\tthis.distance = distance;\n\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t@Override\n\t\tpublic int hashCode() {\n\t\t\tfinal int prime = 31;\n\t\t\tint result = 1;\n\t\t\tresult = prime * result + ((cls == null) ? 0 : cls.hashCode());\n\t\t\tresult = prime * result + distance;\n\t\t\treturn result;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t@Override\n\t\tpublic boolean equals(Object obj) {\n\t\t\tif (this == obj)\n\t\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t\tif (obj == null)\n\t\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t\tif (getClass() != obj.getClass())\n\t\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t\tDistanceHolder other = (DistanceHolder) obj;\n\t\t\tif (cls == null) {\n\t\t\t\tif (other.cls != null)\n\t\t\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\telse if (!cls.equals(other.cls))\n\t\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t\treturn distance == other.distance;\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/file/transform/AbstractLineTokenizer.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file.transform;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\n\nimport org.springframework.util.StringUtils;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\n/**\n * Abstract class handling common concerns of various {@link LineTokenizer}\n * implementations such as dealing with names and actual construction of {@link FieldSet}\n *\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Robert Kasanicky\n * @author Lucas Ward\n * @author Michael Minella\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Stefano Cordio\n */\npublic abstract class AbstractLineTokenizer implements LineTokenizer {\n\n\tprivate static final String EMPTY_TOKEN = \"\";\n\n\tprotected String[] names = new String[0];\n\n\tprivate boolean strict = true;\n\n\tprivate FieldSetFactory fieldSetFactory = new DefaultFieldSetFactory();\n\n\t/**\n\t * Public setter for the strict flag. If true (the default) then number of tokens in\n\t * line must match the number of tokens defined (by {@link Range}, columns, etc.) in\n\t * {@link LineTokenizer}. If false then lines with less tokens will be tolerated and\n\t * padded with empty columns, and lines with more tokens will simply be truncated.\n\t * @param strict the strict flag to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setStrict(boolean strict) {\n\t\tthis.strict = strict;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Provides access to the strict flag for subclasses if needed.\n\t * @return the strict flag value\n\t */\n\tprotected boolean isStrict() {\n\t\treturn strict;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Factory for {@link FieldSet} instances. Can be injected by clients to customize the\n\t * default number and date formats.\n\t * @param fieldSetFactory the {@link FieldSetFactory} to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setFieldSetFactory(FieldSetFactory fieldSetFactory) {\n\t\tthis.fieldSetFactory = fieldSetFactory;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Setter for column names. Optional, but if set, then all lines must have as many or\n\t * fewer tokens.\n\t * @param names names of each column\n\t */\n\tpublic void setNames(String... names) {\n\t\tboolean valid = false;\n\t\tfor (String name : names) {\n\t\t\tif (StringUtils.hasText(name)) {\n\t\t\t\tvalid = true;\n\t\t\t\tbreak;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (valid) {\n\t\t\tthis.names = names.clone();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return {@code true} if column names have been specified\n\t * @see #setNames(String[])\n\t */\n\tpublic boolean hasNames() {\n\t\treturn names.length > 0;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Yields the tokens resulting from the splitting of the supplied <code>line</code>.\n\t * @param line the line to be tokenized (can be <code>null</code>)\n\t * @return the resulting tokens\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic FieldSet tokenize(@Nullable String line) {\n\n\t\tif (line == null) {\n\t\t\tline = \"\";\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tList<String> tokens = new ArrayList<>(doTokenize(line));\n\n\t\t// if names are set and strict flag is false\n\t\tif (names.length != 0 && !strict) {\n\t\t\tadjustTokenCountIfNecessary(tokens);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tString[] values = tokens.toArray(new String[0]);\n\n\t\tif (names.length == 0) {\n\t\t\treturn fieldSetFactory.create(values);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (values.length != names.length) {\n\t\t\tthrow new IncorrectTokenCountException(names.length, values.length, line);\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn fieldSetFactory.create(values, names);\n\t}\n\n\tprotected abstract List<String> doTokenize(String line);\n\n\t/**\n\t * Adds empty tokens or truncates existing token list to match expected (configured)\n\t * number of tokens in {@link LineTokenizer}.\n\t * @param tokens - list of tokens\n\t */\n\tprivate void adjustTokenCountIfNecessary(List<String> tokens) {\n\n\t\tint nameLength = names.length;\n\t\tint tokensSize = tokens.size();\n\n\t\t// if the number of tokens is not what expected\n\t\tif (nameLength != tokensSize) {\n\n\t\t\tif (nameLength > tokensSize) {\n\n\t\t\t\t// add empty tokens until the token list size matches\n\t\t\t\t// the expected number of tokens\n\t\t\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < (nameLength - tokensSize); i++) {\n\t\t\t\t\ttokens.add(EMPTY_TOKEN);\n\t\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\t// truncate token list to match the number of expected tokens\n\t\t\t\ttokens.subList(nameLength, tokensSize).clear();\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/file/transform/BeanWrapperFieldExtractor.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file.transform;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\nimport org.springframework.beans.BeanWrapper;\nimport org.springframework.beans.BeanWrapperImpl;\nimport org.springframework.beans.factory.InitializingBean;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\n\n/**\n * This is a field extractor for a java bean. Given an array of property names, it will\n * reflectively call getters on the item and return an array of all the values.\n *\n * @author Dan Garrette\n * @since 2.0\n */\npublic class BeanWrapperFieldExtractor<T> implements FieldExtractor<T> {\n\n\tprivate String[] names;\n\n\t/**\n\t * Create a new {@link BeanWrapperFieldExtractor} with the provided field names.\n\t * @param names field names to be extracted by the {@link #extract(Object)} method.\n\t * @since 6.0\n\t */\n\tpublic BeanWrapperFieldExtractor(String... names) {\n\t\tthis.names = names;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param names field names to be extracted by the {@link #extract(Object)} method.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setNames(String[] names) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(names, \"Names must be non-null\");\n\t\tthis.names = names.clone();\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic Object[] extract(T item) {\n\t\tList<@Nullable Object> values = new ArrayList<>();\n\n\t\tBeanWrapper bw = new BeanWrapperImpl(item);\n\t\tfor (String propertyName : this.names) {\n\t\t\tvalues.add(bw.getPropertyValue(propertyName));\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn values.toArray();\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/file/transform/FieldExtractor.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file.transform;\n\n/**\n * This class will convert an object to an array of its parts.\n *\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n *\n */\npublic interface FieldExtractor<T> {\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param item the object that contains the information to be extracted.\n\t * @return an array containing item's parts\n\t */\n\tObject[] extract(T item);\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/file/transform/FieldSet.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n* Copyright 2006-2007 the original author or authors.\n*\n* Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n* you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n* You may obtain a copy of the License at\n*\n*      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n*\n* Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n* distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n* WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n* See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n* limitations under the License.\n*/\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file.transform;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport java.math.BigDecimal;\nimport java.sql.ResultSet;\nimport java.util.Date;\nimport java.util.Properties;\n\n/**\n * Interface used by flat file input sources to encapsulate concerns of converting an\n * array of Strings to Java native types. A bit like the role played by {@link ResultSet}\n * in JDBC, clients will know the name or position of strongly typed fields that they want\n * to extract.\n *\n * @author Dave Syer\n *\n */\npublic interface FieldSet {\n\n\t/**\n\t * Accessor for the names of the fields.\n\t * @return the names\n\t * @throws IllegalStateException if the names are not defined\n\t */\n\tString[] getNames();\n\n\t/**\n\t * Check if there are names defined for the fields.\n\t * @return true if there are names for the fields\n\t */\n\tboolean hasNames();\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return fields wrapped by this '<code>FieldSet</code>' instance as String values.\n\t */\n\t@Nullable String[] getValues();\n\n\t/**\n\t * Read the {@link String} value at index '<code>index</code>'.\n\t * @param index the field index.\n\t * @return {@link String} containing the value at the index.\n\t * @throws IndexOutOfBoundsException if the {@code index} is out of bounds.\n\t */\n\t@Nullable String readString(int index);\n\n\t/**\n\t * Read the {@link String} value from column with given '<code>name</code>'.\n\t * @param name the field {@code name}.\n\t * @return {@link String} containing the value from the specified {@code name}.\n\t */\n\t@Nullable String readString(String name);\n\n\t/**\n\t * Read the {@link String} value at index '<code>index</code>' including trailing\n\t * whitespace (don't trim).\n\t * @param index the field index.\n\t * @return {@link String} containing the value from the specified {@code index}.\n\t * @throws IndexOutOfBoundsException if the {@code index} is out of bounds.\n\t */\n\t@Nullable String readRawString(int index);\n\n\t/**\n\t * Read the {@link String} value from column with given '<code>name</code>' including\n\t * trailing whitespace (don't trim).\n\t * @param name the field {@code name}.\n\t * @return {@link String} containing the value from the specified {@code name}.\n\t */\n\t@Nullable String readRawString(String name);\n\n\t/**\n\t       "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/file/transform/RangeArrayPropertyEditor.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file.transform;\n\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\nimport org.springframework.util.StringUtils;\n\nimport java.beans.PropertyEditorSupport;\nimport java.util.Arrays;\nimport java.util.Comparator;\n\n/**\n * Property editor implementation which parses string and creates array of ranges. Ranges\n * can be provided in any order. <br>\n * Input string should be provided in following format: 'range1, range2, range3,...' where\n * range is specified as:\n * <ul>\n * <li>'X-Y', where X is minimum value and Y is maximum value (condition X&lt;=Y is\n * verified)</li>\n * <li>or 'Z', where Z is minimum and maximum is calculated as (minimum of adjacent range\n * - 1). Maximum of the last range is never calculated. Range stays unbound at maximum\n * side if maximum value is not provided.</li>\n * </ul>\n * Minimum and maximum values can be from interval &lt;1, Integer.MAX_VALUE-1&gt;\n * <p>\n * Examples:<br>\n * '1, 15, 25, 38, 55-60' is equal to '1-14, 15-24, 25-37, 38-54, 55-60' <br>\n * '36, 14, 1-10, 15, 49-57' is equal to '36-48, 14-14, 1-10, 15-35, 49-57'\n * <p>\n * Property editor also allows to validate whether ranges are disjoint. Validation can be\n * turned on/off by using {@link #setForceDisjointRanges(boolean)}. By default validation\n * is turned off.\n *\n * @author peter.zozom\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n */\npublic class RangeArrayPropertyEditor extends PropertyEditorSupport {\n\n\tprivate boolean forceDisjointRanges = false;\n\n\t/**\n\t * Set force disjoint ranges. If set to TRUE, ranges are validated to be disjoint. For\n\t * example: defining ranges '1-10, 5-15' will cause IllegalArgumentException in case\n\t * of forceDisjointRanges=TRUE.\n\t * @param forceDisjointRanges true to force disjoint ranges.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setForceDisjointRanges(boolean forceDisjointRanges) {\n\t\tthis.forceDisjointRanges = forceDisjointRanges;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void setAsText(String text) throws IllegalArgumentException {\n\n\t\t// split text into ranges\n\t\tString[] strRanges = text.split(\",\");\n\t\tRange[] ranges = new Range[strRanges.length];\n\n\t\t// parse ranges and create array of Range objects\n\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < strRanges.length; i++) {\n\t\t\tString[] range = strRanges[i].split(\"-\");\n\n\t\t\tint min;\n\t\t\tint max;\n\n\t\t\tif (range.length == 1 && StringUtils.hasText(range[0])) {\n\t\t\t\tmin = Integer.parseInt(range[0].trim());\n\t\t\t\t// correct max value will be assigned later\n\t\t\t\tranges[i] = new Range(min);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\telse if (range.length == 2 && StringUtils.hasText(range[0]) && StringUtils.hasText(range[1])) {\n\t\t\t\tmin = Integer.parseInt(range[0].trim());\n\t\t\t\tmax = Integer.parseInt(range[1].trim());\n\t\t\t\tranges[i] = new Range(min, max);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new IllegalArgumentException(\"Range[\" + i + \"]: range (\" + strRanges[i] + \") is invalid\");\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tsetMaxValues(ranges);\n\t\tsetValue(ranges);\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic String getAsText() {\n\t\tRange[] ranges = (Range[]) getValue();\n\n\t\tStringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();\n\n\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < ranges.length; i++) {\n\t\t\tif (i > 0) {\n\t\t\t\tsb.append(\", \");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tsb.append(ranges[i]);\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn sb.toString();\n\t}\n\n\tprivate void setMaxValues(Range[] ranges) {\n\n\t\t// Array of integers to track range values by index\n\t\tInteger[] c = new Integer[ranges.length];\n\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < c.length; i++) {\n\t\t\tc[i] = i;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// sort array of Ranges\n\t\tArrays.sort(c, Comparator.comparingInt(r -> ranges[r].getMin()));\n\n\t\t// set max values for all unbound ranges (except last range)\n\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < c.length - 1; i++) {\n\t\t\tif (!ranges[c[i]].hasMaxValue()) {\n\t\t\t\t// set max value to (min value - 1) of the next range\n\t\t\t\tranges[c[i]] = new Range(ranges[c[i]].getMin(), ranges[c[i + 1]].getMin() - 1);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (forceDisjointRanges) {\n\t\t\tverifyRanges(ranges);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tprivate void verifyRanges(Range[] ranges) {\n\t\t// verify that ranges are disjoint\n\t\tfor (int i = 1; i < ranges.length; i++) {\n\t\t\tAssert.isTrue(ranges[i - 1].getMax() < ranges[i].getMin(), \"Ranges must be disjoint. Range[\" + (i - 1)\n\t\t\t\t\t+ \"]: (\" + ranges[i - 1] + \") Range[\" + i + \"]: (\" + ranges[i] + \")\");\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/file/transform/RegexLineTokenizer.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file.transform;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.regex.Matcher;\nimport java.util.regex.Pattern;\n\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\n\n/**\n * Line-tokenizer using a regular expression to filter out data (by using matching and\n * non-matching groups). Consider the following regex which picks only the first and last\n * name (notice the non-matching group in the middle): <pre>\n * (.*?)(?: .*)* (.*)\n * </pre> For the names:\n * <ul>\n * <li>\"Graham James Edward Miller\"</li>\n * <li>\"Andrew Gregory Macintyre\"</li>\n * <li>\"No MiddleName\"</li>\n * </ul>\n *\n * the output will be:\n * <ul>\n * <li>\"Miller\", \"Graham\"</li>\n * <li>\"Macintyre\", \"Andrew\"</li>\n * <li>\"MiddleName\", \"No\"</li>\n * </ul>\n *\n * An empty list is returned, in case of a non-match.\n *\n * @see Matcher#group(int)\n * @author Costin Leau\n * @author Stefano Cordio\n */\npublic class RegexLineTokenizer extends AbstractLineTokenizer {\n\n\tprivate Pattern pattern;\n\n\t/**\n\t * Create a new {@link RegexLineTokenizer} with the provided regex.\n\t * @param regex regular expression (as a String)\n\t * @since 6.0\n\t */\n\tpublic RegexLineTokenizer(String regex) {\n\t\tAssert.hasText(regex, \"a valid regex is required\");\n\t\tthis.pattern = Pattern.compile(regex);\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected List<String> doTokenize(String line) {\n\t\tMatcher matcher = pattern.matcher(line);\n\t\tboolean matchFound = matcher.find();\n\n\t\tif (matchFound) {\n\t\t\tList<String> tokens = new ArrayList<>(matcher.groupCount());\n\t\t\tfor (int i = 1; i <= matcher.groupCount(); i++) {\n\t\t\t\ttokens.add(matcher.group(i));\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn tokens;\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn Collections.emptyList();\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Sets the regex pattern to use.\n\t * @param pattern Regular Expression pattern\n\t */\n\tpublic void setPattern(Pattern pattern) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(pattern, \"a non-null pattern is required\");\n\t\tthis.pattern = pattern;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Sets the regular expression to use.\n\t * @param regex regular expression (as a String)\n\t */\n\tpublic void setRegex(String regex) {\n\t\tAssert.hasText(regex, \"a valid regex is required\");\n\t\tthis.pattern = Pattern.compile(regex);\n\t}\n\n}"
    },
    {
      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/jms/JmsMethodInvocationRecoverer.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.jms;\n\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.Log;\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.LogFactory;\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport org.springframework.retry.interceptor.MethodInvocationRecoverer;\nimport org.springframework.jms.JmsException;\nimport org.springframework.jms.core.JmsOperations;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\n\n/**\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n *\n */\npublic class JmsMethodInvocationRecoverer<T> implements MethodInvocationRecoverer<T> {\n\n\tprotected Log logger = LogFactory.getLog(getClass());\n\n\tprivate JmsOperations jmsTemplate;\n\n\t/**\n\t * Create a new {@link JmsMethodInvocationRecoverer} with the provided\n\t * {@link JmsOperations}.\n\t * @param jmsTemplate a {@link JmsOperations} instance\n\t * @since 6.0\n\t */\n\tpublic JmsMethodInvocationRecoverer(JmsOperations jmsTemplate) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(jmsTemplate, \"jmsTemplate must not be null\");\n\t\tthis.jmsTemplate = jmsTemplate;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Setter for jms template.\n\t * @param jmsTemplate a {@link JmsOperations} instance\n\t */\n\tpublic void setJmsTemplate(JmsOperations jmsTemplate) {\n\t\tthis.jmsTemplate = jmsTemplate;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Send one message per item in the argument list using the default destination of the\n\t * jms template. If the recovery is successful {@code null} is returned.\n\t *\n\t * @see MethodInvocationRecoverer#recover(Object[], Throwable)\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic @Nullable T recover(Object[] items, Throwable cause) {\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tfor (Object item : items) {\n\t\t\t\tjmsTemplate.convertAndSend(item);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (JmsException e) {\n\t\t\tlogger.error(\"Could not recover because of JmsException.\", e);\n\t\t\tthrow e;\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/json/GsonJsonObjectReader.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2018-2024 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *       https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.json;\n\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.io.InputStream;\nimport java.io.InputStreamReader;\n\nimport com.google.gson.Gson;\nimport com.google.gson.JsonIOException;\nimport com.google.gson.JsonSyntaxException;\nimport com.google.gson.stream.JsonReader;\nimport com.google.gson.stream.JsonToken;\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ParseException;\nimport org.springframework.core.io.Resource;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\n\n/**\n * Implementation of {@link JsonObjectReader} based on\n * <a href=\"https://github.com/google/gson\">Google Gson</a>.\n *\n * @param <T> type of the target object\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Jimmy Praet\n * @since 4.1\n */\npublic class GsonJsonObjectReader<T> implements JsonObjectReader<T> {\n\n\tprivate final Class<? extends T> itemType;\n\n\tprivate Gson mapper;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable JsonReader jsonReader;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable InputStream inputStream;\n\n\t/**\n\t * Create a new {@link GsonJsonObjectReader} instance.\n\t * @param itemType the target item type\n\t */\n\tpublic GsonJsonObjectReader(Class<? extends T> itemType) {\n\t\tthis(new Gson(), itemType);\n\t}\n\n\tpublic GsonJsonObjectReader(Gson mapper, Class<? extends T> itemType) {\n\t\tthis.mapper = mapper;\n\t\tthis.itemType = itemType;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Set the object mapper to use to map Json objects to domain objects.\n\t * @param mapper the object mapper to use\n\t * @see #GsonJsonObjectReader(Gson, Class)\n\t */\n\tpublic void setMapper(Gson mapper) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(mapper, \"The mapper must not be null\");\n\t\tthis.mapper = mapper;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void open(Resource resource) throws Exception {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(resource, \"The resource must not be null\");\n\t\tthis.inputStream = resource.getInputStream();\n\t\tthis.jsonReader = this.mapper.newJsonReader(new InputStreamReader(this.inputStream));\n\t\tAssert.state(this.jsonReader.peek() == JsonToken.BEGIN_ARRAY,\n\t\t\t\t\"The Json input stream must start with an array of Json objects\");\n\t\tthis.jsonReader.beginArray();\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tpublic @Nullable T read() throws Exception {\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tif (this.jsonReader.hasNext()) {\n\t\t\t\treturn this.mapper.fromJson(this.jsonReader, this.itemType);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (IOException | JsonIOException | JsonSyntaxException e) {\n\t\t\tthrow new ParseException(\"Unable to read next JSON object\", e);\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn null;\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void close() throws Exception {\n\t\tthis.inputStream.close();\n\t\tthis.jsonReader.close();\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void jumpToItem(int itemIndex) throws Exception {\n\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < itemIndex; i++) {\n\t\t\tthis.jsonReader.skipValue();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/support/AbstractFileItemWriter.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *       https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.support;\n\nimport java.io.BufferedWriter;\nimport java.io.File;\nimport java.io.FileOutputStream;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.io.Writer;\nimport java.nio.channels.Channels;\nimport java.nio.channels.FileChannel;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\nimport java.nio.charset.UnsupportedCharsetException;\nimport java.nio.file.Files;\n\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.Log;\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.LogFactory;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.Chunk;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ExecutionContext;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemStream;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemStreamException;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.WriteFailedException;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.WriterNotOpenException;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file.FlatFileFooterCallback;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file.FlatFileHeaderCallback;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file.ResourceAwareItemWriterItemStream;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.util.FileUtils;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.transaction.TransactionAwareBufferedWriter;\nimport org.springframework.beans.factory.InitializingBean;\nimport org.springframework.core.io.WritableResource;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\n\n/**\n * Base class for item writers that write data to a file or stream. This class provides\n * common features like restart, force sync, append etc. The location of the output file\n * is defined by a {@link WritableResource} which must represent a writable file.<br>\n *\n * Uses buffered writer to improve performance.<br>\n *\n * The implementation is <b>not</b> thread-safe.\n *\n * @author Waseem Malik\n * @author Tomas Slanina\n * @author Robert Kasanicky\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Michael Minella\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Glenn Renfro\n * @author Remi Kaeffer\n * @author Elimelec Burghelea\n * @since 4.1\n */\npublic abstract class AbstractFileItemWriter<T> extends AbstractItemStreamItemWriter<T>\n\t\timplements ResourceAwareItemWriterItemStream<T>, InitializingBean {\n\n\tpublic static final boolean DEFAULT_TRANSACTIONAL = true;\n\n\tprotected static final Log logger = LogFactory.getLog(AbstractFileItemWriter.class);\n\n\tpublic static final String DEFAULT_LINE_SEPARATOR = System.lineSeparator();\n\n\tpublic static final String DEFAULT_CHARSET = StandardCharsets.UTF_8.name();\n\n\tprivate static final String WRITTEN_STATISTICS_NAME = \"written\";\n\n\tprivate static final String RESTART_DATA_NAME = \"current.count\";\n\n\tprotected @Nullable WritableResource resource;\n\n\tprotected @Nullable OutputState state;\n\n\tprivate boolean saveState = true;\n\n\tprivate boolean forceSync = false;\n\n\tprotected boolean shouldDeleteIfExists = true;\n\n\tprivate boolean shouldDeleteIfEmpty = false;\n\n\tprivate String encoding = DEFAULT_CHARSET;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable FlatFileHeaderCallback headerCallback;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable FlatFileFooterCallback footerCallback;\n\n\tprotected String lineSeparator = DEFAULT_LINE_SEPARATOR;\n\n\tprivate boolean transactional = DEFAULT_TRANSACTIONAL;\n\n\tprotected boolean append = false;\n\n\t/**\n\t * Flag to indicate that changes should be force-synced to disk on flush. Defaults to\n\t * false, which means that even with a local disk changes could be lost if the OS\n\t * crashes in between a write and a cache flush. Setting to true may result in slower\n\t * performance for usage patterns involving many frequent writes.\n\t * @param forceSync the flag value to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setForceSync(boolean forceSync) {\n\t\tthis.forceSync = forceSync;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Public setter for the line separator. Defaults to the System property\n\t * line.separator.\n\t * @param lineSeparator the line separator to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setLineSeparator(String lineSeparator) {\n\t\tthis.lineSeparator = lineSeparator;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Setter for a writable resource. Represents a file that can be written.\n\t * @param resource the resource to be written to\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void setResource(WritableResource resource) {\n\t\tthis.resource = resource;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Sets encoding for output template.\n\t * @param newEncoding {@link String} containing the encoding to be used for the\n\t * writer.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setEncoding(String newEncoding) {\n\t\tthis.encoding = newEncoding;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Flag to indicate that the target file should be deleted if it already exists,\n\t * otherwise it will be created. Defaults to true, so no appending except on restart.\n\t * If set to false and {@link #setAppendAllowed(boolean) appendAllowed} is also false\n\t * then there will be an exception when the stream is opened to prevent existing data\n\t * being potentially corrupted.\n\t * @param shouldDeleteIfExists the flag value to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setShouldDeleteIfExists(boolean shouldDeleteIfExists) {\n\t\tthis.shouldDeleteIfExists = shouldDeleteIfExists;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Flag to indicate that the target file should be appended if it already exists. If\n\t * this flag is set then the flag {@link #setShouldDeleteIfExists(boolean)\n\t * shouldDeleteIfExists} is automatically set to false, so that flag should not be set\n\t * explicitly. Defaults value is false.\n\t * @param append the flag value to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setAppendAllowed(boolean append) {\n\t\tthis.append = append;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Flag to indicate that the target file should be deleted if no lines have been\n\t * written (other than header and footer) on close. Defaults to false.\n\t * @param shouldDeleteIfEmpty the flag value to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setShouldDeleteIfEmpty(boolean shouldDeleteIfEmpty) {\n\t\tthis.shouldDeleteIfEmpty = shouldDeleteIfEmpty;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Set the flag indicating whether or not state should be saved in the provided\n\t * {@link ExecutionContext} during the {@link ItemStream} call to update. Setting this\n\t * to false means that it will always start at the beginning on a restart.\n\t * @param saveState if true, state will be persisted\n\t */\n\tpublic void setSaveState(boolean saveState) {\n\t\tthis.saveState = saveState;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * headerCallback will be called before writing the first item to file. Newline will\n\t * be automatically appended after the header is written.\n\t * @param headerCallback {@link FlatFileHeaderCallback} to generate the header\n\t *\n\t */\n\tpublic void setHeaderCallback(FlatFileHeaderCallback headerCallback) {\n\t\tthis.headerCallback = headerCallback;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * footerCallback will be called after writing the last item to file, but before the\n\t * file is closed.\n\t * @param footerCallback {@link FlatFileFooterCallback} to generate the footer\n\t *\n\t */\n\tpublic void setFooterCallback(FlatFileFooterCallback footerCallback) {\n\t\tthis.footerCallback = footerCallback;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Flag to indicate that writing to the buffer should be delayed if a transaction is\n\t * active. Defaults to true.\n\t * @param transactional true if writing to buffer should be delayed.\n\t *\n\t */\n\tpublic void setTransactional(boolean transactional) {\n\t\tthis.transactional = transactional;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Writes out a string followed by a \"new line\", where the format of the new line\n\t * separator is determined by the underlying operating system.\n\t * @param items list of items to be written to output stream\n\t * @throws Exception if an error occurs while writing items to the output stream\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void write(Chunk<? extends T> items) throws Exception {\n\t\tif (!getOutputState().isInitialized()) {\n\t\t\tthrow new WriterNotOpenException(\"Writer must be open before it can be written to\");\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (logger.isDebugEnabled()) {\n\t\t\tlogger.debug(\"Writing to file with \" + items.size() + \" items.\");\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tOutputState state = getOutputState();\n\n\t\tString lines = doWrite(items);\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tstate.write(lines);\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (IOException e) {\n\t\t\tthrow new WriteFailedException(\"Could not write data. The file may be corrupt.\", e);\n\t\t}\n\t\tstate.setLinesWritten(state.getLinesWritten() + items.size());\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Write out a string of items followed by a \"new line\", where the format of the new\n\t * line separator is determined by the underlying operating system.\n\t * @param items to be written\n\t * @return written lines\n\t */\n\tprotected abstract String doWrite(Chunk<? extends T> items);\n\n\t/**\n\t * @see ItemStream#close()\n\t */\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void close() {\n\t\tsuper.close();\n\t\tif (state != null) {\n\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\tif (footerCallback != null && state.outputBufferedWriter != null) {\n\t\t\t\t\tfooterCallback.writeFooter(state.outputBufferedWriter);\n\t\t\t\t\tstate.outputBufferedWriter.flush();\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcatch (IOException e) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new ItemStreamException(\"Failed to write footer before closing\", e);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\t\tstate.close();\n\t\t\t\tif (state.linesWritten == 0 && shouldDeleteIfEmpty) {\n\t\t\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tFiles.delete(resource.getFile().toPath());\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tcatch (IOException | SecurityException e) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tthrow new ItemStreamException(\"Failed to delete empty file on close\", e);\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tstate = null;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Initialize the reader. This method may be called multiple times before close is\n\t * called.\n\t *\n\t * @see ItemStream#open(ExecutionContext)\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void open(ExecutionContext executionContext) throws ItemStreamException {\n\t\tsuper.open(executionContext);\n\n\t\tAssert.notNull(resource, \"The resource must be set\");\n\n\t\tif (!getOutputState().isInitialized()) {\n\t\t\tdoOpen(executionContext);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\tprivate void doOpen(ExecutionContext executionContext) throws ItemStreamException {\n\t\tOutputState outputState = getOutputState();\n\t\tif (executionContext.containsKey(getExecutionContextKey(RESTART_DATA_NAME))) {\n\t\t\toutputState.restoreFrom(executionContext);\n\t\t}\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\toutputState.initializeBufferedWriter();\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (IOException ioe) {\n\t\t\tthrow new ItemStreamException(\"Failed to initialize writer\", ioe);\n\t\t}\n\t\tif (outputState.lastMarkedByteOffsetPosition == 0 && !outputState.appending) {\n\t\t\tif (headerCallback != null) {\n\t\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\t\theaderCallback.writeHeader(outputState.outputBufferedWriter);\n\t\t\t\t\toutputState.write(lineSeparator);\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tcatch (IOException e) {\n\t\t\t\t\tthrow new ItemStreamException(\"Could not write headers.  The file may be corrupt.\", e);\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @see ItemStream#update(ExecutionContext)\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void update(ExecutionContext executionContext) {\n\t\tsuper.update(executionContext);\n\t\tif (state == null) {\n\t\t\tthrow new ItemStreamException(\"ItemStream not open or already closed.\");\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tAssert.notNull(executionContext, \"ExecutionContext must not be null\");\n\n\t\tif (saveState) {\n\n\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\texecutionContext.putLong(getExecutionContextKey(RESTART_DATA_NAME), state.position());\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcatch (IOException e) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new ItemStreamException(\"ItemStream does not return current position properly\", e);\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\texecutionContext.putLong(getExecutionContextKey(WRITTEN_STATISTICS_NAME), state.linesWritten);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Returns object representing state.\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\tprotected OutputState getOutputState() {\n\t\tif (state == null) {\n\t\t\tFile file;\n\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\tfile = resource.getFile();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcatch (IOException e) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new ItemStreamException(\"Could not convert resource to file: [\" + resource + \"]\", e);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tAssert.state(!file.exists() || file.canWrite(), \"Resource is not writable: [\" + resource + \"]\");\n\t\t\tstate = new OutputState();\n\t\t\tstate.setDeleteIfExists(shouldDeleteIfExists);\n\t\t\tstate.setAppendAllowed(append);\n\t\t\tstate.setEncoding(encoding);\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn state;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Encapsulates the runtime state of the writer. All state changing operations on the\n\t * writer go through this class.\n\t */\n\tprotected class OutputState {\n\n\t\tprivate @Nullable FileOutputStream os;\n\n\t\t// The bufferedWriter over the file channel that is actually written\n\t\t@Nullable Writer outputBufferedWriter;\n\n\t\t@Nullable FileChannel fileChannel;\n\n\t\t// this represents the charset encoding (if any is needed) for the\n\t\t// output file\n\t\tString encoding = DEFAULT_CHARSET;\n\n\t\tboolean restarted = false;\n\n\t\tlong lastMarkedByteOffsetPosition = 0;\n\n\t\tlong linesWritten = 0;\n\n\t\tboolean shouldDeleteIfExists = true;\n\n\t\tboolean initialized = false;\n\n\t\tprivate boolean append = false;\n\n\t\tprivate boolean appending = false;\n\n\t\t/**\n\t\t * Return the byte offset position of the cursor in the output file as a long\n\t\t * integer.\n\t\t * @return the byte offset position of the cursor in the output file\n\t\t * @throws IOException If unable to get the offset position\n\t\t */\n\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t\tpublic long position() throws IOException {\n\t\t\tif (fileChannel == null) {\n\t\t\t\treturn 0;\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\toutputBufferedWriter.flush();\n\t\t\tlong pos = fileChannel.position();\n\t\t\tif (transactional) {\n\t\t\t\tpos += ((TransactionAwareBufferedWriter) outputBufferedWriter).getBufferSize();\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\treturn pos;\n\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t/**\n\t\t * @param append if true, append to previously created file\n\t\t */\n\t\tpublic void setAppendAllowed(boolean append) {\n\t\t\tthis.append = append;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t/**\n\t\t * @param executionContext state from which to restore writing from\n\t\t */\n\t\tpublic void restoreFrom(ExecutionContext executionContext) {\n\t\t\tlastMarkedByteOffsetPosition = executionContext.getLong(getExecutionContextKey(RESTART_DATA_NAME));\n\t\t\tlinesWritten = executionContext.getLong(getExecutionContextKey(WRITTEN_STATISTICS_NAME));\n\t\t\tif (shouldDeleteIfEmpty && linesWritten == 0) {\n\t\t\t\t// previous execution deleted the output file because no items were\n\t\t\t\t// written\n\t\t\t\trestarted = false;\n\t\t\t\tlastMarkedByteOffsetPosition = 0;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\trestarted = true;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t/**\n\t\t * @param shouldDeleteIfExists indicator\n\t\t */\n\t\tpublic void setDeleteIfExists(boolean shouldDeleteIfExists) {\n\t\t\tthis.shouldDeleteIfExists = shouldDeleteIfExists;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t/**\n\t\t * @param encoding file encoding\n\t\t */\n\t\tpublic void setEncoding(String encoding) {\n\t\t\tthis.encoding = encoding;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tpublic long getLinesWritten() {\n\t\t\treturn linesWritten;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tpublic void setLinesWritten(long linesWritten) {\n\t\t\tthis.linesWritten = linesWritten;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t/**\n\t\t * Close the open resource and reset counters.\n\t\t */\n\t\tpublic void close() {\n\n\t\t\tinitialized = false;\n\t\t\trestarted = false;\n\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\tif (outputBufferedWriter != null) {\n\t\t\t\t\toutputBufferedWriter.close();\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcatch (IOException ioe) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new ItemStreamException(\"Unable to close the ItemWriter\", ioe);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\t\tif (!transactional) {\n\t\t\t\t\tcloseStream();\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tprivate void closeStream() {\n\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\tif (fileChannel != null) {\n\t\t\t\t\tfileChannel.close();\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcatch (IOException ioe) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new ItemStreamException(\"Unable to close the ItemWriter\", ioe);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\t\tif (os != null) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tos.close();\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tcatch (IOException ioe) {\n\t\t\t\t\tthrow new ItemStreamException(\"Unable to close the ItemWriter\", ioe);\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t/**\n\t\t * @param line String to be written to the file\n\t\t * @throws IOException If unable to write the String to the file\n\t\t */\n\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t\tpublic void write(String line) throws IOException {\n\t\t\tif (!initialized) {\n\t\t\t\tinitializeBufferedWriter();\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\toutputBufferedWriter.write(line);\n\t\t\toutputBufferedWriter.flush();\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t/**\n\t\t * Truncate the output at the last known good point.\n\t\t * @throws IOException if unable to work with file\n\t\t */\n\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t\tpublic void truncate() throws IOException {\n\t\t\tfileChannel.truncate(lastMarkedByteOffsetPosition);\n\t\t\tfileChannel.position(lastMarkedByteOffsetPosition);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t/**\n\t\t * Creates the buffered writer for the output file channel based on configuration\n\t\t * information.\n\t\t * @throws IOException if unable to initialize buffer\n\t\t */\n\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t\tprivate void initializeBufferedWriter() throws IOException {\n\n\t\t\tFile file = resource.getFile();\n\t\t\tFileUtils.setUpOutputFile(file, restarted, append, shouldDeleteIfExists);\n\n\t\t\tos = new FileOutputStream(file.getAbsolutePath(), true);\n\t\t\tfileChannel = os.getChannel();\n\n\t\t\toutputBufferedWriter = getBufferedWriter(fileChannel, encoding);\n\t\t\toutputBufferedWriter.flush();\n\n\t\t\tif (append) {\n\t\t\t\t// Bug in IO library? This doesn't work...\n\t\t\t\t// lastMarkedByteOffsetPosition = fileChannel.position();\n\t\t\t\tif (file.length() > 0) {\n\t\t\t\t\tappending = true;\n\t\t\t\t\t// Don't write the headers again\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tAssert.state(outputBufferedWriter != null, \"Unable to initialize buffered writer\");\n\t\t\t// in case of restarting reset position to last committed point\n\t\t\tif (restarted) {\n\t\t\t\tcheckFileSize();\n\t\t\t\ttruncate();\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tinitialized = true;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tpublic boolean isInitialized() {\n\t\t\treturn initialized;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t/**\n\t\t * Returns the buffered writer opened to the beginning of the file specified by\n\t\t * the absolute path name contained in absoluteFileName.\n\t\t */\n\t\tprivate Writer getBufferedWriter(FileChannel fileChannel, String encoding) {\n\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\tfinal FileChannel channel = fileChannel;\n\t\t\t\tif (transactional) {\n\t\t\t\t\tTransactionAwareBufferedWriter writer = new TransactionAwareBufferedWriter(channel,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tthis::closeStream);\n\n\t\t\t\t\twriter.setEncoding(encoding);\n\t\t\t\t\twriter.setForceSync(forceSync);\n\t\t\t\t\treturn writer;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\telse {\n\n\t\t\t\t\treturn new BufferedWriter(Channels.newWriter(fileChannel, encoding)) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\t\t\t\tpublic void flush() throws IOException {\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tsuper.flush();\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tif (forceSync) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tchannel.force(false);\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\t};\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcatch (UnsupportedCharsetException ucse) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new ItemStreamException(\"Bad encoding configuration for output file \" + fileChannel, ucse);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t/**\n\t\t * Checks (on setState) to make sure that the current output file's size is not\n\t\t * smaller than the last saved commit point. If it is, then the file has been\n\t\t * damaged in some way and whole task must be started over again from the\n\t\t * beginning.\n\t\t * @throws IOException if there is an IO problem\n\t\t */\n\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t\tprivate void checkFileSize() throws IOException {\n\t\t\tlong size;\n\n\t\t\toutputBufferedWriter.flush();\n\t\t\tsize = fileChannel.size();\n\n\t\t\tif (size < lastMarkedByteOffsetPosition) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new ItemStreamException(\"Current file size is smaller than size at last commit\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/support/ClassifierCompositeItemWriter.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.support;\n\nimport java.util.LinkedHashMap;\nimport java.util.Map;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.Chunk;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemWriter;\nimport org.springframework.classify.Classifier;\nimport org.springframework.classify.ClassifierSupport;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\n\n/**\n * Calls one of a collection of ItemWriters for each item, based on a router pattern\n * implemented through the provided {@link Classifier}.\n * <p>\n * The implementation is thread-safe if all delegates are thread-safe.\n *\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Glenn Renfro\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @since 2.0\n */\npublic class ClassifierCompositeItemWriter<T> implements ItemWriter<T> {\n\n\tprivate Classifier<T, ItemWriter<? super T>> classifier = new ClassifierSupport<>(null);\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param classifier the classifier to set\n\t */\n\tpublic void setClassifier(Classifier<T, ItemWriter<? super T>> classifier) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(classifier, \"A classifier is required.\");\n\t\tthis.classifier = classifier;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Delegates to injected {@link ItemWriter} instances according to their\n\t * classification by the {@link Classifier}.\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void write(Chunk<? extends T> items) throws Exception {\n\n\t\tMap<ItemWriter<? super T>, Chunk<T>> map = new LinkedHashMap<>();\n\n\t\tfor (T item : items) {\n\t\t\tItemWriter<? super T> key = classifier.classify(item);\n\t\t\tif (!map.containsKey(key)) {\n\t\t\t\tmap.put(key, new Chunk<>());\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tmap.get(key).add(item);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tfor (ItemWriter<? super T> writer : map.keySet()) {\n\t\t\twriter.write(map.get(writer));\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },
