      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/support/ListItemReader.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.support;\n\nimport java.util.LinkedList;\nimport java.util.List;\n\nimport org.springframework.aop.support.AopUtils;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemReader;\n\n/**\n * An {@link ItemReader} that pulls data from a list. Useful for testing.\n *\n * <p>\n * This reader is <b>not</b> thread-safe.\n * </p>\n *\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author jojoldu\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n *\n */\npublic class ListItemReader<T> implements ItemReader<T> {\n\n\tprivate final List<T> list;\n\n\tpublic ListItemReader(List<T> list) {\n\t\t// If it is a proxy we assume it knows how to deal with its own state.\n\t\t// (It's probably transaction aware.)\n\t\tif (AopUtils.isAopProxy(list)) {\n\t\t\tthis.list = list;\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\tthis.list = new LinkedList<>(list);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic @Nullable T read() {\n\t\tif (!list.isEmpty()) {\n\t\t\treturn list.remove(0);\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn null;\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/util/ExecutionContextUserSupport.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.util;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ExecutionContext;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\n\n/**\n * Facilitates assigning names to objects persisting data in {@link ExecutionContext} and\n * generating keys for {@link ExecutionContext} based on the name.\n *\n * @author Robert Kasanicky\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Stefano Cordio\n */\npublic class ExecutionContextUserSupport {\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String name;\n\n\tpublic ExecutionContextUserSupport() {\n\t\tsuper();\n\t}\n\n\tpublic ExecutionContextUserSupport(String name) {\n\t\tsuper();\n\t\tthis.name = name;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return name used to uniquely identify this instance's entries in shared context.\n\t */\n\tpublic @Nullable String getName() {\n\t\treturn this.name;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param name unique name used to create execution context keys.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setName(String name) {\n\t\tthis.name = name;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Prefix the argument with {@link #getName()} to create a unique key that can be\n\t * safely used to identify data stored in {@link ExecutionContext}.\n\t * @param suffix {@link String} to be used to generate the key.\n\t * @return the key that was generated based on the name and the suffix.\n\t */\n\tpublic String getKey(String suffix) {\n\t\tAssert.hasText(name, \"Name must be assigned for the sake of defining the execution context keys prefix.\");\n\t\treturn name + \".\" + suffix;\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/xml/StaxEventItemReader.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.xml;\n\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.io.InputStream;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.NoSuchElementException;\n\nimport javax.xml.namespace.QName;\nimport javax.xml.stream.XMLEventReader;\nimport javax.xml.stream.XMLInputFactory;\nimport javax.xml.stream.XMLStreamException;\nimport javax.xml.stream.events.EndElement;\nimport javax.xml.stream.events.StartElement;\nimport javax.xml.stream.events.XMLEvent;\n\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.Log;\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.LogFactory;\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ExecutionContext;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.NonTransientResourceException;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file.ResourceAwareItemReaderItemStream;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.support.AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.xml.stax.DefaultFragmentEventReader;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.xml.stax.FragmentEventReader;\nimport org.springframework.beans.factory.InitializingBean;\nimport org.springframework.core.io.Resource;\nimport org.springframework.oxm.Unmarshaller;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\nimport org.springframework.util.StringUtils;\nimport org.springframework.util.xml.StaxUtils;\n\n/**\n * Item reader for reading XML input based on StAX.\n * <p>\n * It extracts fragments from the input XML document which correspond to records for\n * processing. The fragments are wrapped with StartDocument and EndDocument events so that\n * the fragments can be further processed like standalone XML documents.\n * <p>\n * The implementation is <b>not</b> thread-safe.\n *\n * @author Robert Kasanicky\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Glenn Renfro\n * @author Jimmy Praet\n */\npublic class StaxEventItemReader<T> extends AbstractItemCountingItemStreamItemReader<T>\n\t\timplements ResourceAwareItemReaderItemStream<T>, InitializingBean {\n\n\tprivate static final Log logger = LogFactory.getLog(StaxEventItemReader.class);\n\n\tpublic static final String DEFAULT_ENCODING = StandardCharsets.UTF_8.name();\n\n\tprivate @Nullable FragmentEventReader fragmentReader;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable XMLEventReader eventReader;\n\n\tprivate Unmarshaller unmarshaller;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable Resource resource;\n\n\tprivate @Nullable InputStream inputStream;\n\n\tprivate final List<QName> fragmentRootElementNames = new ArrayList<>();\n\n\tprivate boolean noInput;\n\n\tprivate boolean strict = true;\n\n\tprivate XMLInputFactory xmlInputFactory = StaxUtils.createDefensiveInputFactory();\n\n\tprivate @Nullable String encoding = DEFAULT_ENCODING;\n\n\t/**\n\t * Create a new {@link StaxEventItemReader} instance. The {@link Unmarshaller} must be\n\t * provided to map XML fragments to objects.\n\t * @param unmarshaller maps xml fragments corresponding to records to objects\n\t * @since 6.0\n\t */\n\tpublic StaxEventItemReader(Unmarshaller unmarshaller) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(unmarshaller, \"The Unmarshaller must not be null.\");\n\t\tthis.unmarshaller = unmarshaller;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * In strict mode the reader will throw an exception on\n\t * {@link #open(ExecutionContext)} if the input resource does not exist.\n\t * @param strict true by default\n\t */\n\tpublic void setStrict(boolean strict) {\n\t\tthis.strict = strict;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void setResource(Resource resource) {\n\t\tthis.resource = resource;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param unmarshaller maps xml fragments corresponding to records to objects\n\t */\n\tpublic void setUnmarshaller(Unmarshaller unmarshaller) {\n\t\tthis.unmarshaller = unmarshaller;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param fragmentRootElementName the name of the fragment's root element\n\t */\n\tpublic void setFragmentRootElementName(String fragmentRootElementName) {\n\t\tsetFragmentRootElementNames(new String[] { fragmentRootElementName });\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param fragmentRootElementNames the names of the fragment's root element\n\t */\n\tpublic void setFragmentRootElementNames(String[] fragmentRootElementNames) {\n\t\tif (!this.fragmentRootElementNames.isEmpty()) {\n\t\t\tthis.fragmentRootElementNames.clear();\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor (String fragmentRootElementName : fragmentRootElementNames) {\n\t\t\tthis.fragmentRootElementNames.add(parseFragmentRootElementName(fragmentRootElementName));\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Set the {@link XMLInputFactory}.\n\t * @param xmlInputFactory to use\n\t */\n\tpublic void setXmlInputFactory(XMLInputFactory xmlInputFactory) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(xmlInputFactory, \"XMLInputFactory must not be null\");\n\t\tthis.xmlInputFactory = xmlInputFactory;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Set encoding to be used for the input file. Defaults to {@link #DEFAULT_ENCODING}.\n\t * @param encoding the encoding to be used. Can be {@code null}, in which case, the\n\t * XML event reader will attempt to auto-detect the encoding from tht input file.\n\t */\n\tpublic void setEncoding(String encoding) {\n\t\tthis.encoding = encoding;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Ensure that all required dependencies for the ItemReader to run are provided after\n\t * all properties have been set.\n\t *\n\t * @see InitializingBean#afterPropertiesSet()\n\t * @throws IllegalArgumentException if the Resource, FragmentDeserializer or\n\t * FragmentRootElementName is null, or if the root element is empty.\n\t * @throws IllegalStateException if the Resource does not exist.\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void afterPropertiesSet() throws Exception {\n\t\tAssert.state(!fragmentRootElementNames.isEmpty(), \"The FragmentRootElementNames must not be empty\");\n\t\tfor (QName fragmentRootElementName : fragmentRootElementNames) {\n\t\t\tAssert.state(StringUtils.hasText(fragmentRootElementName.getLocalPart()),\n\t\t\t\t\t\"The FragmentRootElementNames must not contain empty elements\");\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Responsible for moving the cursor before the StartElement of the fragment root.\n\t * <p>\n\t * This implementation simply looks for the next corresponding element, it does not\n\t * care about element nesting. You will need to override this method to correctly\n\t * handle composite fragments.\n\t * @param reader the {@link XMLEventReader} to be used to find next fragment.\n\t * @return <code>true</code> if next fragment was found, <code>false</code> otherwise.\n\t * @throws NonTransientResourceException if the cursor could not be moved. This will\n\t * be treated as fatal and subsequent calls to read will return null.\n\t */\n\tprotected boolean moveCursorToNextFragment(XMLEventReader reader) throws NonTransientResourceException {\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\twhile (true) {\n\t\t\t\twhile (reader.peek() != null && !reader.peek().isStartElement()) {\n\t\t\t\t\treader.nextEvent();\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif (reader.peek() == null) {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tQName startElementName = ((StartElement) reader.peek()).getName();\n\t\t\t\tif (isFragmentRootElementName(startElementName)) {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treader.nextEvent();\n\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (XMLStreamException e) {\n\t\t\tthrow new NonTransientResourceException(\"Error while reading from event reader\", e);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void doClose() throws Exception {\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tif (fragmentReader != null) {\n\t\t\t\tfragmentReader.close();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif (inputStream != null) {\n\t\t\t\tinputStream.close();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\tfragmentReader = null;\n\t\t\tinputStream = null;\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void doOpen() throws Exception {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(resource, \"The Resource must not be null.\");\n\n\t\tnoInput = true;\n\t\tif (!resource.exists()) {\n\t\t\tif (strict) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new IllegalStateException(\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\"Input resource \" + resource.getURL() + \" must exist (reader is in 'strict' mode)\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tlogger.warn(\"Input resource does not exist \" + resource.getDescription());\n\t\t\treturn;\n\t\t}\n\t\tif (!resource.isReadable()) {\n\t\t\tif (strict) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new IllegalStateException(\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\"Input resource  \" + resource.getURL() + \" must be readable (reader is in 'strict' mode)\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tlogger.warn(\"Input resource is not readable \" + resource.getDescription());\n\t\t\treturn;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tinputStream = resource.getInputStream();\n\t\teventReader = this.encoding != null ? xmlInputFactory.createXMLEventReader(inputStream, this.encoding)\n\t\t\t\t: xmlInputFactory.createXMLEventReader(inputStream);\n\t\tfragmentReader = new DefaultFragmentEventReader(eventReader);\n\t\tnoInput = false;\n\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Move to next fragment and map it to item.\n\t */\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tprotected @Nullable T doRead() throws IOException, XMLStreamException {\n\n\t\tif (noInput) {\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tT item = null;\n\n\t\tboolean success;\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tsuccess = moveCursorToNextFragment(fragmentReader);\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (NonTransientResourceException e) {\n\t\t\t// Prevent caller from retrying indefinitely since this is fatal\n\t\t\tnoInput = true;\n\t\t\tthrow e;\n\t\t}\n\t\tif (success) {\n\t\t\tfragmentReader.markStartFragment();\n\n\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"unchecked\")\n\t\t\t\tT mappedFragment = (T) unmarshaller.unmarshal(StaxUtils.createStaxSource(fragmentReader));\n\t\t\t\titem = mappedFragment;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\t\tfragmentReader.markFragmentProcessed();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn item;\n\t}\n\n\t/*\n\t * jumpToItem is overridden because reading in and attempting to bind an entire\n\t * fragment is unacceptable in a restart scenario, and may cause exceptions to be\n\t * thrown that were already skipped in previous runs.\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tprotected void jumpToItem(int itemIndex) throws Exception {\n\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < itemIndex; i++) {\n\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\tQName fragmentName = readToStartFragment();\n\t\t\t\treadToEndFragment(fragmentName);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcatch (NoSuchElementException e) {\n\t\t\t\tif (itemIndex == (i + 1)) {\n\t\t\t\t\t// we can presume a NoSuchElementException on the last item means the\n\t\t\t\t\t// EOF was reached on the last run\n\t\t\t\t\treturn;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\t\t// if NoSuchElementException occurs on an item other than the last\n\t\t\t\t\t// one, this indicates a problem\n\t\t\t\t\tthrow e;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/*\n\t * Read until the first StartElement tag that matches any of the provided\n\t * fragmentRootElementNames. Because there may be any number of tags in between where\n\t * the reader is now and the fragment start, this is done in a loop until the element\n\t * type and name match.\n\t */\n\tprivate QName readToStartFragment() throws XMLStreamException {\n\t\twhile (true) {\n\t\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t\t\tXMLEvent nextEvent = eventReader.nextEvent();\n\t\t\tif (nextEvent.isStartElement() && isFragmentRootElementName(((StartElement) nextEvent).getName())) {\n\t\t\t\treturn ((StartElement) nextEvent).getName();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/*\n\t * Read until the first EndElement tag that matches the provided\n\t * fragmentRootElementName. Because there may be any number of tags in between where\n\t * the reader is now and the fragment end tag, this is done in a loop until the\n\t * element type and name match\n\t */\n\tprivate void readToEndFragment(QName fragmentRootElementName) throws XMLStreamException {\n\t\twhile (true) {\n\t\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t\t\tXMLEvent nextEvent = eventReader.nextEvent();\n\t\t\tif (nextEvent.isEndElement() && fragmentRootElementName.equals(((EndElement) nextEvent).getName())) {\n\t\t\t\treturn;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tprotected boolean isFragmentRootElementName(QName name) {\n\t\tfor (QName fragmentRootElementName : fragmentRootElementNames) {\n\t\t\tif (fragmentRootElementName.getLocalPart().equals(name.getLocalPart())) {\n\t\t\t\tif (!StringUtils.hasText(fragmentRootElementName.getNamespaceURI())\n\t\t\t\t\t\t|| fragmentRootElementName.getNamespaceURI().equals(name.getNamespaceURI())) {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn false;\n\t}\n\n\tprivate QName parseFragmentRootElementName(String fragmentRootElementName) {\n\t\tString name = fragmentRootElementName;\n\t\tString nameSpace = null;\n\t\tif (fragmentRootElementName.contains(\"{\")) {\n\t\t\tnameSpace = fragmentRootElementName.replaceAll(\"\\\\{(.*)\\\\}.*\", \"$1\");\n\t\t\tname = fragmentRootElementName.replaceAll(\"\\\\{.*\\\\}(.*)\", \"$1\");\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn new QName(nameSpace, name, \"\");\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/xml/StaxEventItemWriter.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.xml;\n\nimport java.io.BufferedWriter;\nimport java.io.File;\nimport {\n\t\t\t\tXMLEventWriter footerCallbackWriter = delegateEventWriter;\n\t\t\t\tif (restarted && !unclosedHeaderCallbackElements.isEmpty()) {\n\t\t\t\t\tfooterCallbackWriter = new UnopenedElementClosingEventWriter(delegateEventWriter, bufferedWriter,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tunclosedHeaderCallbackElements);\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tfooterCallback.write(footerCallbackWriter);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tdelegateEventWriter.flush();\n\t\t\tendDocument(delegateEventWriter);\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (IOException e) {\n\t\t\tthrow new ItemStreamException(\"Failed to write footer items\", e);\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (XMLStreamException e) {\n\t\t\tthrow new ItemStreamException(\"Failed to write end document tag\", e);\n\t\t}\n\t\tfinally {\n\n\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\tdelegateEventWriter.close();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcatch (XMLStreamException e) {\n\t\t\t\tlog.error(\"Unable to close file resource: [\" + resource + \"] \" + e);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\t\tbufferedWriter.close();\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tcatch (IOException e) {\n\t\t\t\t\tlog.error(\"Unable to close file resource: [\" + resource + \"] \" + e);\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tfinally {\n\t\t\t\t\tif (!transactional) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tcloseStream();\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif (currentRecordCount == 0 && shouldDeleteIfEmpty) {\n\t\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\t\tFiles.delete(resource.getFile().toPath());\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tcatch (IOException | SecurityException e) {\n\t\t\t\t\tthrow new ItemStreamException(\"Failed to delete empty file on close\", e);\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tthis.initialized = false;\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\tprivate void closeStream() {\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tchannel.close();\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (IOException ioe) {\n\t\t\tlog.error(\"Unable to close file resource: [\" + resource + \"] \" + ioe);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Write the value objects and flush them to the file.\n\t * @param items the value object\n\t * @throws IOException thrown if general error occurs.\n\t * @throws XmlMappingException thrown if error occurs during XML Mapping.\n\t */\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void write(Chunk<? extends T> items) throws XmlMappingException, IOException {\n\n\t\tif (!this.initialized) {\n\t\t\tthrow new WriterNotOpenException(\"Writer must be open before it can be written to\");\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tcurrentRecordCount += items.size();\n\n\t\tfor (Object object : items) {\n\t\t\tAssert.state(marshaller.supports(object.getClass()),\n\t\t\t\t\t\"Marshaller must support the class of the marshalled object\");\n\t\t\tResult result = createStaxResult();\n\t\t\tmarshaller.marshal(object, result);\n\t\t}\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\teventWriter.flush();\n\t\t\tif (forceSync) {\n\t\t\t\tchannel.force(false);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (XMLStreamException | IOException e) {\n\t\t\tthrow new WriteFailedException(\"Failed to flush the events\", e);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Get the restart data.\n\t * @param executionContext the batch context.\n\t *\n\t * @see ItemStream#update(ExecutionContext)\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void update(ExecutionContext executionContext) {\n\t\tsuper.update(executionContext);\n\t\tif (saveState) {\n\t\t\tAssert.notNull(executionContext, \"ExecutionContext must not be null\");\n\t\t\texecutionContext.putLong(getExecutionContextKey(RESTART_DATA_NAME), getPosition());\n\t\t\texecutionContext.putLong(getExecutionContextKey(WRITE_STATISTICS_NAME), currentRecordCount);\n\t\t\tif (!unclosedHeaderCallbackElements.isEmpty()) {\n\t\t\t\texecutionContext.put(getExecutionContextKey(UNCLOSED_HEADER_CALLBACK_ELEMENTS_NAME),\n\t\t\t\t\t\tunclosedHeaderCallbackElements);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/*\n\t * Get the actual position in file channel. This method flushes any buffered data\n\t * before position is read.\n\t *\n\t * @return byte offset in file channel\n\t */\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\tprivate long getPosition() {\n\t\tlong position;\n\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\teventWriter.flush();\n\t\t\tposition = channel.position();\n\t\t\tif (bufferedWriter instanceof TransactionAwareBufferedWriter transactionAwareBufferedWriter) {\n\t\t\t\tposition += transactionAwareBufferedWriter.getBufferSize();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (Exception e) {\n\t\t\tthrow new ItemStreamException(\"Unable to write to file resource: [\" + resource + \"]\", e);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn position;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Set the file channel position.\n\t * @param newPosition new file channel position\n\t */\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\tprivate void setPosition(long newPosition) {\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tchannel.truncate(newPosition);\n\t\t\tchannel.position(newPosition);\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (IOException e) {\n\t\t\tthrow new ItemStreamException(\"Unable to write to file resource: [\" + resource + \"]\", e);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/xml/stax/UnclosedElementCollectingEventWriter.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2014-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.xml.stax;\n\nimport java.util.LinkedList;\nimport java.util.List;\n\nimport javax.xml.namespace.QName;\nimport javax.xml.stream.XMLEventWriter;\nimport javax.xml.stream.XMLStreamException;\nimport javax.xml.stream.events.XMLEvent;\n\n/**\n * Delegating XMLEventWriter, which collects the QNames of elements that were opened but\n * not closed.\n *\n * @author Jimmy Praet\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @since 3.0\n */\npublic class UnclosedElementCollectingEventWriter extends AbstractEventWriterWrapper {\n\n\tprivate final LinkedList<QName> unclosedElements = new LinkedList<>();\n\n\tpublic UnclosedElementCollectingEventWriter(XMLEventWriter wrappedEventWriter) {\n\t\tsuper(wrappedEventWriter);\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void add(XMLEvent event) throws XMLStreamException {\n\t\tif (event.isStartElement()) {\n\t\t\tunclosedElements.addLast(event.asStartElement().getName());\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (event.isEndElement()) {\n\t\t\tunclosedElements.removeLast();\n\t\t}\n\t\tsuper.add(event);\n\t}\n\n\tpublic List<QName> getUnclosedElements() {\n\t\treturn unclosedElements;\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/xml/stax/UnopenedElementClosingEventWriter.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2014-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.xml.stax;\n\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.io.Writer;\nimport java.util.LinkedList;\nimport java.util.List;\n\nimport javax.xml.namespace.QName;\nimport javax.xml.stream.XMLEventWriter;\nimport javax.xml.stream.XMLStreamException;\nimport javax.xml.stream.events.XMLEvent;\n\nimport org.springframework.util.StringUtils;\n\n/**\n * Delegating XMLEventWriter, which writes EndElement events that match a given collection\n * of QNames directly to the underlying java.io.Writer instead of to the delegate\n * XMLEventWriter.\n *\n * @author Jimmy Praet\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @since 3.0\n */\npublic class UnopenedElementClosingEventWriter extends AbstractEventWriterWrapper {\n\n\tprivate final LinkedList<QName> unopenedElements;\n\n\tprivate final Writer ioWriter;\n\n\tpublic UnopenedElementClosingEventWriter(XMLEventWriter wrappedEventWriter, Writer ioWriter,\n\t\t\tList<QName> unopenedElements) {\n\t\tsuper(wrappedEventWriter);\n\t\tthis.unopenedElements = new LinkedList<>(unopenedElements);\n\t\tthis.ioWriter = ioWriter;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void add(XMLEvent event) throws XMLStreamException {\n\t\tif (isUnopenedElementCloseEvent(event)) {\n\t\t\tQName element = unopenedElements.removeLast();\n\t\t\tString nsPrefix = !StringUtils.hasText(element.getPrefix()) ? \"\" : element.getPrefix() + \":\";\n\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\tsuper.flush();\n\t\t\t\tioWriter.write(\"</\" + nsPrefix + element.getLocalPart() + \">\");\n\t\t\t\tioWriter.flush();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcatch (IOException ioe) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new XMLStreamException(\"Unable to close tag: \" + element, ioe);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\tsuper.add(event);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tprivate boolean isUnopenedElementCloseEvent(XMLEvent event) {\n\t\tif (unopenedElements.isEmpty()) {\n\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (!event.isEndElement()) {\n\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\treturn unopenedElements.getLast().equals(event.asEndElement().getName());\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/poller/DirectPoller.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.poller;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport java.util.concurrent.Callable;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ExecutionException;\nimport java.util.concurrent.Future;\nimport java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;\nimport java.util.concurrent.TimeoutException;\n\n/**\n * A {@link Poller} that uses the callers thread to poll for a result as soon as it is\n * asked for. This is often appropriate if you expect a result relatively quickly, or if\n * there is only one such result expected (otherwise, it is more efficient to use a\n * background thread to do the polling).\n *\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @param <S> the type of the result\n */\npublic class DirectPoller<S> implements Poller<S> {\n\n\tprivate final long interval;\n\n\tpublic DirectPoller(long interval) {\n\t\tthis.interval = interval;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Get a future for a non-null result from the callback. Only when the result is asked\n\t * for (using {@link Future#get()} or {@link Future#get(long, TimeUnit)} will the\n\t * polling actually start.\n\t *\n\t * @see Poller#poll(Callable)\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic Future<S> poll(Callable<S> callable) throws Exception {\n\t\treturn new DirectPollingFuture<>(interval, callable);\n\t}\n\n\tprivate static class DirectPollingFuture<S> implements Future<S> {\n\n\t\tprivate final long startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();\n\n\t\tprivate volatile boolean cancelled;\n\n\t\tprivate volatile @Nullable S result = null;\n\n\t\tprivate final long interval;\n\n\t\tprivate final Callable<S> callable;\n\n\t\tpublic DirectPollingFuture(long interval, Callable<S> callable) {\n\t\t\tthis.interval = interval;\n\t\t\tthis.callable = callable;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t@Override\n\t\tpublic boolean cancel(boolean mayInterruptIfRunning) {\n\t\t\tcancelled = true;\n\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t@Override\n\t\tpublic S get() throws InterruptedException, ExecutionException {\n\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\treturn get(-1, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcatch (TimeoutException e) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new IllegalStateException(\"Unexpected timeout waiting for result\", e);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t@Override\n\t\tpublic S get(long timeout, TimeUnit unit) throws InterruptedException, ExecutionException, TimeoutException {\n\n\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\tresult = callable.call();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcatch (Exception e) {\n\t\t\t\tthrow new ExecutionException(e);\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tlong nextExecutionTime = startTime + interval;\n\t\t\tlong currentTimeMillis = System.currentTimeMillis();\n\t\t\tlong timeoutMillis = TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.convert(timeout, unit);\n\n\t\t\twhile (result == null && !cancelled) {\n\n\t\t\t\tlong delta = nextExecutionTime - startTime;\n\t\t\t\tif (delta >= timeoutMillis && timeoutMillis > 0) {\n\t\t\t\t\tthrow new TimeoutException(\"Timed out waiting for task to return non-null result\");\n\t\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t\tif (nextExecutionTime > currentTimeMillis) {\n\t\t\t\t\tThread.sleep(nextExecutionTime - currentTimeMillis);\n\t\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t\tcurrentTimeMillis = System.currentTimeMillis();\n\t\t\t\tnextExecutionTime = currentTimeMillis + interval;\n\n\t\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\t\tresult = callable.call();\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tcatch (Exception e) {\n\t\t\t\t\tthrow new ExecutionException(e);\n\t\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\treturn result;\n\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t@Override\n\t\tpublic boolean isCancelled() {\n\t\t\treturn cancelled;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t@Override\n\t\tpublic boolean isDone() {\n\t\t\treturn cancelled || result != null;\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/repeat/context/RepeatContextCounter.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.context;\n\nimport java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.RepeatContext;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\n\n/**\n * Helper class for policies that need to count the number of occurrences of some event\n * (e.g. an exception type in the context) in the scope of a batch. The value of the\n * counter can be stored between batches in a nested context, so that the termination\n * decision is based on the aggregate of a number of sibling batches.\n *\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Stefano Cordio\n */\npublic class RepeatContextCounter {\n\n\tprivate final String countKey;\n\n\tprivate final RepeatContext context;\n\n\t/**\n\t * Convenience constructor with useParent=false.\n\t * @param context the current context.\n\t * @param countKey the key to use to store the counter in the context.\n\t */\n\tpublic RepeatContextCounter(RepeatContext context, String countKey) {\n\t\tthis(context, countKey, false);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Construct a new {@link RepeatContextCounter}.\n\t * @param context the current context.\n\t * @param countKey the key to use to store the counter in the context.\n\t * @param useParent true if the counter is to be shared between siblings. The state\n\t * will be stored in the parent of the context (if it exists) instead of the context\n\t * itself.\n\t */\n\tpublic RepeatContextCounter(RepeatContext context, String countKey, boolean useParent) {\n\t\tAssert.notNull(context, \"The context must be provided to initialize a counter\");\n\n\t\tthis.countKey = countKey;\n\n\t\tRepeatContext parent = context.getParent();\n\n\t\tthis.context = useParent && parent != null ? parent : context;\n\t\tif (!this.context.hasAttribute(countKey)) {\n\t\t\tthis.context.setAttribute(countKey, new AtomicInteger());\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * @return the current value of the counter\n\t */\n\tpublic int getCount() {\n\t\treturn getCounter().intValue();\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Increment the counter.\n\t * @param delta the amount by which to increment the counter.\n\t */\n\tpublic final void increment(int delta) {\n\t\tgetCounter().addAndGet(delta);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Increment by 1.\n\t */\n\tpublic final void increment() {\n\t\tincrement(1);\n\t}\n\n\t@SuppressWarnings(\"DataFlowIssue\")\n\tprivate AtomicInteger getCounter() {\n\t\treturn ((AtomicInteger) context.getAttribute(countKey));\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/repeat/context/SynchronizedAttributeAccessor.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.context;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport org.springframework.core.AttributeAccessor;\nimport org.springframework.core.AttributeAccessorSupport;\n\n/**\n * An {@link AttributeAccessor} that synchronizes on a mutex (not this) before modifying\n * or accessing the underlying attributes.\n *\n * @author Dave Syer\n *\n */\npublic class SynchronizedAttributeAccessor implements AttributeAccessor {\n\n\t/**\n\t * All methods are delegated to this support object.\n\t */\n\tAttributeAccessorSupport support = new AttributeAccessorSupport() {\n\t\t/**\n\t\t * Generated serial UID.\n\t\t */\n\t\tprivate static final long serialVersionUID = -7664290016506582290L;\n\n\t};\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic String[] attributeNames() {\n\t\tsynchronized (support) {\n\t\t\treturn support.attributeNames();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic boolean equals(Object other) {\n\t\tif (this == other) {\n\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t}\n\t\tAttributeAccessorSupport that;\n\t\tif (other instanceof SynchronizedAttributeAccessor synchronizedAttributeAccessor) {\n\t\t\tthat = synchronizedAttributeAccessor.support;\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if (other instanceof AttributeAccessorSupport attributeAccessorSupport) {\n\t\t\tthat = attributeAccessorSupport;\n\t\t}\n\t\telse {\n\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t}\n\t\tsynchronized (support) {\n\t\t\treturn support.equals(that);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic @Nullable Object getAttribute(String name) {\n\t\tsynchronized (support) {\n\t\t\treturn support.getAttribute(name);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic boolean hasAttribute(String name) {\n\t\tsynchronized (support) {\n\t\t\treturn support.hasAttribute(name);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic int hashCode() {\n\t\treturn support.hashCode();\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic @Nullable Object removeAttribute(String name) {\n\t\tsynchronized (support) {\n\t\t\treturn support.removeAttribute(name);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void setAttribute(String name, @Nullable Object value) {\n\t\tsynchronized (support) {\n\t\t\tsupport.setAttribute(name, value);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Additional support for atomic put if absent.\n\t * @param name the key for the attribute name\n\t * @param value the value of the attribute\n\t * @return null if the attribute was not already set, the existing value otherwise.\n\t */\n\tpublic @Nullable Object setAttributeIfAbsent(String name, Object value) {\n\t\tsynchronized (support) {\n\t\t\tObject old = getAttribute(name);\n\t\t\tif (old != null) {\n\t\t\t\treturn old;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tsetAttribute(name, value);\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn null;\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic String toString() {\n\t\tStringBuilder buffer = new StringBuilder(\"SynchronizedAttributeAccessor: [\");\n\t\tsynchronized (support) {\n\t\t\tString[] names = attributeNames();\n\t\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {\n\t\t\t\tString name = names[i];\n\t\t\t\tbuffer.append(names[i]).append(\"=\").append(getAttribute(name));\n\t\t\t\tif (i < names.length - 1) {\n\t\t\t\t\tbuffer.append(\", \");\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tbuffer.append(\"]\");\n\t\t\treturn buffer.toString();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },
