      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/repeat/support/ResultHolderResultQueue.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2002-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.support;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.RepeatStatus;\n\nimport java.util.Comparator;\nimport java.util.NoSuchElementException;\nimport java.util.concurrent.BlockingQueue;\nimport java.util.concurrent.PriorityBlockingQueue;\nimport java.util.concurrent.Semaphore;\n\n/**\n * An implementation of the {@link ResultQueue} that throttles the number of expected\n * results, limiting it to a maximum at any given time.\n *\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @deprecated since 5.0 with no replacement. Scheduled for removal in 7.0.\n */\n@SuppressWarnings(\"removal\")\n@Deprecated(since = \"5.0\", forRemoval = true)\npublic class ResultHolderResultQueue implements ResultQueue<ResultHolder> {\n\n\t// Accumulation of result objects as they finish.\n\tprivate final BlockingQueue<ResultHolder> results;\n\n\t// Accumulation of dummy objects flagging expected results in the future.\n\tprivate final Semaphore waits;\n\n\tprivate final Object lock = new Object();\n\n\tprivate volatile int count = 0;\n\n\t/**\n\t * @param throttleLimit the maximum number of results that can be expected at any\n\t * given time.\n\t */\n\tpublic ResultHolderResultQueue(int throttleLimit) {\n\t\tresults = new PriorityBlockingQueue<>(throttleLimit, new ResultHolderComparator());\n\t\twaits = new Semaphore(throttleLimit);\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic boolean isEmpty() {\n\t\treturn results.isEmpty();\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic boolean isExpecting() {\n\t\t// Base the decision about whether we expect more results on a\n\t\t// counter of the number of expected results actually collected.\n\t\t// Do not synchronize! Otherwise put and expect can deadlock.\n\t\treturn count > 0;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Tell the queue to expect one more result. Blocks until a new result is available if\n\t * already expecting too many (as determined by the throttle limit).\n\t *\n\t * @see ResultQueue#expect()\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void expect() throws InterruptedException {\n\t\twaits.acquire();\n\t\t// Don't acquire the lock in a synchronized block - might deadlock\n\t\tsynchronized (lock) {\n\t\t\tcount++;\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic void put(ResultHolder holder) throws IllegalArgumentException {\n\t\tif (!isExpecting()) {\n\t\t\tthrow new IllegalArgumentException(\"Not expecting a result.  Call expect() before put().\");\n\t\t}\n\t\tresults.add(holder);\n\t\t// Take from the waits queue now to allow another result to\n\t\t// accumulate. But don't decrement the counter.\n\t\twaits.release();\n\t\tsynchronized (lock) {\n\t\t\tlock.notifyAll();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Get the next result as soon as it becomes available. <br>\n\t * <br>\n\t * Release result immediately if:\n\t * <ul>\n\t * <li>There is a result that is continuable.</li>\n\t * </ul>\n\t * Otherwise block if either:\n\t * <ul>\n\t * <li>There is no result (as per contract of {@link ResultQueue}).</li>\n\t * <li>The number of results is less than the number expected.</li>\n\t * </ul>\n\t * Error if either:\n\t * <ul>\n\t * <li>Not expecting.</li>\n\t * <li>Interrupted.</li>\n\t * </ul>\n\t *\n\t * @see ResultQueue#take()\n\t */\n\t@Override\n\tpublic ResultHolder take() throws NoSuchElementException, InterruptedException {\n\t\tif (!isExpecting()) {\n\t\t\tthrow new NoSuchElementException(\"Not expecting a result.  Call expect() before take().\");\n\t\t}\n\t\tResultHolder value;\n\t\tsynchronized (lock) {\n\t\t\tvalue = results.take();\n\t\t\tif (isContinuable(value)) {\n\t\t\t\t// Decrement the counter only when the result is collected.\n\t\t\t\tcount--;\n\t\t\t\treturn value;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tresults.put(value);\n\t\tsynchronized (lock) {\n\t\t\twhile (count > results.size()) {\n\t\t\t\tlock.wait();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tvalue = results.take();\n\t\t\tcount--;\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn value;\n\t}\n\n\tprivate boolean isContinuable(ResultHolder value) {\n\t\treturn value.getResult() != null && value.getResult().isContinuable();\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Compares ResultHolders so that one that is continuable ranks lowest.\n\t *\n\t * @author Dave Syer\n\t *\n\t */\n\tprivate static class ResultHolderComparator implements Comparator<ResultHolder> {\n\n\t\t@Override\n\t\tpublic int compare(ResultHolder h1, ResultHolder h2) {\n\t\t\tRepeatStatus result1 = h1.getResult();\n\t\t\tRepeatStatus result2 = h2.getResult();\n\t\t\tif (result1 == null && result2 == null) {\n\t\t\t\treturn 0;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif (result1 == null) {\n\t\t\t\treturn -1;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\telse if (result2 == null) {\n\t\t\t\treturn 1;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif ((result1.isContinuable() && result2.isContinuable())\n\t\t\t\t\t|| (!result1.isContinuable() && !result2.isContinuable())) {\n\t\t\t\treturn 0;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif (result1.isContinuable()) {\n\t\t\t\treturn -1;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn 1;\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/main/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/support/PatternMatcher.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2021 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Comparator;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Map;\n\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\n\n/**\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Dan Garrette\n * @author Marten Deinum\n */\npublic class PatternMatcher<S> {\n\n\tprivate final Map<String, S> map;\n\n\tprivate final List<String> sorted;\n\n\t/**\n\t * Initialize a new {@link PatternMatcher} with a map of patterns to values\n\t * @param map a map from String patterns to values\n\t */\n\tpublic PatternMatcher(Map<String, S> map) {\n\t\tsuper();\n\t\tthis.map = map;\n\t\t// Sort keys to start with the most specific\n\t\tsorted = new ArrayList<>(map.keySet());\n\t\tsorted.sort(Comparator.reverseOrder());\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Lifted from AntPathMatcher in Spring Core. Tests whether a string matches against a\n\t * pattern. The pattern may contain two special characters:<br>\n\t * '*' means zero or more characters<br>\n\t * '?' means one and only one character\n\t * @param pattern pattern to match against. Must not be <code>null</code>.\n\t * @param str string which must be matched against the pattern. Must not be\n\t * <code>null</code>.\n\t * @return <code>true</code> if the string matches against the pattern, or\n\t * <code>false</code> otherwise.\n\t */\n\tpublic static boolean match(String pattern, String str) {\n\t\tint patIdxStart = 0;\n\t\tint patIdxEnd = pattern.length() - 1;\n\t\tint strIdxStart = 0;\n\t\tint strIdxEnd = str.length() - 1;\n\t\tchar ch;\n\n\t\tboolean containsStar = pattern.contains(\"*\");\n\n\t\tif (!containsStar) {\n\t\t\t// No '*'s, so we make a shortcut\n\t\t\tif (patIdxEnd != strIdxEnd) {\n\t\t\t\treturn false; // Pattern and string do not have the same size\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tfor (int i = 0; i <= patIdxEnd; i++) {\n\t\t\t\tch = pattern.charAt(i);\n\t\t\t\tif (ch != '?') {\n\t\t\t\t\tif (ch != str.charAt(i)) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\treturn false;// Character mismatch\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn true; // String matches against pattern\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (patIdxEnd == 0) {\n\t\t\treturn true; // Pattern contains only '*', which matches anything\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Process characters before first star\n\t\twhile ((ch = pattern.charAt(patIdxStart)) != '*' && strIdxStart <= strIdxEnd) {\n\t\t\tif (ch != '?') {\n\t\t\t\tif (ch != str.charAt(strIdxStart)) {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn false;// Character mismatch\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tpatIdxStart++;\n\t\t\tstrIdxStart++;\n\t\t}\n\t\tif (strIdxStart > strIdxEnd) {\n\t\t\t// All characters in the string are used. Check if only '*'s are\n\t\t\t// left in the pattern. If so, we succeeded. Otherwise failure.\n\t\t\tfor (int i = patIdxStart; i <= patIdxEnd; i++) {\n\t\t\t\tif (pattern.charAt(i) != '*') {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Process characters after last star\n\t\twhile ((ch = pattern.charAt(patIdxEnd)) != '*' && strIdxStart <= strIdxEnd) {\n\t\t\tif (ch != '?') {\n\t\t\t\tif (ch != str.charAt(strIdxEnd)) {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn false;// Character mismatch\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tpatIdxEnd--;\n\t\t\tstrIdxEnd--;\n\t\t}\n\t\tif (strIdxStart > strIdxEnd) {\n\t\t\t// All characters in the string are used. Check if only '*'s are\n\t\t\t// left in the pattern. If so, we succeeded. Otherwise failure.\n\t\t\tfor (int i = patIdxStart; i <= patIdxEnd; i++) {\n\t\t\t\tif (pattern.charAt(i) != '*') {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// process pattern between stars. padIdxStart and patIdxEnd point\n\t\t// always to a '*'.\n\t\twhile (patIdxStart != patIdxEnd && strIdxStart <= strIdxEnd) {\n\t\t\tint patIdxTmp = -1;\n\t\t\tfor (int i = patIdxStart + 1; i <= patIdxEnd; i++) {\n\t\t\t\tif (pattern.charAt(i) == '*') {\n\t\t\t\t\tpatIdxTmp = i;\n\t\t\t\t\tbreak;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif (patIdxTmp == patIdxStart + 1) {\n\t\t\t\t// Two stars next to each other, skip the first one.\n\t\t\t\tpatIdxStart++;\n\t\t\t\tcontinue;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Find the pattern between padIdxStart & padIdxTmp in str between\n\t\t\t// strIdxStart & strIdxEnd\n\t\t\tint patLength = (patIdxTmp - patIdxStart - 1);\n\t\t\tint strLength = (strIdxEnd - strIdxStart + 1);\n\t\t\tint foundIdx = -1;\n\t\t\tstrLoop: for (int i = 0; i <= strLength - patLength; i++) {\n\t\t\t\tfor (int j = 0; j < patLength; j++) {\n\t\t\t\t\tch = pattern.charAt(patIdxStart + j + 1);\n\t\t\t\t\tif (ch != '?') {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tif (ch != str.charAt(strIdxStart + i + j)) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tcontinue strLoop;\n\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t\tfoundIdx = strIdxStart + i;\n\t\t\t\tbreak;\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif (foundIdx == -1) {\n\t\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tpatIdxStart = patIdxTmp;\n\t\t\tstrIdxStart = foundIdx + patLength;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// All characters in the string are used. Check if only '*'s are left\n\t\t// in the pattern. If so, we succeeded. Otherwise failure.\n\t\tfor (int i = patIdxStart; i <= patIdxEnd; i++) {\n\t\t\tif (pattern.charAt(i) != '*') {\n\t\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn true;\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * <p>\n\t * This method takes a String key and a map from Strings to values of any type. During\n\t * processing, the method will identify the most specific key in the map that matches\n\t * the line. Once the correct is identified, its value is returned. Note that if the\n\t * map contains the wildcard string \"*\" as a key, then it will serve as the \"default\"\n\t * case, matching every line that does not match anything else.\n\t *\n\t * <p>\n\t * If no matching prefix is found, a {@link IllegalStateException} will be thrown.\n\t *\n\t * <p>\n\t * Null keys are not allowed in the map.\n\t * @param line An input string\n\t * @return the value whose prefix matches the given line\n\t */\n\tpublic S match(String line) {\n\n\t\tS value = null;\n\t\tAssert.notNull(line, \"A non-null key must be provided to match against.\");\n\n\t\tfor (String key : sorted) {\n\t\t\tif (PatternMatcher.match(key, line)) {\n\t\t\t\tvalue = map.get(key);\n\t\t\t\tbreak;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (value == null) {\n\t\t\tthrow new IllegalStateException(\"Could not find a matching pattern for key=[\" + line + \"]\");\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn value;\n\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/test/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/ExecutionContextTests.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item;\n\nimport java.io.Serializable;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Map;\n\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ExecutionContext;\nimport org.springframework.util.SerializationUtils;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\n\n/**\n * @author Lucas Ward\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n * @author Seokmun Heo\n */\nclass ExecutionContextTests {\n\n\tprivate final ExecutionContext context = new ExecutionContext();\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testNormalUsage() {\n\n\t\tcontext.putString(\"1\", \"testString1\");\n\t\tcontext.putString(\"2\", \"testString2\");\n\t\tcontext.putLong(\"3\", 3);\n\t\tcontext.putDouble(\"4\", 4.4);\n\t\tcontext.putInt(\"5\", 5);\n\n\t\tassertEquals(\"testString1\", context.getString(\"1\"));\n\t\tassertEquals(\"testString2\", context.getString(\"2\"));\n\t\tassertEquals(\"defaultString\", context.getString(\"55\", \"defaultString\"));\n\t\tassertEquals(4.4, context.getDouble(\"4\"), 0);\n\t\tassertEquals(5.5, context.getDouble(\"55\", 5.5), 0);\n\t\tassertEquals(3, context.getLong(\"3\"));\n\t\tassertEquals(5, context.getLong(\"55\", 5));\n\t\tassertEquals(5, context.getInt(\"5\"));\n\t\tassertEquals(6, context.getInt(\"55\", 6));\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testInvalidCast() {\n\t\tcontext.putLong(\"1\", 1);\n\t\tassertThrows(ClassCastException.class, () -> context.getDouble(\"1\"));\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testIsEmpty() {\n\t\tassertTrue(context.isEmpty());\n\t\tcontext.putString(\"1\", \"test\");\n\t\tassertFalse(context.isEmpty());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testDirtyFlag() {\n\t\tassertFalse(context.isDirty());\n\t\tcontext.putString(\"1\", \"test\");\n\t\tassertTrue(context.isDirty());\n\t\tcontext.clearDirtyFlag();\n\t\tassertFalse(context.isDirty());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testNotDirtyWithDuplicate() {\n\t\tcontext.putString(\"1\", \"test\");\n\t\tassertTrue(context.isDirty());\n\t\tcontext.clearDirtyFlag();\n\t\tcontext.putString(\"1\", \"test\");\n\t\tassertFalse(context.isDirty());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testDirtyWithRemoveMissing() {\n\t\tcontext.putString(\"1\", \"test\");\n\t\tassertTrue(context.isDirty());\n\t\tcontext.putString(\"1\", null); // remove an item that was present\n\t\tassertTrue(context.isDirty());\n\n\t\tcontext.clearDirtyFlag();\n\t\tcontext.putString(\"1\", null); // remove a non-existent item\n\t\tassertFalse(context.isDirty());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testContains() {\n\t\tcontext.putString(\"1\", \"testString\");\n\t\tassertTrue(context.containsKey(\"1\"));\n\t\tassertTrue(context.containsValue(\"testString\"));\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testEquals() {\n\t\tcontext.putString(\"1\", \"testString\");\n\t\tExecutionContext tempContext = new ExecutionContext();\n\t\tassertNotEquals(tempContext, context);\n\t\ttempContext.putString(\"1\", \"testString\");\n\t\tassertEquals(tempContext, context);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Putting null value is equivalent to removing the entry for the given key.\n\t */\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testPutNull() {\n\t\tcontext.put(\"1\", null);\n\t\tassertNull(context.get(\"1\"));\n\t\tassertFalse(context.containsKey(\"1\"));\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testGetNull() {\n\t\tassertNull(context.get(\"does not exist\"));\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testSerialization() {\n\n\t\tTestSerializable s = new TestSerializable();\n\t\ts.value = 7;\n\n\t\tcontext.putString(\"1\", \"testString1\");\n\t\tcontext.putString(\"2\", \"testString2\");\n\t\tcontext.putLong(\"3\", 3);\n\t\tcontext.putDouble(\"4\", 4.4);\n\t\tcontext.put(\"5\", s);\n\t\tcontext.putInt(\"6\", 6);\n\n\t\tExecutionContext clone = SerializationUtils.clone(context);\n\n\t\tassertEquals(context, clone);\n\t\tassertEquals(7, ((TestSerializable) clone.get(\"5\")).value);\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testCopyConstructor() {\n\t\tExecutionContext context = new ExecutionContext();\n\t\tcontext.put(\"foo\", \"bar\");\n\t\tExecutionContext copy = new ExecutionContext(context);\n\t\tassertEquals(copy, context);\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testCopyConstructorNullInput() {\n\t\tExecutionContext context = new ExecutionContext((ExecutionContext) null);\n\t\tassertTrue(context.isEmpty());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testDirtyWithDuplicate() {\n\t\tExecutionContext context = new ExecutionContext();\n\t\tcontext.put(\"1\", \"testString1\");\n\t\tassertTrue(context.isDirty());\n\t\tcontext.put(\"1\", \"testString1\"); // put the same value\n\t\tassertTrue(context.isDirty());\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Value object for testing serialization\n\t */\n\tprivate static class TestSerializable implements Serializable {\n\n\t\tint value;\n\n\t\t@Override\n\t\tpublic int hashCode() {\n\t\t\tfinal int prime = 31;\n\t\t\tint result = 1;\n\t\t\tresult = prime * result + value;\n\t\t\treturn result;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t@Override\n\t\tpublic boolean equals(Object obj) {\n\t\t\tif (this == obj) {\n\t\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif (obj == null) {\n\t\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif (getClass() != obj.getClass()) {\n\t\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tTestSerializable other = (TestSerializable) obj;\n\t\t\tif (value != other.value) {\n\t\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n\t@DisplayName(\"testGetByType\")\n\t@Test\n\tvoid givenAList_whenGettingAccordingToListType_thenReturnCorrectObject() {\n\t\t// given - a list\n\t\tString key = \"aListObject\";\n\t\tList<String> value = List.of(\"value1\", \"value2\");\n\t\tcontext.put(key, value);\n\t\t// when - getting according to list type\n\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"unchecked\")\n\t\tList<String> result = (List<String>) context.get(key, List.class);\n\t\t// then - return the correct list\n\t\tassertEquals(result, value);\n\t\tassertEquals(result.get(0), value.get(0));\n\t\tassertEquals(result.get(1), value.get(1));\n\t}\n\n\t@DisplayName(\"testGetNullByDefaultParam\")\n\t@Test\n\tvoid givenANonExistingKey_whenGettingTheNullList_thenReturnNull() {\n\t\t// given - a non existing key\n\t\tString key = \"aListObjectButNull\";\n\t\t// when - getting according to the key\n\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"unchecked\")\n\t\tList<String> result = (List<String>) context.get(key, List.class, null);\n\t\tList<String> result2 = (List<String>) context.get(key, List.class);\n\t\t// then - return the defined null list\n\t\tassertNull(result);\n\t\tassertNull(result2);\n\t}\n\n\t@DisplayName(\"testGetNullByNotNullDefaultParam\")\n\t@Test\n\tvoid givenAnNullList_whenGettingNullWithNonNullDefault_thenReturnDefinedDefaultValue() {\n\t\t// given - a non existing key\n\t\tString key = \"aListObjectButNull\";\n\t\tList<String> defaultValue = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\tdefaultValue.add(\"value1\");\n\t\t@SuppressWarnings(\"unchecked\")\n\t\t// when - getting according to list type and default value\n\t\tList<String> result = (List<String>) context.get(key, List.class, defaultValue);\n\t\t// then - return defined default value\n\t\tassertNotNull(result);\n\t\tassertEquals(result, defaultValue);\n\t\tassertEquals(result.get(0), defaultValue.get(0));\n\t}\n\n\t@DisplayName(\"testGetWithWrongType\")\n\t@Test\n\tvoid givenAList_whenGettingWithWrongType_thenThrowClassCastException() {\n\t\t// given - another normal list\n\t\tString key = \"anotherListObject\";\n\t\tList<String> value = List.of(\"value1\", \"value2\", \"value3\");\n\t\tcontext.put(key, value);\n\t\t// when - getting according to map type\n\t\t// then - throw exception\n\t\tassertThrows(ClassCastException.class, () -> context.get(key, Map.class));\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/test/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/database/CompositeKeyFooDao.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.database;\n\nimport java.util.Map;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.sample.Foo;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.core.RowMapper;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.core.support.JdbcDaoSupport;\n\nimport javax.sql.DataSource;\n\n/**\n * @author Lucas Ward\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n *\n */\npublic class CompositeKeyFooDao extends JdbcDaoSupport implements FooDao {\n\n\tpublic CompositeKeyFooDao(DataSource dataSource) {\n\t\tthis.setDataSource(dataSource);\n\t}\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic Foo getFoo(Object key) {\n\n\t\tMap<?, ?> keys = (Map<?, ?>) key;\n\t\tObject[] args = keys.values().toArray();\n\n\t\tRowMapper<Foo> fooMapper = (rs, rowNum) -> {\n\t\t\tFoo foo = new Foo();\n\t\t\tfoo.setId(rs.getInt(1));\n\t\t\tfoo.setName(rs.getString(2));\n\t\t\tfoo.setValue(rs.getInt(3));\n\t\t\treturn foo;\n\t\t};\n\n\t\treturn getJdbcTemplate().query(\"SELECT ID, NAME, VALUE from T_FOOS where ID = ? and VALUE = ?\", fooMapper, args)\n\t\t\t.get(0);\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/test/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/database/SingleKeyFooDao.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2008-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.database;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.sample.Foo;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.core.RowMapper;\nimport org.springframework.jdbc.core.support.JdbcDaoSupport;\n\npublic class SingleKeyFooDao extends JdbcDaoSupport implements FooDao {\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic Foo getFoo(Object key) {\n\n\t\tRowMapper<Foo> fooMapper = (rs, rowNum) -> {\n\t\t\tFoo foo = new Foo();\n\t\t\tfoo.setId(rs.getInt(1));\n\t\t\tfoo.setName(rs.getString(2));\n\t\t\tfoo.setValue(rs.getInt(3));\n\t\t\treturn foo;\n\t\t};\n\n\t\treturn getJdbcTemplate().query(\"SELECT ID, NAME, VALUE from T_FOOS where ID = ?\", fooMapper, key).get(0);\n\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/test/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/item/support/TransactionAwareListItemReaderTests.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.support;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\n\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.support.ListItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.transaction.ResourcelessTransactionManager;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.transaction.TransactionAwareProxyFactory;\nimport org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;\nimport org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionCallback;\nimport org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\n\nclass TransactionAwareListItemReaderTests {\n\n\tprivate final ListItemReader<String> reader = new ListItemReader<>(\n\t\t\tTransactionAwareProxyFactory.createTransactionalList(List.of(\"a\", \"b\", \"c\")));\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testNext() {\n\t\tassertEquals(\"a\", reader.read());\n\t\tassertEquals(\"b\", reader.read());\n\t\tassertEquals(\"c\", reader.read());\n\t\tassertNull(reader.read());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testCommit() {\n\t\tPlatformTransactionManager transactionManager = new ResourcelessTransactionManager();\n\t\tfinal List<Object> taken = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\tnew TransactionTemplate(transactionManager).execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\ttaken.add(reader.read());\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t});\n\t\tassertEquals(1, taken.size());\n\t\tassertEquals(\"a\", taken.get(0));\n\t\ttaken.clear();\n\t\tObject next = reader.read();\n\t\twhile (next != null) {\n\t\t\ttaken.add(next);\n\t\t\tnext = reader.read();\n\t\t}\n\t\tassertFalse(taken.contains(\"a\"));\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testTransactionalExhausted() {\n\t\tPlatformTransactionManager transactionManager = new ResourcelessTransactionManager();\n\t\tfinal List<Object> taken = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\tnew TransactionTemplate(transactionManager).execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\tObject next = reader.read();\n\t\t\twhile (next != null) {\n\t\t\t\ttaken.add(next);\n\t\t\t\tnext = reader.read();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t});\n\t\tassertEquals(3, taken.size());\n\t\tassertEquals(\"a\", taken.get(0));\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testRollback() {\n\t\tPlatformTransactionManager transactionManager = new ResourcelessTransactionManager();\n\t\tfinal List<Object> taken = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\tException exception = assertThrows(RuntimeException.class,\n\t\t\t\t() -> new TransactionTemplate(transactionManager).execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\t\t\ttaken.add(reader.read());\n\t\t\t\t\tthrow new RuntimeException(\"Rollback!\");\n\t\t\t\t}));\n\t\tassertEquals(\"Rollback!\", exception.getMessage());\n\t\tassertEquals(1, taken.size());\n\t\tassertEquals(\"a\", taken.get(0));\n\t\ttaken.clear();\n\t\tObject next = reader.read();\n\t\twhile (next != null) {\n\t\t\ttaken.add(next);\n\t\t\tnext = reader.read();\n\t\t}\n\t\tassertTrue(taken.contains(\"a\"));\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/test/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/repeat/listener/RepeatListenerTests.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.listener;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\n\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.RepeatStatus;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.RepeatContext;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.RepeatListener;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.support.RepeatTemplate;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.support.TaskExecutorRepeatTemplate;\nimport org.springframework.core.task.SimpleAsyncTaskExecutor;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;\n\nclass RepeatListenerTests {\n\n\tprivate int count = 0;\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testBeforeInterceptors() {\n\t\tRepeatTemplate template = new RepeatTemplate();\n\t\tfinal List<Object> calls = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\ttemplate.setListeners(new RepeatListener[] { new RepeatListener() {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic void before(RepeatContext context) {\n\t\t\t\tcalls.add(\"1\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}, new RepeatListener() {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic void before(RepeatContext context) {\n\t\t\t\tcalls.add(\"2\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} });\n\t\ttemplate.iterate(context -> {\n\t\t\tcount++;\n\t\t\treturn RepeatStatus.continueIf(count <= 1);\n\t\t});\n\t\t// 2 calls: the second time there is no processing\n\t\t// (despite the fact that the callback returned null and batch was\n\t\t// complete). Is this OK?\n\t\tassertEquals(2, count);\n\t\t// ... but the interceptor before() was called:\n\t\tassertEquals(\"[1, 2, 1, 2]\", calls.toString());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testBeforeInterceptorCanVeto() {\n\t\tRepeatTemplate template = new RepeatTemplate();\n\t\tfinal List<Object> calls = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\ttemplate.registerListener(new RepeatListener() {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic void before(RepeatContext context) {\n\t\t\t\tcalls.add(\"1\");\n\t\t\t\tcontext.setCompleteOnly();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t});\n\t\ttemplate.iterate(context -> {\n\t\t\tcount++;\n\t\t\treturn RepeatStatus.FINISHED;\n\t\t});\n\t\tassertEquals(0, count);\n\t\t// ... but the interceptor before() was called:\n\t\tassertEquals(\"[1]\", calls.toString());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testAfterInterceptors() {\n\t\tRepeatTemplate template = new RepeatTemplate();\n\t\tfinal List<Object> calls = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\ttemplate.setListeners(new RepeatListener[] { new RepeatListener() {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic void after(RepeatContext context, RepeatStatus result) {\n\t\t\t\tcalls.add(\"1\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}, new RepeatListener() {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic void after(RepeatContext context, RepeatStatus result) {\n\t\t\t\tcalls.add(\"2\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} });\n\t\ttemplate.iterate(context -> {\n\t\t\tcount++;\n\t\t\treturn RepeatStatus.continueIf(count <= 1);\n\t\t});\n\t\t// 2 calls to the callback, and the second one had no processing...\n\t\tassertEquals(2, count);\n\t\t// ... so the interceptor after() is not called:\n\t\tassertEquals(\"[2, 1]\", calls.toString());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testOpenInterceptors() {\n\t\tRepeatTemplate template = new RepeatTemplate();\n\t\tfinal List<Object> calls = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\ttemplate.setListeners(new RepeatListener[] { new RepeatListener() {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic void open(RepeatContext context) {\n\t\t\t\tcalls.add(\"1\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}, new RepeatListener() {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic void open(RepeatContext context) {\n\t\t\t\tcalls.add(\"2\");\n\t\t\t\tcontext.setCompleteOnly();\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} });\n\t\ttemplate.iterate(context -> {\n\t\t\tcount++;\n\t\t\treturn RepeatStatus.CONTINUABLE;\n\t\t});\n\t\tassertEquals(0, count);\n\t\tassertEquals(\"[1, 2]\", calls.toString());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testSingleOpenInterceptor() {\n\t\tRepeatTemplate template = new RepeatTemplate();\n\t\tfinal List<Object> calls = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\ttemplate.registerListener(new RepeatListener() {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic void open(RepeatContext context) {\n\t\t\t\tcalls.add(\"1\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t});\n\t\ttemplate.iterate(context -> {\n\t\t\tcount++;\n\t\t\tcontext.setCompleteOnly();\n\t\t\treturn RepeatStatus.FINISHED;\n\t\t});\n\t\tassertEquals(1, count);\n\t\tassertEquals(\"[1]\", calls.toString());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testCloseInterceptors() {\n\t\tRepeatTemplate template = new RepeatTemplate();\n\t\tfinal List<Object> calls = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\ttemplate.setListeners(new RepeatListener[] { new RepeatListener() {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic void close(RepeatContext context) {\n\t\t\t\tcalls.add(\"1\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}, new RepeatListener() {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic void close(RepeatContext context) {\n\t\t\t\tcalls.add(\"2\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} });\n\t\ttemplate.iterate(context -> {\n\t\t\tcount++;\n\t\t\treturn RepeatStatus.continueIf(count < 2);\n\t\t});\n\t\t// Test that more than one call comes in to the callback...\n\t\tassertEquals(2, count);\n\t\t// ... but the interceptor is only called once.\n\t\tassertEquals(\"[2, 1]\", calls.toString());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testOnErrorInterceptors() {\n\t\tRepeatTemplate template = new RepeatTemplate();\n\t\tfinal List<Object> calls = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\ttemplate.setListeners(new RepeatListener[] { new RepeatListener() {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic void onError(RepeatContext context, Throwable t) {\n\t\t\t\tcalls.add(\"1\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}, new RepeatListener() {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic void onError(RepeatContext context, Throwable t) {\n\t\t\t\tcalls.add(\"2\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} });\n\t\tassertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, () -> template.iterate(context -> {\n\t\t\tthrow new IllegalStateException(\"Bogus\");\n\t\t}));\n\t\tassertEquals(0, count);\n\t\tassertEquals(\"[2, 1]\", calls.toString());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testOnErrorInterceptorsPrecedence() {\n\t\tRepeatTemplate template = new RepeatTemplate();\n\t\tfinal List<Object> calls = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\ttemplate.setListeners(new RepeatListener[] { new RepeatListener() {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic void after(RepeatContext context, RepeatStatus result) {\n\t\t\t\tcalls.add(\"1\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}, new RepeatListener() {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic void onError(RepeatContext context, Throwable t) {\n\t\t\t\tcalls.add(\"2\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} });\n\t\tassertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, () -> template.iterate(context -> {\n\t\t\tthrow new IllegalStateException(\"Bogus\");\n\t\t}));\n\t\tassertEquals(0, count);\n\t\t// The after is not executed, if there is an error...\n\t\tassertEquals(\"[2]\", calls.toString());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testAsynchronousOnErrorInterceptorsPrecedence() {\n\t\tTaskExecutorRepeatTemplate template = new TaskExecutorRepeatTemplate();\n\t\ttemplate.setTaskExecutor(new SimpleAsyncTaskExecutor());\n\t\tfinal List<Object> calls = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\tfinal List<Object> fails = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\ttemplate.setListeners(new RepeatListener[] { new RepeatListener() {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic void after(RepeatContext context, RepeatStatus result) {\n\t\t\t\tcalls.add(\"1\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}, new RepeatListener() {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic void onError(RepeatContext context, Throwable t) {\n\t\t\t\tcalls.add(\"2\");\n\t\t\t\tfails.add(\"2\");\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} });\n\t\tException exception = assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, () -> template.iterate(context -> {\n\t\t\tthrow new IllegalStateException(\"Bogus\");\n\t\t}));\n\t\tassertEquals(\"Bogus\", exception.getMessage());\n\t\tassertEquals(0, count);\n\t\t// The after is not executed on error...\n\t\tassertEquals(\"2\", calls.get(0));\n\t\tassertEquals(\"2\", calls.get(calls.size() - 1));\n\t\tassertFalse(calls.contains(\"1\"));\n\t\tassertEquals(fails.size(), calls.size());\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/test/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/repeat/support/ChunkedRepeatTests.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.support;\n\nimport org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ItemReader;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.RepeatCallback;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.RepeatContext;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.RepeatStatus;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.callback.NestedRepeatCallback;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.policy.SimpleCompletionPolicy;\nimport org.springframework.core.task.SimpleAsyncTaskExecutor;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\n\n/**\n * Test various approaches to chunking of a batch. Not really a unit test, but it should\n * be fast.\n *\n * @author Dave Syer\n *\n */\nclass ChunkedRepeatTests extends AbstractTradeBatchTests {\n\n\tprivate int count = 0;\n\n\t/**\n\t * Chunking using a dedicated TerminationPolicy. Transactions would be laid on at the\n\t * level of chunkTemplate.execute() or the surrounding callback.\n\t */\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testChunkedBatchWithTerminationPolicy() {\n\n\t\tRepeatTemplate repeatTemplate = new RepeatTemplate();\n\t\tfinal RepeatCallback callback = new ItemReaderRepeatCallback<>(provider, processor);\n\n\t\tfinal RepeatTemplate chunkTemplate = new RepeatTemplate();\n\t\t// The policy is resettable so we only have to resolve this dependency\n\t\t// once\n\t\tchunkTemplate.setCompletionPolicy(new SimpleCompletionPolicy(2));\n\n\t\tRepeatStatus result = repeatTemplate.iterate(new NestedRepeatCallback(chunkTemplate, callback) {\n\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic RepeatStatus doInIteration(RepeatContext context) throws Exception {\n\t\t\t\tcount++; // for test assertion\n\t\t\t\treturn super.doInIteration(context);\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t});\n\n\t\tassertEquals(NUMBER_OF_ITEMS, processor.count);\n\t\t// The chunk executes 3 times because the last one\n\t\t// returns false. We terminate the main batch when\n\t\t// we encounter a partially empty chunk.\n\t\tassertEquals(3, count);\n\t\tassertFalse(result.isContinuable());\n\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Chunking with an asynchronous taskExecutor in the chunks. Transactions have to be\n\t * at the level of the business callback.\n\t */\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testAsynchronousChunkedBatchWithCompletionPolicy() {\n\n\t\tRepeatTemplate repeatTemplate = new RepeatTemplate();\n\t\tfinal RepeatCallback callback = new ItemReaderRepeatCallback<>(provider, processor);\n\n\t\tfinal TaskExecutorRepeatTemplate chunkTemplate = new TaskExecutorRepeatTemplate();\n\t\t// The policy is resettable so we only have to resolve this dependency\n\t\t// once\n\t\tchunkTemplate.setCompletionPolicy(new SimpleCompletionPolicy(2));\n\t\tchunkTemplate.setTaskExecutor(new SimpleAsyncTaskExecutor());\n\n\t\tRepeatStatus result = repeatTemplate.iterate(new NestedRepeatCallback(chunkTemplate, callback) {\n\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic RepeatStatus doInIteration(RepeatContext context) throws Exception {\n\t\t\t\tcount++; // for test assertion\n\t\t\t\treturn super.doInIteration(context);\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t});\n\n\t\tassertEquals(NUMBER_OF_ITEMS, processor.count);\n\t\tassertFalse(result.isContinuable());\n\t\tassertTrue(count >= 3, \"Expected at least 3 chunks but found: \" + count);\n\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Explicit chunking of input data. Transactions would be laid on at the level of\n\t * template.execute().\n\t */\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testChunksWithTruncatedItemProvider() {\n\n\t\tRepeatTemplate template = new RepeatTemplate();\n\n\t\t// This pattern would work with an asynchronous callback as well\n\t\t// (but non-transactional in that case).\n\n\t\tclass Chunker {\n\n\t\t\tboolean ready = false;\n\n\t\t\tint count = 0;\n\n\t\t\tvoid set() {\n\t\t\t\tready = true;\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tboolean ready() {\n\t\t\t\treturn ready;\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tboolean first() {\n\t\t\t\treturn count == 0;\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tvoid reset() {\n\t\t\t\tcount = 0;\n\t\t\t\tready = false;\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tvoid increment() {\n\t\t\t\tcount++;\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tfinal Chunker chunker = new Chunker();\n\n\t\twhile (!chunker.ready()) {\n\n\t\t\tItemReader<Trade> truncated = new ItemReader<>() {\n\t\t\t\tint count = 0;\n\n\t\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\t\tpublic @Nullable Trade read() throws Exception {\n\t\t\t\t\tif (count++ < 2)\n\t\t\t\t\t\treturn provider.read();\n\t\t\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t};\n\t\t\tchunker.reset();\n\t\t\ttemplate.iterate(new ItemReaderRepeatCallback<>(truncated, processor) {\n\n\t\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\t\tpublic RepeatStatus doInIteration(RepeatContext context) throws Exception {\n\t\t\t\t\tRepeatStatus result = super.doInIteration(context);\n\t\t\t\t\tif (!result.isContinuable() && chunker.first()) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tchunker.set();\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tchunker.increment();\n\t\t\t\t\treturn result;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t});\n\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tassertEquals(NUMBER_OF_ITEMS, processor.count);\n\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/test/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/repeat/support/TaskExecutorRepeatTemplateAsynchronousTests.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.support;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotSame;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertSame;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.HashSet;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Set;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;\n\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.Chunk;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.ExecutionContext;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file.mapping.DefaultLineMapper;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.item.file.transform.DelimitedLineTokenizer;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.RepeatCallback;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.RepeatContext;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.RepeatStatus;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.callback.NestedRepeatCallback;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.policy.SimpleCompletionPolicy;\nimport org.springframework.core.task.SimpleAsyncTaskExecutor;\n\nclass TaskExecutorRepeatTemplateAsynchronousTests extends AbstractTradeBatchTests {\n\n\tprivate final RepeatTemplate template = getRepeatTemplate();\n\n\tprivate int count = 0;\n\n\tprivate RepeatTemplate getRepeatTemplate() {\n\t\tTaskExecutorRepeatTemplate template = new TaskExecutorRepeatTemplate();\n\t\ttemplate.setTaskExecutor(new SimpleAsyncTaskExecutor());\n\t\t// Set default completion above number of items in input file\n\t\ttemplate.setCompletionPolicy(new SimpleCompletionPolicy(8));\n\t\treturn template;\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testEarlyCompletionWithException() {\n\n\t\tTaskExecutorRepeatTemplate template = new TaskExecutorRepeatTemplate();\n\t\tSimpleAsyncTaskExecutor taskExecutor = new SimpleAsyncTaskExecutor();\n\t\ttemplate.setCompletionPolicy(new SimpleCompletionPolicy(20));\n\t\ttaskExecutor.setConcurrencyLimit(2);\n\t\ttemplate.setTaskExecutor(taskExecutor);\n\t\tException exception = assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, () -> template.iterate(context -> {\n\t\t\tcount++;\n\t\t\tthrow new IllegalStateException(\"foo!\");\n\t\t}));\n\t\tassertEquals(\"foo!\", exception.getMessage());\n\n\t\tassertTrue(count >= 1, \"Too few attempts: \" + count);\n\t\tassertTrue(count <= 10, \"Too many attempts: \" + count);\n\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testExceptionHandlerSwallowsException() {\n\n\t\tTaskExecutorRepeatTemplate template = new TaskExecutorRepeatTemplate();\n\t\tSimpleAsyncTaskExecutor taskExecutor = new SimpleAsyncTaskExecutor();\n\t\ttemplate.setCompletionPolicy(new SimpleCompletionPolicy(4));\n\t\ttaskExecutor.setConcurrencyLimit(2);\n\t\ttemplate.setTaskExecutor(taskExecutor);\n\n\t\ttemplate.setExceptionHandler((context, throwable) -> count++);\n\t\ttemplate.iterate(context -> {\n\t\t\tthrow new IllegalStateException(\"foo!\");\n\t\t});\n\n\t\tassertTrue(count >= 1, \"Too few attempts: \" + count);\n\t\tassertTrue(count <= 10, \"Too many attempts: \" + count);\n\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testNestedSession() {\n\n\t\tRepeatTemplate outer = getRepeatTemplate();\n\t\tRepeatTemplate inner = new RepeatTemplate();\n\n\t\touter.iterate(new NestedRepeatCallback(inner, context -> {\n\t\t\tcount++;\n\t\t\tassertNotNull(context);\n\t\t\tassertNotSame(context, context.getParent(), \"Nested batch should have new session\");\n\t\t\tassertSame(context, RepeatSynchronizationManager.getContext());\n\t\t\treturn RepeatStatus.FINISHED;\n\t\t}) {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic RepeatStatus doInIteration(RepeatContext context) throws Exception {\n\t\t\t\tcount++;\n\t\t\t\tassertNotNull(context);\n\t\t\t\tassertSame(context, RepeatSynchronizationManager.getContext());\n\t\t\t\treturn super.doInIteration(context);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t});\n\n\t\tassertTrue(count >= 1, \"Too few attempts: \" + count);\n\t\tassertTrue(count <= 10, \"Too many attempts: \" + count);\n\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Run a batch with a single template that itself has an async task executor. The\n\t * result is a batch that runs in multiple threads (up to the throttle limit of the\n\t * template).\n\t */\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testMultiThreadAsynchronousExecution() {\n\n\t\tfinal String threadName = Thread.currentThread().getName();\n\t\tfinal Set<String> threadNames = new HashSet<>();\n\n\t\tfinal RepeatCallback callback = context -> {\n\t\t\tassertNotSame(threadName, Thread.currentThread().getName());\n\t\t\tthreadNames.add(Thread.currentThread().getName());\n\t\t\tThread.sleep(100);\n\t\t\tTrade item = provider.read();\n\t\t\tif (item != null) {\n\t\t\t\tprocessor.write(Chunk.of(item));\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn RepeatStatus.continueIf(item != null);\n\t\t};\n\n\t\ttemplate.iterate(callback);\n\t\t// Shouldn't be necessary to wait:\n\t\t// Thread.sleep(500);\n\t\tassertEquals(NUMBER_OF_ITEMS, processor.count);\n\t\tassertTrue(threadNames.size() > 1);\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testThrottleLimit() {\n\n\t\tint throttleLimit = 600;\n\n\t\tTaskExecutorRepeatTemplate template = new TaskExecutorRepeatTemplate();\n\t\tSimpleAsyncTaskExecutor taskExecutor = new SimpleAsyncTaskExecutor();\n\t\ttaskExecutor.setConcurrencyLimit(300);\n\t\ttemplate.setTaskExecutor(taskExecutor);\n\n\t\tString threadName = Thread.currentThread().getName();\n\t\tSet<String> threadNames = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();\n\t\tList<String> items = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>());\n\n\t\tRepeatCallback callback = context -> {\n\t\t\tassertNotSame(threadName, Thread.currentThread().getName());\n\t\t\tTrade item = provider.read();\n\t\t\tthreadNames.add(Thread.currentThread().getName() + \" : \" + item);\n\t\t\titems.add(String.valueOf(item));\n\t\t\tif (item != null) {\n\t\t\t\tprocessor.write(Chunk.of(item));\n\t\t\t\t// Do some more I/O\n\t\t\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {\n\t\t\t\t\tDefaultLineMapper<Trade> mapper = new DefaultLineMapper<>();\n\t\t\t\t\tmapper.setLineTokenizer(new DelimitedLineTokenizer());\n\t\t\t\t\tmapper.setFieldSetMapper(new TradeMapper());\n\t\t\t\t\tTradeItemReader provider = new TradeItemReader(mapper);\n\t\t\t\t\tprovider.setResource(resource);\n\t\t\t\t\tprovider.open(new ExecutionContext());\n\t\t\t\t\twhile (provider.read() != null)\n\t\t\t\t\t\tcontinue;\n\t\t\t\t\tprovider.close();\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn RepeatStatus.continueIf(item != null);\n\t\t};\n\n\t\ttemplate.iterate(callback);\n\t\t// Shouldn't be necessary to wait:\n\t\t// Thread.sleep(500);\n\t\tassertEquals(NUMBER_OF_ITEMS, processor.count);\n\t\tassertTrue(threadNames.size() > 1);\n\t\tint frequency = Collections.frequency(items, \"null\");\n\t\tassertTrue(frequency <= throttleLimit);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Wrap an otherwise synchronous batch in a callback to an asynchronous template.\n\t */\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testSingleThreadAsynchronousExecution() {\n\t\tTaskExecutorRepeatTemplate jobTemplate = new TaskExecutorRepeatTemplate();\n\t\tfinal RepeatTemplate stepTemplate = new RepeatTemplate();\n\t\tSimpleAsyncTaskExecutor taskExecutor = new SimpleAsyncTaskExecutor();\n\t\ttaskExecutor.setConcurrencyLimit(2);\n\t\tjobTemplate.setTaskExecutor(taskExecutor);\n\n\t\tfinal String threadName = Thread.currentThread().getName();\n\t\tfinal Set<String> threadNames = new HashSet<>();\n\n\t\tfinal RepeatCallback stepCallback = new ItemReaderRepeatCallback<>(provider, processor) {\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic RepeatStatus doInIteration(RepeatContext context) throws Exception {\n\t\t\t\tassertNotSame(threadName, Thread.currentThread().getName());\n\t\t\t\tthreadNames.add(Thread.currentThread().getName());\n\t\t\t\tThread.sleep(100);\n\t\t\t\tDefaultLineMapper<Trade> mapper = new DefaultLineMapper<>();\n\t\t\t\tmapper.setLineTokenizer(new DelimitedLineTokenizer());\n\t\t\t\tmapper.setFieldSetMapper(new TradeMapper());\n\t\t\t\tTradeItemReader provider = new TradeItemReader(mapper);\n\t\t\t\tprovider.setResource(resource);\n\t\t\t\tprovider.open(new ExecutionContext());\n\t\t\t\twhile (provider.read() != null)\n\t\t\t\t\t;\n\t\t\t\treturn super.doInIteration(context);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t};\n\t\tRepeatCallback jobCallback = context -> {\n\t\t\tstepTemplate.iterate(stepCallback);\n\t\t\treturn RepeatStatus.FINISHED;\n\t\t};\n\n\t\tjobTemplate.iterate(jobCallback);\n\t\t// Shouldn't be necessary to wait:\n\t\t// Thread.sleep(500);\n\t\tassertEquals(NUMBER_OF_ITEMS, processor.count);\n\t\t// Because of the throttling and queueing internally to a TaskExecutor,\n\t\t// more than one thread will be used - the number used is the\n\t\t// concurrency limit in the task executor, plus 1.\n\t\tassertTrue(threadNames.size() >= 1);\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/test/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/repeat/support/TaskExecutorRepeatTemplateBulkAsynchronousTests.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.support;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;\n\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.Log;\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.LogFactory;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.RepeatCallback;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.RepeatContext;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.RepeatStatus;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.policy.SimpleCompletionPolicy;\nimport org.springframework.scheduling.concurrent.ThreadPoolTaskExecutor;\n\n/**\n * Simple tests for concurrent behaviour in repeat template, in particular the barrier at\n * the end of the iteration. N.B. these tests may fail if insufficient threads are\n * available (e.g. on a single-core machine, or under load). They shouldn't deadlock\n * though.\n *\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n *\n */\nclass TaskExecutorRepeatTemplateBulkAsynchronousTests {\n\n\tstatic Log logger = LogFactory.getLog(TaskExecutorRepeatTemplateBulkAsynchronousTests.class);\n\n\tprivate int total = 1000;\n\n\tprivate volatile int early = Integer.MAX_VALUE;\n\n\tprivate volatile int error = Integer.MAX_VALUE;\n\n\tprivate TaskExecutorRepeatTemplate template;\n\n\tprivate RepeatCallback callback;\n\n\tprivate List<String> items;\n\n\tprivate final ThreadPoolTaskExecutor threadPool = new ThreadPoolTaskExecutor();\n\n\t@BeforeEach\n\tvoid setUp() {\n\n\t\ttemplate = new TaskExecutorRepeatTemplate();\n\t\tthreadPool.setMaxPoolSize(300);\n\t\tthreadPool.setCorePoolSize(10);\n\t\tthreadPool.setQueueCapacity(0);\n\t\tthreadPool.afterPropertiesSet();\n\t\ttemplate.setTaskExecutor(threadPool);\n\n\t\titems = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>());\n\n\t\tcallback = new RepeatCallback() {\n\n\t\t\tprivate final AtomicInteger count = new AtomicInteger(0);\n\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic RepeatStatus doInIteration(RepeatContext context) throws Exception {\n\t\t\t\tint position = count.incrementAndGet();\n\t\t\t\tString item = position <= total ? String.valueOf(position) : null;\n\t\t\t\titems.add(item);\n\t\t\t\tif (item != null) {\n\t\t\t\t\tbeBusy();\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t/*\n\t\t\t\t * In a multi-threaded task, one of the callbacks can call FINISHED early,\n\t\t\t\t * while other threads are still working, and would do more work if the\n\t\t\t\t * callback was called again. (This happens for instance if there is a\n\t\t\t\t * failure and you want to retry the work.)\n\t\t\t\t */\n\t\t\t\tRepeatStatus result = RepeatStatus.continueIf(position != early && item != null);\n\t\t\t\tif (position == error) {\n\t\t\t\t\tthrow new RuntimeException(\"Planned\");\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif (!result.isContinuable()) {\n\t\t\t\t\tlogger.debug(\"Returning \" + result + \" for count=\" + position);\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treturn result;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t};\n\n\t}\n\n\t@AfterEach\n\tvoid tearDown() {\n\t\tthreadPool.destroy();\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testErrorThrownByCallback() {\n\n\t\tcallback = new RepeatCallback() {\n\n\t\t\tprivate final AtomicInteger count = new AtomicInteger(0);\n\n\t\t\t@Override\n\t\t\tpublic RepeatStatus doInIteration(RepeatContext context) throws Exception {\n\t\t\t\tint position = count.incrementAndGet();\n\n\t\t\t\tif (position == 4) {\n\t\t\t\t\tthrow new OutOfMemoryError(\"Planned\");\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn RepeatStatus.CONTINUABLE;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t};\n\n\t\ttemplate.setCompletionPolicy(new SimpleCompletionPolicy(10));\n\n\t\tError error = assertThrows(OutOfMemoryError.class, () -> template.iterate(callback));\n\t\tassertEquals(\"Planned\", error.getMessage());\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Slightly flakey convenience method. If this doesn't do something that lasts\n\t * sufficiently long for another worker to be launched while it is busy, the early\n\t * completion tests will fail. \"Sufficiently long\" is the problem so we try and block\n\t * until we know someone else is busy?\n\t * @throws Exception if interrupted while being busy\n\t */\n\tprivate void beBusy() throws Exception {\n\t\tsynchronized (this) {\n\t\t\twait(100L);\n\t\t\tnotifyAll();\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/test/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/repeat/support/TaskExecutorRepeatTemplateTests.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2025 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.repeat.support;\n\n/**\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n */\npublic class TaskExecutorRepeatTemplateTests extends SimpleRepeatTemplateTests {\n\n\t@Override\n\tpublic RepeatTemplate getRepeatTemplate() {\n\t\treturn new TaskExecutorRepeatTemplate();\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "spring-batch-infrastructure/src/test/java/org/springframework/batch/infrastructure/support/transaction/ConcurrentTransactionAwareProxyTests.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright 2006-2023 the original author or authors.\n *\n * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n * You may obtain a copy of the License at\n *\n *      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n *\n * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n * distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.transaction;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.HashMap;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Map;\nimport java.util.Set;\nimport java.util.concurrent.CompletionService;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ExecutionException;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ExecutorCompletionService;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;\nimport java.util.concurrent.Executors;\n\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.Log;\nimport org.apache.commons.logging.LogFactory;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.condition.DisabledOnOs;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.condition.OS;\n\nimport org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;\nimport org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionCallback;\nimport org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;\nimport org.springframework.util.Assert;\n\n/**\n * @author Dave Syer\n * @author Mahmoud Ben Hassine\n *\n */\n@DisabledOnOs(value = OS.WINDOWS, disabledReason = \"Intermittent false-negative failures\") // #3847\nclass ConcurrentTransactionAwareProxyTests {\n\n\tprivate static final Log logger = LogFactory.getLog(ConcurrentTransactionAwareProxyTests.class);\n\n\tprivate final PlatformTransactionManager transactionManager = new ResourcelessTransactionManager();\n\n\tint outerMax = 20;\n\n\tint innerMax = 30;\n\n\tprivate ExecutorService executor;\n\n\tprivate CompletionService<List<String>> completionService;\n\n\t@BeforeEach\n\tvoid init() {\n\t\texecutor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(outerMax);\n\t\tcompletionService = new ExecutorCompletionService<>(executor);\n\t}\n\n\t@AfterEach\n\tvoid close() {\n\t\texecutor.shutdown();\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testConcurrentTransactionalSet() {\n\t\tSet<String> set = TransactionAwareProxyFactory.createTransactionalSet();\n\t\tassertThrows(Throwable.class, () -> testSet(set));\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testConcurrentTransactionalAppendOnlySet() throws Exception {\n\t\tSet<String> set = TransactionAwareProxyFactory.createAppendOnlyTransactionalSet();\n\t\ttestSet(set);\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testConcurrentTransactionalAppendOnlyList() throws Exception {\n\t\tList<String> list = TransactionAwareProxyFactory.createAppendOnlyTransactionalList();\n\t\ttestList(list, false);\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testConcurrentTransactionalAppendOnlyMap() throws Exception {\n\t\tMap<Long, Map<String, String>> map = TransactionAwareProxyFactory.createAppendOnlyTransactionalMap();\n\t\ttestMap(map);\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testConcurrentTransactionalMap() {\n\t\tMap<Long, Map<String, String>> map = TransactionAwareProxyFactory.createTransactionalMap();\n\t\tassertThrows(ExecutionException.class, () -> testMap(map));\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testTransactionalContains() {\n\t\tfinal Map<Long, Map<String, String>> map = TransactionAwareProxyFactory.createAppendOnlyTransactionalMap();\n\t\tboolean result = new TransactionTemplate(transactionManager).execute(status -> map.containsKey(0L));\n\t\tassertFalse(result);\n\t}\n\n\tprivate void testSet(Set<String> set) throws Exception {\n\n\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < outerMax; i++) {\n\n\t\t\tfinal int count = i;\n\t\t\tcompletionService.submit(() -> {\n\t\t\t\tList<String> list = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\t\t\tfor (int i1 = 0; i1 < innerMax; i1++) {\n\t\t\t\t\tString value = count + \"bar\" + i1;\n\t\t\t\t\tsaveInSetAndAssert(set, value);\n\t\t\t\t\tlist.add(value);\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treturn list;\n\t\t\t});\n\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < outerMax; i++) {\n\t\t\tList<String> result = completionService.take().get();\n\t\t\tassertEquals(innerMax, result.size());\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tassertEquals(innerMax * outerMax, set.size());\n\n\t}\n\n\tprivate void testList(List<String> list, final boolean mutate) throws Exception {\n\n\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < outerMax; i++) {\n\n\t\t\tcompletionService.submit(() -> {\n\t\t\t\tList<String> result = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\t\t\tfor (int i1 = 0; i1 < innerMax; i1++) {\n\t\t\t\t\tString value = \"bar\" + i1;\n\t\t\t\t\tsaveInListAndAssert(list, value);\n\t\t\t\t\tresult.add(value);\n\t\t\t\t\t// Need to slow it down to allow threads to interleave\n\t\t\t\t\tThread.sleep(10L);\n\t\t\t\t\tif (mutate) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tlist.remove(value);\n\t\t\t\t\t\tlist.add(value);\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tlogger.info(\"Added: \" + innerMax + \" values\");\n\t\t\t\treturn result;\n\t\t\t});\n\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < outerMax; i++) {\n\t\t\tList<String> result = completionService.take().get();\n\t\t\tassertEquals(innerMax, result.size(), \"Wrong number of results in inner task\");\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tassertEquals(innerMax * outerMax, list.size(), \"Wrong number of results in aggregate\");\n\n\t}\n\n\tprivate void testMap(Map<Long, Map<String, String>> map) throws Exception {\n\n\t\tint numberOfKeys = outerMax;\n\n\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < outerMax; i++) {\n\n\t\t\tfor (int j = 0; j < numberOfKeys; j++) {\n\t\t\t\tfinal long id = j * 1000L + 123L + i;\n\n\t\t\t\tcompletionService.submit(() -> {\n\t\t\t\t\tList<String> list = new ArrayList<>();\n\t\t\t\t\tfor (int i1 = 0; i1 < innerMax; i1++) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tString value = \"bar\" + i1;\n\t\t\t\t\t\tlist.add(saveInMapAndAssert(map, id, value).get(\"foo\"));\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\treturn list;\n\t\t\t\t});\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tfor (int j = 0; j < numberOfKeys; j++) {\n\t\t\t\tcompletionService.take().get();\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n\tprivate String saveInSetAndAssert(Set<String> set, final String value) {\n\n\t\tnew TransactionTemplate(transactionManager).execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\tset.add(value);\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t});\n\n\t\tAssert.state(set.contains(value), \"Lost update: value=\" + value);\n\n\t\treturn value;\n\n\t}\n\n\tprivate String saveInListAndAssert(List<String> list, final String value) {\n\n\t\tnew TransactionTemplate(transactionManager).execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\tlist.add(value);\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t});\n\n\t\tAssert.state(list.contains(value), \"Lost update: value=\" + value);\n\n\t\treturn value;\n\n\t}\n\n\tprivate Map<String, String> saveInMapAndAssert(Map<Long, Map<String, String>> map, final Long id,\n\t\t\tfinal String value) {\n\n\t\tnew TransactionTemplate(transactionManager).execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\tif (!map.containsKey(id)) {\n\t\t\t\tmap.put(id, new HashMap<>());\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tmap.get(id).put(\"foo\", value);\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t});\n\n\t\tMap<String, String> result = map.get(id);\n\t\tAssert.state(result != null, \"Lost insert: null String at value=\" + value);\n\t\tString foo = result.get(\"foo\");\n\t\tAssert.state(value.equals(foo), \"Lost update: wrong value=\" + value + \" (found \" + foo + \") for id=\" + id);\n\n\t\treturn result;\n\n\t}\n\n}\n"
    },

language governing permissions and\n * limitations under the License.\n */\n\npackage org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.transaction;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\n\nimport java.util.Arrays;\nimport java.util.List;\n\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.transaction.ResourcelessTransactionManager;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.transaction.TransactionAwareProxyFactory;\nimport org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionCallback;\nimport org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;\n\nclass TransactionAwareListFactoryTests {\n\n\tprivate final TransactionTemplate transactionTemplate = new TransactionTemplate(\n\t\t\tnew ResourcelessTransactionManager());\n\n\tprivate List<String> list;\n\n\t@BeforeEach\n\tvoid setUp() {\n\t\tlist = TransactionAwareProxyFactory.createTransactionalList(Arrays.asList(\"foo\", \"bar\", \"spam\"));\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testAdd() {\n\t\tassertEquals(3, list.size());\n\t\tlist.add(\"bucket\");\n\t\tassertTrue(list.contains(\"bucket\"));\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testRemove() {\n\t\tassertEquals(3, list.size());\n\t\tassertTrue(list.contains(\"spam\"));\n\t\tlist.remove(\"spam\");\n\t\tassertFalse(list.contains(\"spam\"));\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testClear() {\n\t\tassertEquals(3, list.size());\n\t\tlist.clear();\n\t\tassertEquals(0, list.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testTransactionalAdd() {\n\t\ttransactionTemplate.execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\ttestAdd();\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t});\n\t\tassertEquals(4, list.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testTransactionalRemove() {\n\t\ttransactionTemplate.execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\ttestRemove();\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t});\n\t\tassertEquals(2, list.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testTransactionalClear() {\n\t\ttransactionTemplate.execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\ttestClear();\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t});\n\t\tassertEquals(0, list.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testTransactionalAddWithRollback() {\n\t\tException exception = assertThrows(RuntimeException.class,\n\t\t\t\t() -> transactionTemplate.execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\t\t\ttestAdd();\n\t\t\t\t\tthrow new RuntimeException(\"Rollback!\");\n\t\t\t\t}));\n\t\tassertEquals(\"Rollback!\", exception.getMessage());\n\t\tassertEquals(3, list.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testTransactionalRemoveWithRollback() {\n\t\tException exception = assertThrows(RuntimeException.class,\n\t\t\t\t() -> transactionTemplate.execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\t\t\ttestRemove();\n\t\t\t\t\tthrow new RuntimeException(\"Rollback!\");\n\t\t\t\t}));\n\t\tassertEquals(\"Rollback!\", exception.getMessage());\n\t\tassertEquals(3, list.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testTransactionalClearWithRollback() {\n\t\tException exception = assertThrows(RuntimeException.class,\n\t\t\t\t() -> transactionTemplate.execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\t\t\ttestClear();\n\t\t\t\t\tthrow new RuntimeException(\"Rollback!\");\n\t\t\t\t}));\n\t\tassertEquals(\"Rollback!\", exception.getMessage());\n\t\tassertEquals(3, list.size());\n\t}\n\n}\n"

java.util.Map;\n\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.transaction.ResourcelessTransactionManager;\nimport org.springframework.batch.infrastructure.support.transaction.TransactionAwareProxyFactory;\nimport org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionCallback;\nimport org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\n\nclass TransactionAwareMapFactoryTests {\n\n\tprivate final TransactionTemplate transactionTemplate = new TransactionTemplate(\n\t\t\tnew ResourcelessTransactionManager());\n\n\tprivate Map<String, String> map;\n\n\t@BeforeEach\n\tvoid setUp() {\n\t\tMap<String, String> seed = new HashMap<>();\n\t\tseed.put(\"foo\", \"oof\");\n\t\tseed.put(\"bar\", \"bar\");\n\t\tseed.put(\"spam\", \"maps\");\n\t\tmap = TransactionAwareProxyFactory.createTransactionalMap(seed);\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testAdd() map.size());\n\t\tmap.put(\"bucket\", \"crap\");\n\t\tassertFalse(map.isEmpty());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testValues() {\n\t\tassertEquals(3, map.size());\n\t\tmap.put(\"bucket\", \"crap\");\n\t\tassertEquals(4, map.keySet().size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testRemove() {\n\t\tassertEquals(3, map.size());\n\t\tassertTrue(map.containsKey(\"spam\"));\n\t\tmap.remove(\"spam\");\n\t\tassertFalse(map.containsKey(\"spam\"));\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testClear() {\n\t\tassertEquals(3, map.size());\n\t\tmap.clear();\n\t\tassertEquals(0, map.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testTransactionalAdd() {\n\t\ttransactionTemplate.execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\ttestAdd();\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t});\n\t\tassertEquals(4, map.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testTransactionalEmpty() {\n\t\ttransactionTemplate.execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\ttestEmpty();\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t});\n\t\tassertEquals(4, map.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testTransactionalValues() {\n\t\ttransactionTemplate.execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\ttestValues();\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t});\n\t\tassertEquals(4, map.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testTransactionalRemove() {\n\t\ttransactionTemplate.execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\ttestRemove();\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t});\n\t\tassertEquals(2, map.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testTransactionalClear() {\n\t\ttransactionTemplate.execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\ttestClear();\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t});\n\t\tassertEquals(0, map.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testTransactionalAddWithRollback() {\n\t\tException exception = assertThrows(RuntimeException.class,\n\t\t\t\t() -> transactionTemplate.execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\t\t\ttestAdd();\n\t\t\t\t\tthrow new RuntimeException(\"Rollback!\");\n\t\t\t\t}));\n\t\tassertEquals(\"Rollback!\", exception.getMessage());\n\t\tassertEquals(3, map.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testTransactionalRemoveWithRollback() {\n\t\tException exception = assertThrows(RuntimeException.class,\n\t\t\t\t() -> transactionTemplate.execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\t\t\ttestRemove();\n\t\t\t\t\tthrow new RuntimeException(\"Rollback!\");\n\t\t\t\t}));\n\t\tassertEquals(\"Rollback!\", exception.getMessage());\n\t\tassertEquals(3, map.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testTransactionalClearWithRollback() {\n\t\tException exception = assertThrows(RuntimeException.class,\n\t\t\t\t() -> transactionTemplate.execute((TransactionCallback<Void>) status -> {\n\t\t\t\t\ttestClear();\n\t\t\t\t\tthrow new RuntimeException(\"Rollback!\");\n\t\t\t\t}));\n\t\tassertEquals(\"Rollback!\", exception.getMessage());\n\t\tassertEquals(3, map.size());\n\t}\n\n}\n"

{\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testCreateList() {\n\t\tList<String> list = TransactionAwareProxyFactory.createTransactionalList();\n\t\tlist.add(\"foo\");\n\t\tassertEquals(1, list.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testCreateListWithValues() {\n\t\tList<String> list = TransactionAwareProxyFactory.createTransactionalList(Collections.singletonList(\"foo\"));\n\t\tassertEquals(1, list.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testCreateSet() {\n\t\tSet<String> set = TransactionAwareProxyFactory.createTransactionalSet(Collections.singleton(\"foo\"));\n\t\tassertEquals(1, list.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testCreateMap() {\n\t\tMap<String, String> map = TransactionAwareProxyFactory.createTransactionalMap();\n\t\tmap.put(\"foo\", \"bar\");\n\t\tassertEquals(1, map.size());\n\t}\n\n\t@Test\n\tvoid testCreateMapWithValues() {\n\t\tMap<String, String> map = TransactionAwareProxyFactory\n\t\t\t.createTransactionalMap(Collections.singletonMap(\"foo\", \"bar\"));\n\t\tassertEquals(1, map.size());\n\t}\n\n}\n"
